# Lort Phase 1B: Web App Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:executing-plans (recommended) or superpowers:subagent-driven-development (can be usage intensive) to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Build `web/`, the Next.js app that the `lort` binary from 1A supervises and proxies to. It covers the public board (`/feedback`, request pages, accounts, Suggest something), and the dashboard (sign-in, invites, the requests table, the request page with triage and merge, and the Branding, Boards, Tags and Team sections of Settings). It also adds the three server endpoints those pages need, and passes the owner's branding to Next.js on every page request.

**Architecture:** One Next.js 16 app in `web/`, built with `output: 'standalone'`. The Go server proxies every non-API path to it and adds an `X-Lort-Config` header carrying the brand (name, logo, colour, feature switches) as base64 JSON. Server components read that header, so the first paint already has the owner's colour and name, without a round trip to PocketBase. Everything else loads in the browser from the same origin with the PocketBase JS SDK and TanStack Query: PocketBase's record API (guarded by 1A's hooks) for creating requests and comments with images, `/api/lort/*` for everything with business rules. Board visitors and admins have separate auth stores. An admin's dashboard session also signs them in on the board as their linked profile, so their comments carry the Team badge. The design-system components from `design/components` are ported into `web/components/lort`. shadcn/ui supplies only the accessible primitives (dialog, popover, dropdown menu, sheet, tooltip, command), restyled with Lort's classes.

**Tech Stack:** Next.js 16.4 (App Router, Turbopack), React 19.3, TypeScript 5.9, Tailwind CSS 4, shadcn/ui 4 (Radix, `nova` preset) with Tabler icons (`@tabler/icons-react`), TanStack Query 5, PocketBase JS SDK 0.28, dnd-kit (core, sortable), `next/font` (Bricolage Grotesque and Figtree), Vitest 5 with Testing Library and jsdom, Playwright 1.64 with `@axe-core/playwright`, Prettier with `prettier-plugin-tailwindcss`, pnpm. Server additions in Go 1.27 against PocketBase v0.40.5, as in 1A.

**Spec:** `.superpowers/specs/2026-10-15-lort-design.md` (sections 2.2, 2.3, 4, 5.1–5.4, 5.6, 5.7, 6 intro, 6.1, 6.2, 6.6 Branding/Boards/Tags/Team, 10–12, and all of Part 2 for these pages). This is plan 2 of the Phase 1 trio: 1A server (done, merged), 1B web (this), 1C packaging (Dockerfile, setup scripts, CI).

**Design references:** `design/canvas/*.dc.html` holds the approved page designs; `DESIGN.md` holds the tokens and rules. Every page in this plan was built against them: `P-Feedback` (desktop and phone), `P-Request`, `P-Suggest`, `P-Auth`, `P-Profile`, `D-Login`, `D-Requests`, `D-Request`, `D-Settings` and the `S-*` skeletons.

## Global Constraints

- **Built on 1A.** The Go server in `server/` is the source of truth for data and rules. Never put status transitions, vote counting, merges or visibility rules in Next.js.
- **Next.js 16.** Before using any Next.js API that this plan doesn't show, read the matching guide in `web/node_modules/next/dist/docs/` (the scaffold's `AGENTS.md` says the same): this version differs from older ones. `params` and `searchParams` are promises; `PageProps<'/route'>` and `LayoutProps<'/route'>` are global types generated by `next typegen`, which is why `pnpm typecheck` runs it first.
- **shadcn components come from the CLI only:** `pnpm dlx shadcn@latest add …`, never written from memory. The CLI needs network access to `ui.shadcn.com`; if it fails, stop and ask for access rather than writing the component.
- **Icons:** Tabler (outline, 2px stroke) for UI icons. Lort's own SVG components for statuses and the sticker. Never Unicode glyphs as icons. `Button iconOnly` from the design system hides its children, so icon-only buttons with a Tabler icon use `<button className="lt-btn lt-btn-ghost lt-btn-icon">` with an `aria-label`.
- **Status label rule:** a status shows its icon with its label, or its coloured text alone. Never both styles at once, never neither. In the requests table the status column is icon-only with a tooltip and `aria-label`, as the spec says.
- **Class names:** build conditional class lists with `cx()` from `@/components/lort`, never template strings. The Prettier Tailwind plugin trims the leading space in `` `a${x ? ' b' : ''}` `` and silently glues the classes together.
- **Formatting:** semicolons, single quotes, 120 columns, trailing commas (`web/.prettierrc`). `pnpm format:check` and `pnpm lint` must be clean. The React Compiler lint rules are on: derive state instead of copying it in effects, and key a form to reset it.
- **Statuses and triage** exactly as in 1A: `pending`, `reviewing`, `planned`, `progress`, `completed`, `closed`; the open statuses (the default filter) are the first four. Complete shows a 60-second Undo toast.
- **Branding** comes from `X-Lort-Config` on the server and `GET /api/lort/config` in the browser. The primary colour is always passed through `deriveBrand`, so `on-primary`, `primary-text` and `primary-soft` meet WCAG AA in both themes.
- **Images:** at most 3 per request or comment, 5 MB each, PNG/JPEG/WebP/GIF; the Composer reports errors inline. Hidden requests' images are protected files: fetch a file token first.
- **Copy:** friendly and encouraging, sentence case, errors name the problem and the fix. Use the strings in this plan and in `design/canvas` as written.
- **Accessibility:** WCAG 2.2 AA. Every interactive element has a name, focus is visible, dialogs trap focus and restore it, reorder works from the keyboard, and the axe spec in Task 7 must pass in both themes.
- **Skeletons:** every page has its own skeleton matching its layout (`S-*` canvases). No spinners for page loads.
- **Themes:** dark by default, light when the visitor's system asks for it. Test both: every Playwright spec runs in a `dark` and a `light` project.

## Decisions this plan makes (recorded in the spec)

- **Request page endpoint.** `GET /api/lort/requests/{id}` returns the request, its images, the merge target, the timeline and the comment thread in one response. Visibility and internal notes are decided in Go, from the caller's token. The comment list is called `thread` in JSON, because `comments` is already the count on every request.
- **Board moves and counts.** `POST /api/lort/requests/{id}/board` moves a request and records a `board_changed` timeline entry (the record API refuses board changes, as 1A set up). `GET /api/lort/stats` gives the dashboard header's totals and the sidebar's "N new" badge.
- **Brand header.** The proxy deletes any `X-Lort-Config` a visitor sends and sets its own on every request to Next.js. The value is base64 JSON, so any app name survives an HTTP header.
- **Manual refresh is dashboard-only.** The public board relies on realtime with the 3-minute polling fallback; the rate-limited refresh button lives on the requests table.
- **"New request" in the dashboard** opens Suggest something as the admin's board profile. Creating a request on behalf of an email is left out until email (phase 3) can tell that person about it.
- **Admin sessions end only when refused.** Renewing the board profile signs the admin out only on a 401 or 403. An aborted request (navigating away mid-fetch) or a network blip keeps them signed in.
- **Deferred to later phases:** the "Get your own board" card and its Settings switch (phase 6, with `/lort-landing`); Roadmap, Changelog and the Roadmap settings section (phase 2); "Check your inbox", reset by email and notification switches (phase 3); Docs and the version line in the sidebar (phases 5–6). Nothing in this plan links to a page that doesn't exist yet.
- **E2E harness.** Playwright runs against the real stack: `e2e/start.sh` builds the Go server and this app's standalone output, then runs `lort` on a throwaway data directory. Global setup turns Lort's rate limits off, since the suite signs up more people per minute than one IP is allowed.

## File structure

```
server/
  internal/requests/detail.go       request page (Get), MoveBoard, Stats
  internal/api/api.go               + GET /requests/{id}, POST /requests/{id}/board, GET /stats, Brand, BrandHeader
  internal/web/web.go               Proxy adds X-Lort-Config; supervisor log fix
  main.go                           passes BrandHeader to the proxy
design/components/src/Composer.tsx  previews derived with useMemo (lint fix, upstreamed)
web/
  .prettierrc, eslint.config.mjs, next.config.ts, vitest.config.mts, vitest.setup.ts, playwright.config.ts
  app/
    tokens.css, lort.css, app.css, globals.css   Lort tokens, design-system classes, app classes, Tailwind mapping
    layout.tsx, providers.tsx, page.tsx          fonts, brand colour, query client, toasts; / → /feedback
    (board)/layout.tsx                           brand, auth, top bar
    (board)/feedback/page.tsx, [id]/page.tsx     the board and request pages, each with the "Powered by Lort" footer
    (board)/reset-password/page.tsx              set a new password from a reset link
    dashboard/layout.tsx                         brand for every dashboard page
    dashboard/(app)/layout.tsx                   the signed-in shell
    dashboard/(app)/page.tsx                     → /dashboard/requests
    dashboard/(app)/requests/page.tsx, [id]/page.tsx, settings/page.tsx
    dashboard/login/page.tsx, invite/page.tsx
  components/
    ui/                  shadcn primitives (CLI only)
    lort/                the design system, ported
    board/               branding, auth dialog, profile, top bar, feed, filters, suggest, votes, skeletons, request page
    request/             RequestView and postComment, shared by the board and the dashboard
    dashboard/           shell, sign-in and invites, requests table, triage, popovers, dialogs, request page
    dashboard/settings/  switches, sortable rows, branding, boards, tags, team
  lib/                   types, colour, errors, brand, URL state, time, PocketBase clients, sessions, toasts, data hooks
  e2e/                   start.sh, global setup, helpers, accounts, board, dashboard, settings, a11y specs
```

## Tasks


### Task 1: Server additions for the web app

**Files:**
- Create: `server/internal/requests/detail.go`, `server/internal/requests/detail_test.go`
- Modify: `server/internal/api/api.go`, `server/internal/api/api_test.go`
- Modify: `server/internal/web/web.go`, `server/internal/web/web_test.go`
- Modify: `server/main.go`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes (from 1A): `requests.Service` (`find`, `items`, `logActivity`, `Merge`, `SetStatus`, `MarkSeen`), `requests.Item/Ref/Person`, `testutil.*`, `api.Deps`, `web.Proxy`.
- Produces (Go): `(*requests.Service).Get(id string, viewer *core.Record, team bool) (Detail, error)`; `requests.Detail{Item; Images []string; MergedInto *Ref; Activity []Activity; Thread []Comment}` (JSON: the Item fields plus `images`, `mergedInto`, `activity`, `thread`; `comments` stays the count); `requests.Activity{ID, Kind string; Actor Person; From, To string; Created time.Time}`; `requests.Comment{ID, Body string; Images []string; Internal bool; Author Person; Created time.Time; Replies []Comment}`; `(*Service).MoveBoard(requestID, boardID string, actor *core.Record) error`; `requests.ErrNoBoard`; `(*Service).Stats() (Stats, error)` with `Stats{Total, Waiting, New int64}` (JSON `total`, `waiting`, `new`); `api.Brand{AppName, Logo, PrimaryColor string; ShowLandingCard, RoadmapPublic bool; Features map[string]bool}`; `api.LoadBrand(app) (Brand, error)`; `api.BrandHeader(app) string` (base64 JSON); `web.ConfigHeader = "X-Lort-Config"`; `web.Proxy(target *url.URL, config func() string)`.
- Produces (HTTP): `GET /api/lort/requests/{id}` (public, user or superuser token; 404 "That request is gone." when the viewer may not see it) → `Detail`; `POST /api/lort/requests/{id}/board {board}` (superuser) → 204; `GET /api/lort/stats` (superuser) → `Stats`. Every page request the proxy sends to Next.js carries `X-Lort-Config: <base64 JSON Brand>`; a value sent by the visitor is always replaced.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

Four test files. The request-page tests cover what a visitor, the author, an admin's board profile and the dashboard each see; the proxy test proves a forged config header never reaches Next.js.

`server/internal/requests/detail_test.go`:

```go
package requests_test

import (
	"errors"
	"testing"

	"github.com/pocketbase/dbx"
	"github.com/pocketbase/pocketbase/core"

	"github.com/hacker4ofakind/lort/server/internal/requests"
	"github.com/hacker4ofakind/lort/server/internal/status"
	"github.com/hacker4ofakind/lort/server/internal/testutil"
)

func comment(t *testing.T, app core.App, req, author *core.Record, body, parent string, internal bool) *core.Record {
	t.Helper()
	c, err := app.FindCollectionByNameOrId("comments")
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	r := core.NewRecord(c)
	r.Set("request", req.Id)
	r.Set("author", author.Id)
	r.Set("body", body)
	r.Set("parent", parent)
	r.Set("internal", internal)
	if err := app.Save(r); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return r
}

func TestGetBuildsThePage(t *testing.T) {
	svc, app, board, mara := setup(t)
	luk := testutil.User(t, app, "luk@driftnote.app")
	luk.Set("isAdmin", true)
	if err := app.Save(luk); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	r := testutil.Request(t, app, board, mara, "Sync drops entries", "pending", 0)
	if _, err := svc.SetStatus(r.Id, status.Accept, luk); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	top := comment(t, app, r, mara, "Same on iOS", "", false)
	comment(t, app, r, luk, "Which version?", top.Id, false)
	comment(t, app, r, luk, "Reproduced it", "", true)

	pub, err := svc.Get(r.Id, nil, false)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if pub.Title != "Sync drops entries" || pub.Status != "reviewing" || len(pub.Activity) != 1 || pub.Activity[0].Actor.ID != luk.Id || !pub.Activity[0].Actor.Team {
		t.Fatalf("public page = %+v", pub)
	}
	if len(pub.Thread) != 1 || len(pub.Thread[0].Replies) != 1 || !pub.Thread[0].Replies[0].Author.Team {
		t.Fatalf("public thread = %+v", pub.Thread)
	}
	team, _ := svc.Get(r.Id, nil, true)
	if len(team.Thread) != 2 || !team.Thread[1].Internal {
		t.Errorf("team should see the internal note, got %+v", team.Thread)
	}
	asAdmin, _ := svc.Get(r.Id, luk, false)
	if len(asAdmin.Thread) != 2 {
		t.Errorf("an admin's profile should see internal notes too")
	}
}

func TestGetHidesWhatTheViewerCantSee(t *testing.T) {
	svc, app, board, mara := setup(t)
	devon := testutil.User(t, app, "devon@example.com")
	r := testutil.Request(t, app, board, devon, "Waiting", "pending", 0)
	r.Set("visible", false)
	if err := app.Save(r); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if _, err := svc.Get(r.Id, nil, false); !errors.Is(err, requests.ErrNotFound) {
		t.Errorf("anonymous err = %v", err)
	}
	if _, err := svc.Get(r.Id, mara, false); !errors.Is(err, requests.ErrNotFound) {
		t.Errorf("other user err = %v", err)
	}
	if _, err := svc.Get(r.Id, devon, false); err != nil {
		t.Errorf("the author should see their own request: %v", err)
	}
}

func TestGetShowsWhereAMergedRequestWent(t *testing.T) {
	svc, app, board, mara := setup(t)
	src := testutil.Request(t, app, board, mara, "Night theme", "pending", 0)
	dst := testutil.Request(t, app, board, mara, "Dark mode", "planned", 0)
	if err := svc.Merge(src.Id, dst.Id, mara); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	d, err := svc.Get(src.Id, nil, false)
	if err != nil || d.MergedInto == nil || d.MergedInto.Name != "Dark mode" {
		t.Fatalf("merged page = %+v, %v", d.MergedInto, err)
	}
}

func TestMoveBoard(t *testing.T) {
	svc, app, board, mara := setup(t)
	bugs := testutil.Board(t, app, "Bugs", true, false)
	r := testutil.Request(t, app, board, mara, "Crash", "pending", 0)
	if err := svc.MoveBoard(r.Id, bugs.Id, mara); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	r, _ = app.FindRecordById("requests", r.Id)
	if r.GetString("board") != bugs.Id {
		t.Fatal("board not changed")
	}
	a, err := app.FindFirstRecordByFilter("activity", "request = {:r} && kind = 'board_changed'", dbx.Params{"r": r.Id})
	if err != nil || a.GetString("from") != "Features" || a.GetString("to") != "Bugs" {
		t.Fatalf("activity = %v, %v", a, err)
	}
	if err := svc.MoveBoard(r.Id, "nope", mara); !errors.Is(err, requests.ErrNoBoard) {
		t.Errorf("unknown board err = %v", err)
	}
}

func TestStats(t *testing.T) {
	svc, app, board, mara := setup(t)
	testutil.Request(t, app, board, mara, "Idea A", "pending", 0)
	b := testutil.Request(t, app, board, mara, "Idea B", "reviewing", 0)
	testutil.Request(t, app, board, mara, "Idea C", "planned", 0)
	if err := svc.MarkSeen(b.Id); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	st, err := svc.Stats()
	if err != nil || st.Total != 3 || st.Waiting != 2 || st.New != 2 {
		t.Fatalf("stats = %+v, %v", st, err)
	}
}
```

`server/internal/api/api_test.go`:

```go
package api_test

import (
	"encoding/base64"
	"encoding/json"
	"net/http"
	"strings"
	"testing"

	"github.com/pocketbase/pocketbase/core"
	"github.com/pocketbase/pocketbase/tests"

	"github.com/hacker4ofakind/lort/server/internal/api"
	"github.com/hacker4ofakind/lort/server/internal/config"
	"github.com/hacker4ofakind/lort/server/internal/requests"
	"github.com/hacker4ofakind/lort/server/internal/testutil"
)

type fixture struct {
	app         *tests.TestApp
	board, mara *core.Record
	su          *core.Record
	req         *core.Record
}

func newFixture(t testing.TB) *fixture {
	app := testutil.NewApp(t)
	svc := requests.New(app)
	app.OnServe().BindFunc(func(se *core.ServeEvent) error {
		api.Register(se, api.Deps{Svc: svc, Cfg: config.Config{URL: "https://f.example"}})
		return se.Next()
	})
	f := &fixture{app: app}
	f.board = testutil.Board(t, app, "Features", true, false)
	f.mara = testutil.User(t, app, "mara@example.com")
	f.su = testutil.Superuser(t, app, "luk@driftnote.app")
	f.req = testutil.Request(t, app, f.board, f.mara, "Dark mode for the editor", "reviewing", 0)
	return f
}

func (f *fixture) run(t *testing.T, s tests.ApiScenario) {
	s.TestAppFactory = func(testing.TB) *tests.TestApp { return f.app }
	s.DisableTestAppCleanup = true
	s.Test(t)
}

func TestPublicEndpoints(t *testing.T) {
	f := newFixture(t)
	f.run(t, tests.ApiScenario{Method: http.MethodGet, URL: "/api/lort/health", ExpectedStatus: 200, ExpectedContent: []string{`"ok":true`}})
	f.run(t, tests.ApiScenario{Method: http.MethodGet, URL: "/api/lort/config", ExpectedStatus: 200,
		ExpectedContent: []string{`"primaryColor":"#cc3a6d"`, `"name":"Features"`, `"email":false`}})
	f.run(t, tests.ApiScenario{Method: http.MethodGet, URL: "/api/lort/requests?status=reviewing&sort=top", ExpectedStatus: 200,
		ExpectedContent: []string{`"title":"Dark mode for the editor"`, `"totalItems":1`}})
	f.run(t, tests.ApiScenario{Method: http.MethodGet, URL: "/api/lort/similar?q=dark", ExpectedStatus: 200,
		ExpectedContent: []string{`"Dark mode for the editor"`}})
}

func TestVoteNeedsAUser(t *testing.T) {
	f := newFixture(t)
	f.run(t, tests.ApiScenario{Method: http.MethodPost, URL: "/api/lort/requests/" + f.req.Id + "/vote", ExpectedStatus: 401, ExpectedContent: []string{`"data":{}`}})
	f.run(t, tests.ApiScenario{Method: http.MethodPost, URL: "/api/lort/requests/" + f.req.Id + "/vote",
		Headers:        map[string]string{"Authorization": testutil.Token(t, f.mara)},
		ExpectedStatus: 200, ExpectedContent: []string{`"voted":true`, `"votes":1`}})
}

func TestTriageIsTeamOnly(t *testing.T) {
	f := newFixture(t)
	body := `{"action":"roadmap"}`
	f.run(t, tests.ApiScenario{Method: http.MethodPost, URL: "/api/lort/requests/" + f.req.Id + "/status",
		Headers: map[string]string{"Authorization": testutil.Token(t, f.mara)}, Body: strings.NewReader(body),
		ExpectedStatus: 403, ExpectedContent: []string{`"data":{}`}})
	f.run(t, tests.ApiScenario{Method: http.MethodPost, URL: "/api/lort/requests/" + f.req.Id + "/status",
		Headers: map[string]string{"Authorization": testutil.Token(t, f.su)}, Body: strings.NewReader(body),
		ExpectedStatus: 200, ExpectedContent: []string{`"to":"planned"`, `"roadmap":"added"`}})
	f.run(t, tests.ApiScenario{Method: http.MethodPost, URL: "/api/lort/requests/" + f.req.Id + "/status",
		Headers: map[string]string{"Authorization": testutil.Token(t, f.su)}, Body: strings.NewReader(`{"action":"complete"}`),
		ExpectedStatus: 400, ExpectedContent: []string{"not available"}})
}

func TestTeamMeReturnsProfileToken(t *testing.T) {
	f := newFixture(t)
	f.run(t, tests.ApiScenario{Method: http.MethodGet, URL: "/api/lort/team/me",
		Headers: map[string]string{"Authorization": testutil.Token(t, f.su)}, ExpectedStatus: 200,
		ExpectedContent: []string{`"isAdmin":true`, `"token":"`}})
}

func TestTeamInviteAndResetLink(t *testing.T) {
	f := newFixture(t)
	auth := map[string]string{"Authorization": testutil.Token(t, f.su)}
	f.run(t, tests.ApiScenario{Method: http.MethodPost, URL: "/api/lort/team/invite", Headers: auth,
		Body: strings.NewReader(`{"email":"sofia@driftnote.app"}`), ExpectedStatus: 200,
		ExpectedContent: []string{`https://f.example/dashboard/invite?token=`}})
	f.run(t, tests.ApiScenario{Method: http.MethodPost, URL: "/api/lort/users/" + f.mara.Id + "/reset-link", Headers: auth,
		ExpectedStatus: 200, ExpectedContent: []string{`https://f.example/reset-password?token=`}})
	f.run(t, tests.ApiScenario{Method: http.MethodGet, URL: "/api/lort/users/" + f.mara.Id, Headers: auth,
		ExpectedStatus: 200, ExpectedContent: []string{`"email":"mara@example.com"`, `"requests":1`}})
}

func TestRequestPage(t *testing.T) {
	f := newFixture(t)
	f.run(t, tests.ApiScenario{Method: http.MethodGet, URL: "/api/lort/requests/" + f.req.Id, ExpectedStatus: 200,
		ExpectedContent: []string{`"title":"Dark mode for the editor"`, `"thread":[]`, `"comments":0`, `"activity":[]`}})
	f.run(t, tests.ApiScenario{Method: http.MethodGet, URL: "/api/lort/requests/nope", ExpectedStatus: 404,
		ExpectedContent: []string{"That request is gone."}})
}

func TestMoveBoardAndStatsAreTeamOnly(t *testing.T) {
	f := newFixture(t)
	bugs := testutil.Board(t, f.app, "Bugs", true, false)
	body := `{"board":"` + bugs.Id + `"}`
	f.run(t, tests.ApiScenario{Method: http.MethodPost, URL: "/api/lort/requests/" + f.req.Id + "/board",
		Headers: map[string]string{"Authorization": testutil.Token(t, f.mara)}, Body: strings.NewReader(body),
		ExpectedStatus: 403, ExpectedContent: []string{`"data":{}`}})
	f.run(t, tests.ApiScenario{Method: http.MethodPost, URL: "/api/lort/requests/" + f.req.Id + "/board",
		Headers: map[string]string{"Authorization": testutil.Token(t, f.su)}, Body: strings.NewReader(body),
		ExpectedStatus: 204})
	f.run(t, tests.ApiScenario{Method: http.MethodGet, URL: "/api/lort/stats",
		Headers: map[string]string{"Authorization": testutil.Token(t, f.su)}, ExpectedStatus: 200,
		ExpectedContent: []string{`"total":1`, `"waiting":1`, `"new":1`}})
}

func TestBrandHeaderIsBase64JSON(t *testing.T) {
	f := newFixture(t)
	raw, err := base64.StdEncoding.DecodeString(api.BrandHeader(f.app))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	var b api.Brand
	if err := json.Unmarshal(raw, &b); err != nil || b.PrimaryColor != "#cc3a6d" || b.AppName != "My app" {
		t.Fatalf("brand = %+v, %v", b, err)
	}
}
```

`server/internal/web/web_test.go`:

```go
package web_test

import (
	"context"
	"io"
	"log/slog"
	"net/http"
	"net/http/httptest"
	"net/url"
	"os"
	"path/filepath"
	"strings"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/pocketbase/pocketbase/core"
	"github.com/pocketbase/pocketbase/tests"

	"github.com/hacker4ofakind/lort/server/internal/testutil"
	"github.com/hacker4ofakind/lort/server/internal/web"
)

func TestProxyForwardsAndExplainsOutages(t *testing.T) {
	next := httptest.NewServer(http.HandlerFunc(func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
		_, _ = io.WriteString(w, "next says hi to "+r.URL.Path)
	}))
	up, _ := url.Parse(next.URL)
	down, _ := url.Parse("http://127.0.0.1:1")

	for _, c := range []struct {
		target *url.URL
		status int
		want   string
	}{{up, 200, "next says hi to /feedback"}, {down, 502, "starting up"}} {
		app := testutil.NewApp(t)
		target := c.target
		app.OnServe().BindFunc(func(se *core.ServeEvent) error {
			se.Router.Any("/{path...}", web.Proxy(target, func() string { return "" }))
			return se.Next()
		})
		(&tests.ApiScenario{
			Method: http.MethodGet, URL: "/feedback", ExpectedStatus: c.status, ExpectedContent: []string{c.want},
			TestAppFactory: func(testing.TB) *tests.TestApp { return app }, DisableTestAppCleanup: true,
		}).Test(t)
	}
}

func TestProxyReplacesTheConfigHeader(t *testing.T) {
	next := httptest.NewServer(http.HandlerFunc(func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
		_, _ = io.WriteString(w, "config="+r.Header.Get(web.ConfigHeader))
	}))
	target, _ := url.Parse(next.URL)
	app := testutil.NewApp(t)
	app.OnServe().BindFunc(func(se *core.ServeEvent) error {
		se.Router.Any("/{path...}", web.Proxy(target, func() string { return "e30=" }))
		return se.Next()
	})
	(&tests.ApiScenario{
		Method: http.MethodGet, URL: "/feedback", Headers: map[string]string{web.ConfigHeader: "forged"},
		ExpectedStatus: 200, ExpectedContent: []string{"config=e30="}, NotExpectedContent: []string{"forged"},
		TestAppFactory: func(testing.TB) *tests.TestApp { return app }, DisableTestAppCleanup: true,
	}).Test(t)
}

func TestSuperviseRestarts(t *testing.T) {
	marker := filepath.Join(t.TempDir(), "runs")
	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), 2500*time.Millisecond)
	defer cancel()
	web.Supervise(ctx, []string{"sh", "-c", "echo x >> " + marker}, slog.New(slog.NewTextHandler(io.Discard, nil)))
	data, _ := os.ReadFile(marker)
	if runs := strings.Count(string(data), "x"); runs < 2 {
		t.Errorf("runs = %d, want at least 2", runs)
	}
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement the request page, board moves and counts**

`Get` applies the same visibility as the requests view rule (public, or the viewer's own) unless the caller is the dashboard or an admin's board profile, who also see internal notes. `MoveBoard` records a `board_changed` timeline entry. `Stats` leaves merged requests out.

`server/internal/requests/detail.go`:

```go
package requests

import (
	"errors"
	"time"

	"github.com/pocketbase/dbx"
	"github.com/pocketbase/pocketbase/core"
)

var ErrNoBoard = errors.New("pick a board that exists")

// Detail is everything a request page shows.
type Detail struct {
	Item
	Images     []string   `json:"images"`
	MergedInto *Ref       `json:"mergedInto"`
	Activity   []Activity `json:"activity"`
	// Thread is the comments with their replies. (Item.Comments is the count.)
	Thread []Comment `json:"thread"`
}

// Activity is one timeline entry.
type Activity struct {
	ID      string    `json:"id"`
	Kind    string    `json:"kind"`
	Actor   Person    `json:"actor"`
	From    string    `json:"from"`
	To      string    `json:"to"`
	Created time.Time `json:"created"`
}

// Comment is a top-level comment with its replies, or a reply (Replies empty).
type Comment struct {
	ID       string    `json:"id"`
	Body     string    `json:"body"`
	Images   []string  `json:"images"`
	Internal bool      `json:"internal"`
	Author   Person    `json:"author"`
	Created  time.Time `json:"created"`
	Replies  []Comment `json:"replies"`
}

// Get returns a request page. team is a superuser; viewer is a signed-in board user or nil.
// Anyone who can't see the request gets ErrNotFound, the same as for a missing one.
func (s *Service) Get(id string, viewer *core.Record, team bool) (Detail, error) {
	r, err := s.find(s.App, id)
	if err != nil {
		return Detail{}, err
	}
	insider := team || (viewer != nil && viewer.GetBool("isAdmin"))
	if !insider {
		ok, err := publicOrOwn(s.App, r, viewer)
		if err != nil || !ok {
			return Detail{}, ErrNotFound
		}
	}
	items, err := s.items([]string{r.Id}, viewer, insider)
	if err != nil || len(items) == 0 {
		return Detail{}, ErrNotFound
	}
	d := Detail{Item: items[0], Images: r.GetStringSlice("images"), Activity: []Activity{}, Thread: []Comment{}}

	if target := r.GetString("mergedInto"); target != "" {
		if t, err := s.App.FindRecordById("requests", target); err == nil {
			if ok, _ := publicOrOwn(s.App, t, viewer); ok || insider {
				d.MergedInto = &Ref{ID: t.Id, Name: t.GetString("title")}
			}
		}
	}

	acts, err := s.App.FindRecordsByFilter("activity", "request = {:r}", "created", 0, 0, dbx.Params{"r": r.Id})
	if err != nil {
		return Detail{}, err
	}
	s.App.ExpandRecords(acts, []string{"actor"}, nil)
	for _, a := range acts {
		d.Activity = append(d.Activity, Activity{
			ID: a.Id, Kind: a.GetString("kind"), Actor: person(a.ExpandedOne("actor"), a.GetString("actorName")),
			From: a.GetString("from"), To: a.GetString("to"), Created: a.GetDateTime("created").Time(),
		})
	}

	filter := "request = {:r}"
	if !insider {
		filter += " && internal = false"
	}
	cs, err := s.App.FindRecordsByFilter("comments", filter, "created", 0, 0, dbx.Params{"r": r.Id})
	if err != nil {
		return Detail{}, err
	}
	s.App.ExpandRecords(cs, []string{"author"}, nil)
	index := map[string]int{}
	for _, c := range cs {
		cm := Comment{
			ID: c.Id, Body: c.GetString("body"), Images: c.GetStringSlice("images"), Internal: c.GetBool("internal"),
			Author: person(c.ExpandedOne("author"), "Deleted account"), Created: c.GetDateTime("created").Time(), Replies: []Comment{},
		}
		if p := c.GetString("parent"); p != "" {
			if i, ok := index[p]; ok {
				d.Thread[i].Replies = append(d.Thread[i].Replies, cm)
			}
			continue
		}
		index[c.Id] = len(d.Thread)
		d.Thread = append(d.Thread, cm)
	}
	return d, nil
}

// publicOrOwn matches the requests view rule: visible on a public board, or the viewer's own.
func publicOrOwn(app core.App, r *core.Record, viewer *core.Record) (bool, error) {
	if viewer != nil && r.GetString("author") == viewer.Id {
		return true, nil
	}
	if !r.GetBool("visible") {
		return false, nil
	}
	b, err := app.FindRecordById("boards", r.GetString("board"))
	if err != nil {
		return false, err
	}
	return b.GetBool("isPublic"), nil
}

func person(r *core.Record, fallback string) Person {
	if r == nil {
		return Person{Name: fallback}
	}
	return Person{ID: r.Id, Name: r.GetString("name"), Avatar: r.GetString("avatar"), Team: r.GetBool("isAdmin")}
}

// MoveBoard moves a request to another board and records it on the timeline.
func (s *Service) MoveBoard(requestID, boardID string, actor *core.Record) error {
	return s.App.RunInTransaction(func(tx core.App) error {
		r, err := s.find(tx, requestID)
		if err != nil {
			return err
		}
		to, err := tx.FindRecordById("boards", boardID)
		if err != nil {
			return ErrNoBoard
		}
		if r.GetString("board") == to.Id {
			return nil
		}
		fromName := ""
		if from, err := tx.FindRecordById("boards", r.GetString("board")); err == nil {
			fromName = from.GetString("name")
		}
		r.Set("board", to.Id)
		if err := tx.Save(r); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		return logActivity(tx, r.Id, actor, "board_changed", fromName, to.GetString("name"))
	})
}

// Stats are the dashboard's counts. Merged requests are left out.
type Stats struct {
	Total   int64 `json:"total"`
	Waiting int64 `json:"waiting"` // pending or reviewing
	New     int64 `json:"new"`     // not opened by the team yet
}

func (s *Service) Stats() (Stats, error) {
	live := dbx.HashExp{"mergedInto": ""}
	var st Stats
	var err error
	if st.Total, err = s.App.CountRecords("requests", live); err != nil {
		return st, err
	}
	if st.Waiting, err = s.App.CountRecords("requests", live, dbx.In("status", "pending", "reviewing")); err != nil {
		return st, err
	}
	st.New, err = s.App.CountRecords("requests", live, dbx.HashExp{"teamSeenAt": ""})
	return st, err
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Add the endpoints and the brand header**

The whole file, with `get`, `moveBoard`, `stats`, the shared `viewer` helper and `LoadBrand`/`BrandHeader` added and `config` now built on `LoadBrand`:

`server/internal/api/api.go`:

```go
// Package api serves Lort's own endpoints under /api/lort.
package api

import (
	"encoding/base64"
	"encoding/json"
	"errors"
	"net/http"
	"strconv"
	"strings"

	"github.com/pocketbase/pocketbase/apis"
	"github.com/pocketbase/pocketbase/core"

	"github.com/hacker4ofakind/lort/server/internal/config"
	"github.com/hacker4ofakind/lort/server/internal/requests"
	"github.com/hacker4ofakind/lort/server/internal/status"
	"github.com/hacker4ofakind/lort/server/internal/team"
)

type Deps struct {
	Svc *requests.Service
	Cfg config.Config
}

// Register adds every /api/lort route.
func Register(se *core.ServeEvent, d Deps) {
	g := se.Router.Group("/api/lort")
	g.GET("/health", func(e *core.RequestEvent) error {
		return e.JSON(http.StatusOK, map[string]any{"ok": true})
	})
	g.GET("/config", d.config)
	g.GET("/requests", d.list)
	g.GET("/requests/{id}", d.get)
	g.GET("/similar", d.similar)
	g.POST("/requests/{id}/vote", d.vote).Bind(apis.RequireAuth("users"))

	team := g.Group("").Bind(apis.RequireSuperuserAuth())
	team.POST("/requests/{id}/status", d.setStatus)
	team.POST("/requests/{id}/undo", d.undo)
	team.POST("/requests/{id}/merge", d.merge)
	team.POST("/requests/{id}/seen", d.seen)
	team.POST("/requests/{id}/board", d.moveBoard)
	team.GET("/stats", d.stats)
	team.GET("/team", d.teamList)
	team.GET("/team/me", d.teamMe)
	team.POST("/team/invite", d.teamInvite)
	team.DELETE("/team/{id}", d.teamRemove)
	team.GET("/users/{id}", d.userSummary)
	team.POST("/users/{id}/ban", d.userBan)
	team.POST("/users/{id}/reset-link", d.userResetLink)
}

// fail maps service errors to friendly HTTP errors.
func fail(e *core.RequestEvent, err error) error {
	switch {
	case errors.Is(err, requests.ErrNotFound):
		return e.NotFoundError("That request is gone.", nil)
	case errors.Is(err, requests.ErrNotVoting), errors.Is(err, requests.ErrUndoGone),
		errors.Is(err, requests.ErrBadMerge), errors.Is(err, requests.ErrNoBoard), errors.Is(err, team.ErrRemoveSelf), errors.Is(err, team.ErrLastAdmin):
		return e.BadRequestError(err.Error(), nil)
	default:
		return e.BadRequestError(err.Error(), nil)
	}
}

// actor is the signed-in superuser's users profile, used for timeline entries and comments.
func actor(e *core.RequestEvent) (*core.Record, error) {
	return team.Profile(e.App, e.Auth)
}

// Brand is what every page needs before it renders: the owner's branding and which
// optional features are on. The proxy also hands it to Next.js in the X-Lort-Config header.
type Brand struct {
	AppName         string          `json:"appName"`
	Logo            string          `json:"logo"`
	PrimaryColor    string          `json:"primaryColor"`
	ShowLandingCard bool            `json:"showLandingCard"`
	RoadmapPublic   bool            `json:"roadmapPublic"`
	Features        map[string]bool `json:"features"`
}

func LoadBrand(app core.App) (Brand, error) {
	settings, err := app.FindFirstRecordByFilter("settings", "")
	if err != nil {
		return Brand{}, err
	}
	logo := ""
	if f := settings.GetString("logo"); f != "" {
		logo = "/api/files/settings/" + settings.Id + "/" + f
	}
	return Brand{
		AppName:         settings.GetString("appName"),
		Logo:            logo,
		PrimaryColor:    settings.GetString("primaryColor"),
		ShowLandingCard: settings.GetBool("showLandingCard"),
		RoadmapPublic:   settings.GetBool("roadmapPublic"),
		Features:        map[string]bool{"email": false, "voice": false, "turnstile": false},
	}, nil
}

// BrandHeader is the brand as base64 JSON for the X-Lort-Config header, or "" if it can't be read.
func BrandHeader(app core.App) string {
	b, err := LoadBrand(app)
	if err != nil {
		return ""
	}
	raw, err := json.Marshal(b)
	if err != nil {
		return ""
	}
	return base64.StdEncoding.EncodeToString(raw)
}

func (d Deps) config(e *core.RequestEvent) error {
	brand, err := LoadBrand(e.App)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	boards, err := e.App.FindRecordsByFilter("boards", "isPublic = true", "order", 0, 0)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	tags, err := e.App.FindRecordsByFilter("tags", "", "order", 0, 0)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	type ref struct {
		ID   string `json:"id"`
		Name string `json:"name"`
	}
	toRefs := func(rs []*core.Record) []ref {
		out := make([]ref, len(rs))
		for i, r := range rs {
			out[i] = ref{r.Id, r.GetString("name")}
		}
		return out
	}
	return e.JSON(http.StatusOK, struct {
		Brand
		Boards []ref `json:"boards"`
		Tags   []ref `json:"tags"`
	}{brand, toRefs(boards), toRefs(tags)})
}

func csv(v string) []string {
	if v == "" {
		return nil
	}
	return strings.Split(v, ",")
}

// viewer is the signed-in board user, or nil for visitors and superusers.
func viewer(e *core.RequestEvent) *core.Record {
	if e.Auth != nil && e.Auth.Collection().Name == "users" {
		return e.Auth
	}
	return nil
}

func (d Deps) list(e *core.RequestEvent) error {
	q := e.Request.URL.Query()
	page, _ := strconv.Atoi(q.Get("page"))
	perPage, _ := strconv.Atoi(q.Get("perPage"))
	viewer := viewer(e)
	res, err := d.Svc.List(requests.Query{
		Sort: q.Get("sort"), Statuses: csv(q.Get("status")), Boards: csv(q.Get("board")),
		Tags: csv(q.Get("tag")), Visibility: q.Get("visibility"), Search: q.Get("q"),
		Page: page, PerPage: perPage,
	}, viewer, e.HasSuperuserAuth())
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	return e.JSON(http.StatusOK, res)
}

func (d Deps) get(e *core.RequestEvent) error {
	page, err := d.Svc.Get(e.Request.PathValue("id"), viewer(e), e.HasSuperuserAuth())
	if err != nil {
		return fail(e, err)
	}
	return e.JSON(http.StatusOK, page)
}

func (d Deps) moveBoard(e *core.RequestEvent) error {
	var body struct {
		Board string `json:"board"`
	}
	if err := e.BindBody(&body); err != nil || body.Board == "" {
		return e.BadRequestError("Pick a board.", err)
	}
	who, err := actor(e)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	if err := d.Svc.MoveBoard(e.Request.PathValue("id"), body.Board, who); err != nil {
		return fail(e, err)
	}
	return e.NoContent(http.StatusNoContent)
}

func (d Deps) stats(e *core.RequestEvent) error {
	st, err := d.Svc.Stats()
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	return e.JSON(http.StatusOK, st)
}

func (d Deps) similar(e *core.RequestEvent) error {
	items, err := d.Svc.Similar(e.Request.URL.Query().Get("q"), 3)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	return e.JSON(http.StatusOK, map[string]any{"items": items})
}

func (d Deps) vote(e *core.RequestEvent) error {
	if e.Auth.GetBool("banned") {
		return e.ForbiddenError("Your account can't vote on this board.", nil)
	}
	voted, count, err := d.Svc.ToggleVote(e.Request.PathValue("id"), e.Auth.Id)
	if err != nil {
		return fail(e, err)
	}
	return e.JSON(http.StatusOK, map[string]any{"voted": voted, "votes": count})
}

func (d Deps) setStatus(e *core.RequestEvent) error {
	var body struct {
		Action status.Action `json:"action"`
	}
	if err := e.BindBody(&body); err != nil {
		return e.BadRequestError("Send an action.", err)
	}
	who, err := actor(e)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	ch, err := d.Svc.SetStatus(e.Request.PathValue("id"), body.Action, who)
	if err != nil {
		return fail(e, err)
	}
	return e.JSON(http.StatusOK, ch)
}

func (d Deps) undo(e *core.RequestEvent) error {
	ch, err := d.Svc.UndoComplete(e.Request.PathValue("id"))
	if err != nil {
		return fail(e, err)
	}
	return e.JSON(http.StatusOK, ch)
}

func (d Deps) merge(e *core.RequestEvent) error {
	var body struct {
		Target string `json:"target"`
	}
	if err := e.BindBody(&body); err != nil || body.Target == "" {
		return e.BadRequestError("Pick a request to merge into.", err)
	}
	who, err := actor(e)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	if err := d.Svc.Merge(e.Request.PathValue("id"), body.Target, who); err != nil {
		return fail(e, err)
	}
	return e.NoContent(http.StatusNoContent)
}

func (d Deps) seen(e *core.RequestEvent) error {
	if err := d.Svc.MarkSeen(e.Request.PathValue("id")); err != nil {
		return fail(e, err)
	}
	return e.NoContent(http.StatusNoContent)
}

func (d Deps) teamList(e *core.RequestEvent) error {
	members, err := team.List(e.App)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	return e.JSON(http.StatusOK, map[string]any{"items": members, "you": e.Auth.Id})
}

// teamMe returns the admin's public profile and a token for it, so the dashboard can
// comment, vote and suggest as that profile on the public board.
func (d Deps) teamMe(e *core.RequestEvent) error {
	p, err := actor(e)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	token, err := p.NewAuthToken()
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	return e.JSON(http.StatusOK, map[string]any{"profile": p, "token": token})
}

func (d Deps) teamInvite(e *core.RequestEvent) error {
	var body struct {
		Email string `json:"email"`
	}
	if err := e.BindBody(&body); err != nil || !strings.Contains(body.Email, "@") {
		return e.BadRequestError("Enter an email address.", err)
	}
	link, err := team.Invite(e.App, d.Cfg.URL, strings.TrimSpace(body.Email))
	if err != nil {
		return fail(e, err)
	}
	return e.JSON(http.StatusOK, map[string]any{"link": link})
}

func (d Deps) teamRemove(e *core.RequestEvent) error {
	if err := team.Remove(e.App, e.Auth, e.Request.PathValue("id")); err != nil {
		return fail(e, err)
	}
	return e.NoContent(http.StatusNoContent)
}

func (d Deps) userSummary(e *core.RequestEvent) error {
	s, err := team.UserSummary(e.App, e.Request.PathValue("id"))
	if err != nil {
		return e.NotFoundError("That person is gone.", nil)
	}
	return e.JSON(http.StatusOK, s)
}

func (d Deps) userBan(e *core.RequestEvent) error {
	var body struct {
		Banned bool `json:"banned"`
	}
	if err := e.BindBody(&body); err != nil {
		return e.BadRequestError("Send banned: true or false.", err)
	}
	if err := team.SetBanned(e.App, e.Request.PathValue("id"), body.Banned); err != nil {
		return fail(e, err)
	}
	return e.NoContent(http.StatusNoContent)
}

func (d Deps) userResetLink(e *core.RequestEvent) error {
	link, err := team.ResetLink(e.App, d.Cfg.URL, e.Request.PathValue("id"))
	if err != nil {
		return fail(e, err)
	}
	return e.JSON(http.StatusOK, map[string]any{"link": link})
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Pass the brand to Next.js and fix the supervisor's log line**

The proxy now deletes any incoming `X-Lort-Config` and sets its own. The supervisor logs the backoff as a string: PocketBase's JSON log store can't encode a `time.Duration` and dropped the whole line.

`server/internal/web/web.go`:

```go
// Package web proxies page requests to the Next.js server and keeps that server running.
package web

import (
	"context"
	"log/slog"
	"net/http"
	"net/http/httputil"
	"net/url"
	"os"
	"os/exec"
	"time"

	"github.com/pocketbase/pocketbase/core"
)

const unavailable = `<!doctype html><meta charset="utf-8"><title>Starting up</title>
<body style="font-family:system-ui;background:#1a1716;color:#f5efea;display:grid;place-items:center;height:100vh;margin:0">
<p>The board is starting up. Refresh in a few seconds.</p>`

// ConfigHeader carries the board's branding from the Go server to Next.js. The proxy always
// replaces it, so a visitor can't send their own.
const ConfigHeader = "X-Lort-Config"

// Proxy returns a handler that forwards everything to target, adding config() as ConfigHeader.
func Proxy(target *url.URL, config func() string) func(*core.RequestEvent) error {
	p := httputil.NewSingleHostReverseProxy(target)
	p.ErrorHandler = func(w http.ResponseWriter, _ *http.Request, _ error) {
		w.Header().Set("Content-Type", "text/html; charset=utf-8")
		w.Header().Set("Retry-After", "5")
		w.WriteHeader(http.StatusBadGateway)
		_, _ = w.Write([]byte(unavailable))
	}
	return func(e *core.RequestEvent) error {
		e.Request.Header.Del(ConfigHeader)
		if v := config(); v != "" {
			e.Request.Header.Set(ConfigHeader, v)
		}
		p.ServeHTTP(e.Response, e.Request)
		return nil
	}
}

// Supervise runs cmd until ctx ends, restarting it with backoff (1s doubling to 30s)
// whenever it exits. A run that lasted over a minute resets the backoff.
func Supervise(ctx context.Context, cmd []string, log *slog.Logger) {
	backoff := time.Second
	for ctx.Err() == nil {
		started := time.Now()
		c := exec.CommandContext(ctx, cmd[0], cmd[1:]...)
		c.Stdout, c.Stderr = os.Stdout, os.Stderr
		c.Env = append(os.Environ(), "HOSTNAME=127.0.0.1", "PORT=3000")
		err := c.Run()
		if ctx.Err() != nil {
			return
		}
		if time.Since(started) > time.Minute {
			backoff = time.Second
		}
		// String it: PocketBase's JSON log store can't encode a time.Duration and drops the line.
		log.Warn("web server exited, restarting", "err", err, "in", backoff.String())
		select {
		case <-ctx.Done():
			return
		case <-time.After(backoff):
		}
		backoff = min(backoff*2, 30*time.Second)
	}
}
```

`server/main.go`:

```go
// Command lort is the whole Lort server: PocketBase, Lort's rules and API, HTTPS, and the
// supervisor and proxy for the Next.js web app.
package main

import (
	"context"
	"log"
	"net/url"
	"os"
	"strings"

	"github.com/pocketbase/pocketbase"
	"github.com/pocketbase/pocketbase/core"

	"github.com/hacker4ofakind/lort/server/internal/api"
	"github.com/hacker4ofakind/lort/server/internal/config"
	"github.com/hacker4ofakind/lort/server/internal/hooks"
	"github.com/hacker4ofakind/lort/server/internal/requests"
	"github.com/hacker4ofakind/lort/server/internal/schema"
	"github.com/hacker4ofakind/lort/server/internal/team"
	"github.com/hacker4ofakind/lort/server/internal/web"
)

func main() {
	cfg := config.FromEnv()
	if len(os.Args) == 1 {
		os.Args = append(os.Args, cfg.ServeArgs()...)
	}

	app := pocketbase.NewWithConfig(pocketbase.Config{DefaultDataDir: cfg.DataDir})
	svc := requests.New(app)
	hooks.Register(app, svc)

	webURL, err := url.Parse(cfg.WebURL)
	if err != nil {
		log.Fatalf("LORT_WEB_URL: %v", err)
	}

	app.OnServe().BindFunc(func(se *core.ServeEvent) error {
		if err := schema.Ensure(se.App); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		if err := team.SeedFirstAdmin(se.App, cfg.AdminEmail, cfg.AdminPassword); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		se.App.Cron().MustAdd("lort_trending", "0 * * * *", func() {
			if err := svc.RecomputeTrending(); err != nil {
				se.App.Logger().Error("trending", "err", err)
			}
		})

		if !cfg.PBAdmin {
			se.Router.BindFunc(func(e *core.RequestEvent) error {
				if strings.HasPrefix(e.Request.URL.Path, "/_/") {
					return e.NotFoundError("", nil)
				}
				return e.Next()
			})
		}
		api.Register(se, api.Deps{Svc: svc, Cfg: cfg})
		se.Router.Any("/{path...}", web.Proxy(webURL, func() string { return api.BrandHeader(se.App) }))

		if len(cfg.WebCmd) > 0 {
			ctx, cancel := context.WithCancel(context.Background())
			se.App.OnTerminate().BindFunc(func(e *core.TerminateEvent) error {
				cancel()
				return e.Next()
			})
			go web.Supervise(ctx, cfg.WebCmd, se.App.Logger())
		}
		return se.Next()
	})

	if err := app.Start(); err != nil {
		log.Fatal(err)
	}
}
```

### Task 2: Scaffold the web app, its styles and the design system

**Files:**
- Create: `web/` (scaffolded by the shadcn CLI), then `web/.prettierrc`, `web/eslint.config.mjs`, `web/next.config.ts`, `web/vitest.config.mts`, `web/vitest.setup.ts`
- Create: `web/app/tokens.css`, `web/app/lort.css`, `web/app/app.css`; replace `web/app/globals.css`, `web/app/layout.tsx`, `web/app/page.tsx`
- Create: `web/components/lort/*` (ported from `design/components/src`), `web/components/lort/__tests__/components.test.tsx`
- Modify: `design/components/src/Composer.tsx` (thumbnail previews derived, not copied into state)
- Delete: `web/components/theme-provider.tsx`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `design/components/src/*.tsx`, `design/components/lort.css`, the tokens in `DESIGN.md`.
- Produces: the Next.js app in `web/` (Next 16, React 19, Tailwind 4, shadcn `radix-nova` style with Tabler icons); shadcn primitives `@/components/ui/{dialog,popover,dropdown-menu,sheet,tooltip,command}`; the design-system components exported from `@/components/lort` (same names and props as `design/components/src`, plus: `RequestCard` links with `next/link`; `FloatingBar.boards` is `{ value: string; label: string }[]` (value = board id)); CSS classes from `lort.css` (`lt-*`) and `app.css` (`lt-dialog`, `lt-field`, `lt-label`, `lt-hint`, `lt-error`, `lt-segment(-item)`, `lt-toggle-tag`, `lt-topbar`, `lt-page`, `lt-floating`, `sk`, `sk-card`, `lt-empty`, `dash*`, `side-item`, `rq-*`, `sec`, `set-*`, `sw`, `swatch`, `grip`, `badge`, `lt-pop(-row)`). Tailwind colour names (`bg-surface`, `text-ink`, `text-muted-foreground`…) map to Lort tokens.

```js
import { defineConfig, globalIgnores } from 'eslint/config';
import nextVitals from 'eslint-config-next/core-web-vitals';
import nextTs from 'eslint-config-next/typescript';

const eslintConfig = defineConfig([
  ...nextVitals,
  ...nextTs,
  {
    rules: {
      // Images are people's uploads served by PocketBase (some protected by file tokens);
      // next/image would re-encode them through the Next server for no gain.
      '@next/next/no-img-element': 'off',

  // Override default ignores of eslint-config-next.
  globalIgnores([

    '.next/**',
    'out/**',
    'build/**',
    'next-env.d.ts',
    'playwright-report/**',
    'test-results/**',
  ]),
]);

export default eslintConfig;
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the failing component tests**

They pin the behaviour the app relies on: the sticker's counts and labels, the status label rule, the triage actions per status, the one-board rule in the floating bar, and the composer keeping its draft when a send fails.

`web/components/lort/__tests__/components.test.tsx`:

```tsx
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { render, screen, waitFor } from '@testing-library/react';
import userEvent from '@testing-library/user-event';

import { Composer, FloatingBar, StatusIcon, StatusText, TriageBar, VoteSticker } from '@/components/lort';

describe('VoteSticker', () => {
  it('slaps on and peels off, counting the viewer', async () => {
    const onVotedChange = vi.fn();
    render(<VoteSticker count={4} subject="Dark mode" onVotedChange={onVotedChange} />);
    const button = screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Upvote Dark mode, 4 votes' });
    await userEvent.click(button);
    expect(button.getAttribute('aria-pressed')).toBe('true');
    expect(button.textContent).toBe('5');
    expect(onVotedChange).toHaveBeenLastCalledWith(true);
    await userEvent.click(button);
    expect(button.textContent).toBe('4');
  });

  it('is plain text when read-only', () => {
    render(<VoteSticker count={48} readOnly />);
    expect(screen.queryByRole('button')).toBeNull();
    expect(screen.getByLabelText('48 votes')).toBeTruthy();
  });
});

describe('Status label rule', () => {
  it('names an icon-only status and shows the label once otherwise', () => {
    const { container, rerender } = render(<StatusIcon status="progress" />);
    expect(screen.getByRole('img', { name: 'In progress' })).toBeTruthy();
    rerender(<StatusIcon status="progress" showLabel />);
    expect(container.textContent).toBe('In progress');
    rerender(<StatusText status="completed" />);
    expect(screen.getByText('Completed').getAttribute('style')).toContain('--status-completed-text');
  });
});

describe('TriageBar', () => {
  it('offers the actions for the status, forward action last', async () => {
    const onAction = vi.fn();
    render(<TriageBar status="reviewing" onAction={onAction} />);
    const names = screen.getAllByRole('button').map((b) => b.textContent);
    expect(names).toEqual(['Close', 'Merge', 'Skip', 'Add to roadmap']);
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Add to roadmap' }));
    expect(onAction.mock.calls[0][0]).toMatchObject({ id: 'roadmap', next: 'planned' });
  });
});

describe('FloatingBar', () => {
  it('hides the board filter when there is one board', () => {
    const { rerender } = render(<FloatingBar boards={[{ value: 'b1', label: 'Features' }]} />);
    expect(screen.queryByRole('button', { name: /All boards/ })).toBeNull();
    rerender(
      <FloatingBar
        boards={[
          { value: 'b1', label: 'Features' },
          { value: 'b2', label: 'Bugs' },
        ]}
      />,
    );
    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: /All boards/ })).toBeTruthy();
    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: /Open ideas/ })).toBeTruthy();
  });
});

describe('Composer', () => {
  it('locks while sending and keeps the draft when sending fails', async () => {
    let fail: (e: Error) => void = () => {};
    const onSubmit = vi.fn(() => new Promise<void>((_, reject) => (fail = reject)));
    render(<Composer onSubmit={onSubmit} />);
    const field = screen.getByRole('textbox');
    await userEvent.type(field, 'Same on iOS');
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Send' }));
    expect(onSubmit).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ text: 'Same on iOS', images: [], visibility: 'public' });
    expect(field.hasAttribute('readonly')).toBe(true);
    fail(new Error('You are going too fast. Try again in a minute.'));
    await waitFor(() => expect(screen.getByRole('alert').textContent).toContain('going too fast'));
    expect((field as HTMLTextAreaElement).value).toBe('Same on iOS');
  });

  it('clears after a successful send', async () => {
    render(<Composer onSubmit={async () => {}} />);
    const field = screen.getByRole('textbox') as HTMLTextAreaElement;
    await userEvent.type(field, 'Thanks!');
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Send' }));
    await waitFor(() => expect(field.value).toBe(''));
  });

  it('shows the visibility switch only to admins', () => {
    const { rerender } = render(<Composer />);
    expect(screen.queryByRole('button', { name: /Visibility/ })).toBeNull();
    rerender(<Composer canUseInternal />);
    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: /Visibility: Public/ })).toBeTruthy();
  });
});
```
