- [ ] **Step 5: Fix the composer's previews in the design system**

React's hook rules reject copying derived data into state inside an effect. The thumbnails' object URLs are now derived with `useMemo` and only released in the effect. Replace `design/components/src/Composer.tsx` with:

`design/components/src/Composer.tsx`:

```tsx
import * as React from 'react';
import { Button } from './Button';
import { Icon } from './Icon';
import { cx } from './utils';

export type Visibility = 'public' | 'internal';

export interface ComposerSubmit {
  text: string;
  images: File[];
  visibility: Visibility;
}

export interface ComposerProps {
  placeholder?: string;
  /** Admins can switch a comment to an internal note. */
  canUseInternal?: boolean;
  /** Voice dictation is on in Settings. When false the mic is not rendered at all. */
  voiceEnabled?: boolean;
  /** Image attachments (admins in v1). */
  allowImages?: boolean;
  /** Called while recording is toggled; the app streams audio to /api/transcribe. */
  onDictationChange?: (recording: boolean) => void;
  /**
   * Called with the draft. Return a promise to keep the draft until it resolves: the draft is
   * locked while it sends, clears on success, and on rejection stays with the error's message shown.
   */
  onSubmit?: (value: ComposerSubmit) => void | Promise<void>;
  className?: string;
}

const MAX_IMAGES = 3;
const MAX_BYTES = 5 * 1024 * 1024;

/** The prompt-box comment field on request pages. */
export function Composer({
  placeholder = 'Write a comment…',
  canUseInternal = false,
  voiceEnabled = false,
  allowImages = false,
  onDictationChange,
  onSubmit,
  className,
}: ComposerProps) {
  const [text, setText] = React.useState('');
  const [visibility, setVisibility] = React.useState<Visibility>('public');
  const [images, setImages] = React.useState<File[]>([]);
  const [error, setError] = React.useState('');
  const [recording, setRecording] = React.useState(false);
  const [pending, setPending] = React.useState(false);
  const fileRef = React.useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);
  const fieldId = React.useId();

  // Object URLs for the thumbnails, released when the images change or the composer goes away.
  const previews = React.useMemo(() => images.map((f) => URL.createObjectURL(f)), [images]);
  React.useEffect(() => () => previews.forEach((u) => URL.revokeObjectURL(u)), [previews]);

  const addFiles = (list: FileList | null) => {
    if (!list || pending) return;
    const picked = Array.from(list).filter((f) => f.type.startsWith('image/'));
    const tooBig = picked.find((f) => f.size > MAX_BYTES);
    if (tooBig) setError(`${tooBig.name} is over 5 MB. Try a smaller screenshot.`);
    const ok = picked.filter((f) => f.size <= MAX_BYTES);
    const room = MAX_IMAGES - images.length;
    if (ok.length > room) setError(`You can attach up to ${MAX_IMAGES} images.`);
    setImages([...images, ...ok.slice(0, room)]);
  };

  const submit = async () => {
    if (!text.trim() || pending) return;
    setPending(true);
    setError('');
    try {
      await onSubmit?.({ text: text.trim(), images, visibility });
      setText('');
      setImages([]);
    } catch (err) {
      setError(
        err instanceof Error && err.message ? err.message : "That didn't send. Your text is still here, so try again.",
      );
    } finally {
      setPending(false);
    }
  };

  const internal = visibility === 'internal';

  return (
    <form
      className={cx('lt-composer', internal && 'is-internal', className)}
      onSubmit={(e) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        void submit();
      }}
    >
      <label htmlFor={fieldId} className="lt-sr">
        {internal ? 'Internal note' : 'Comment'}
      </label>
      {previews.length > 0 && (
        <div className="lt-thumbs">
          {previews.map((src, i) => (
            <span key={src} className="lt-thumb">
              <img src={src} alt={images[i]?.name ?? ''} />
              <button
                type="button"
                className="lt-thumb-x"
                aria-label={`Remove ${images[i]?.name ?? 'image'}`}
                disabled={pending}
                onClick={() => setImages(images.filter((_, j) => j !== i))}
              >
                <Icon name="x" size={12} strokeWidth={3} />
              </button>
            </span>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
      <textarea
        id={fieldId}
        className="lt-composer-field"
        rows={2}
        value={text}
        readOnly={pending}
        aria-busy={pending}
        placeholder={recording ? 'Listening…' : internal ? 'Leave a note only the team can see…' : placeholder}
        onChange={(e) => {
          if (!pending) setText(e.target.value);
        }}
        onKeyDown={(e) => {
          if (e.key === 'Enter' && (e.metaKey || e.ctrlKey)) {
            e.preventDefault();
            void submit();
          }
        }}
      />
      {error && (
        <p className="lt-composer-error" role="alert">
          {error}
        </p>
      )}
      <div className="lt-composer-row">
        {allowImages && (
          <>
            <input
              ref={fileRef}
              type="file"
              accept="image/*"
              multiple
              hidden
              onChange={(e) => {
                addFiles(e.target.files);
                e.target.value = '';
              }}
            />
            <Button
              variant="ghost"
              icon="photo"
              iconOnly
              aria-label={`Attach images (up to ${MAX_IMAGES})`}
              disabled={pending || images.length >= MAX_IMAGES}
              onClick={() => fileRef.current?.click()}
            />
          </>
        )}
        {canUseInternal && (
          <button
            type="button"
            className="lt-visibility"
            disabled={pending}
            aria-label={`Visibility: ${internal ? 'Internal' : 'Public'}. Switch to ${internal ? 'public' : 'internal'}`}
            onClick={() => setVisibility(internal ? 'public' : 'internal')}
          >
            <Icon name={internal ? 'lock' : 'globe'} size={15} />
            {internal ? 'Internal' : 'Public'}
          </button>
        )}
        <span className="lt-grow" />
        {voiceEnabled && (
          <Button
            variant="ghost"
            icon="mic"
            iconOnly
            aria-label={recording ? 'Stop dictation' : 'Dictate'}
            aria-pressed={recording}
            className={cx(recording && 'is-recording')}
            disabled={pending}
            onClick={() => {
              setRecording(!recording);
              onDictationChange?.(!recording);
            }}
          />
        )}
        <Button
          variant="primary"
          type="submit"
          icon="arrowUp"
          iconOnly
          aria-label="Send"
          aria-busy={pending}
          disabled={!text.trim() || pending}
        />
      </div>
    </form>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Port the design-system components**

Copy every component in as a client component, then replace the two files that change for the app.

```bash
cd web && mkdir -p components/lort
for f in ../design/components/src/*.ts*; do
  b=$(basename "$f")
  case "$b" in
    index.ts|utils.ts) cp "$f" components/lort/ ;;
    *) { printf "'use client';\n\n"; cat "$f"; } > "components/lort/$b" ;;
  esac
done
```

`web/components/lort/RequestCard.tsx`:

```tsx
'use client';

import * as React from 'react';
import Link from 'next/link';
import { Avatar } from './Avatar';
import { Icon } from './Icon';
import { StatusIcon } from './StatusIcon';
import { VoteSticker } from './VoteSticker';
import { cx, type Status } from './utils';

export interface RequestSummary {
  title: string;
  body: string;
  status: Status;
  board: string;
  author: { name: string; avatarUrl?: string };
  comments: number;
  votes: number;
  voted: boolean;
  /** Hidden from the board until an admin approves it (only its author sees it). */
  awaitingApproval?: boolean;
}

export interface RequestCardProps {
  request: RequestSummary;
  /** Link to the request page, e.g. /feedback/abc123. */
  href: string;
  onVotedChange?: (voted: boolean) => void;
  className?: string;
}

/** One request in the public /feedback list: vote sticker, title, two-line excerpt, meta row. */
export function RequestCard({ request: r, href, onVotedChange, className }: RequestCardProps) {
  return (
    <article className={cx('lt-card', className)}>
      <VoteSticker
        count={r.votes}
        defaultVoted={onVotedChange ? undefined : r.voted}
        voted={onVotedChange ? r.voted : undefined}
        onVotedChange={onVotedChange}
        subject={r.title}
      />
      <div className="lt-card-main">
        <h2 className="lt-card-title">
          <Link href={href} className="lt-card-link">
            {r.title}
          </Link>
        </h2>
        <p className="lt-card-body">{r.body}</p>
        <div className="lt-meta">
          <StatusIcon status={r.status} keyline showLabel size={18} className="lt-meta-status" />
          <span className="lt-meta-pill">{r.board}</span>
          <span className="lt-meta-item">
            <Avatar name={r.author.name} src={r.author.avatarUrl} size={20} />
            {r.author.name}
          </span>
          <span className="lt-meta-item" aria-label={`${r.comments} comments`}>
            <Icon name="message" size={15} />
            {r.comments}
          </span>
          {r.awaitingApproval && <span className="lt-meta-item">Waiting for approval</span>}
        </div>
      </div>
    </article>
  );
}
```

`web/components/lort/FloatingBar.tsx`:

```tsx
'use client';

import * as React from 'react';
import { Button } from './Button';
import { FilterMenu } from './FilterMenu';
import { SortTabs } from './SortTabs';
import { StatusIcon } from './StatusIcon';
import { cx, STATUS, STATUS_ORDER } from './utils';

export interface FloatingBarProps {
  /** Public boards as filter options (value = board id). The Board filter is omitted when there is only one. */
  boards: { value: string; label: string }[];
  sort?: string;
  onSortChange?: (sort: string) => void;
  selectedBoards?: string[];
  onSelectedBoardsChange?: (boards: string[]) => void;
  selectedStatuses?: string[];
  onSelectedStatusesChange?: (statuses: string[]) => void;
  onSuggest?: () => void;
  /** Phone layout: sort tabs, one filter button, a round "+". */
  compact?: boolean;
  onOpenFilters?: () => void;
  className?: string;
}

/** Statuses still in play: the board's default status filter. */
const OPEN = ['pending', 'reviewing', 'planned', 'progress'];

/** The pill bar at the bottom of /feedback: sort, filters and the one primary action. */
export function FloatingBar({
  boards,
  sort,
  onSortChange,
  selectedBoards,
  onSelectedBoardsChange,
  selectedStatuses,
  onSelectedStatusesChange,
  onSuggest,
  compact = false,
  onOpenFilters,
  className,
}: FloatingBarProps) {
  const statusOptions = STATUS_ORDER.map((s) => ({
    value: s,
    label: STATUS[s].label,
    icon: <StatusIcon status={s} />,
  }));
  if (compact) {
    return (
      <div className={cx('lt-bar', 'lt-bar-compact', className)}>
        <SortTabs value={sort} onValueChange={onSortChange} />
        <Button variant="secondary" icon="filter" iconOnly aria-label="Filter" onClick={onOpenFilters} />
        <Button variant="primary" icon="plus" iconOnly aria-label="Suggest something" onClick={onSuggest} />
      </div>
    );
  }
  return (
    <div className={cx('lt-bar', className)}>
      <SortTabs value={sort} onValueChange={onSortChange} />
      {boards.length > 1 && (
        <FilterMenu
          allLabel="All boards"
          noun="boards"
          options={boards}
          selected={selectedBoards}
          onSelectedChange={onSelectedBoardsChange}
        />
      )}
      <FilterMenu
        allLabel="Every status"
        noun="statuses"
        options={statusOptions}
        selected={selectedStatuses}
        defaultSelected={OPEN}
        onSelectedChange={onSelectedStatusesChange}
        summarize={(v) => (v.length === OPEN.length && OPEN.every((s) => v.includes(s)) ? 'Open ideas' : undefined)}
      />
      <Button variant="primary" icon="plus" onClick={onSuggest}>
        Suggest something
      </Button>
    </div>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 7: Add the tokens and styles**

`tokens.css` follows the visitor's system theme (dark by default) and lets `data-theme` force one; `lort.css` is `design/components/lort.css` without its font import and preview-frame rules; `app.css` holds the app's own classes, matched to `design/canvas`; `globals.css` maps shadcn's colour names onto Lort's tokens, since both use `--primary`, `--border` and `--muted` (Lort's `--muted` is a text colour).

`web/app/tokens.css`:

```css
/* Lort design tokens (DESIGN.md). Dark is the default; the visitor's system setting picks light,
   and data-theme="dark" or data-theme="light" on any element forces a theme inside it. */
:root,
[data-theme='dark'] {
  color-scheme: dark;
  --ground: #1a1716;
  --ground-sunken: #151211;
  --surface: #24201e;
  --surface-2: #2c2725;
  --surface-3: #352f2c;
  --border: #352f2c;
  --border-strong: #423a36;
  --dot-empty: #7a6e67;
  --ink: #f5efea;
  --ink-2: #d8cdc5;
  --muted: #b3a79e;
  --primary: #cc3a6d;
  --on-primary: #ffffff;
  --primary-text: #f08aae;
  --primary-soft: #3a1e29;
  --keyline: #f5efea;
  --focus: #f5efea;
  --danger-text: #ff8a7a;
  --note-bg: #2a2418;
  --note-border: #6b5a33;
  --note-ink: #e8c77e;
  --status-pending: #eab308;
  --status-reviewing: #f97316;
  --status-planned: #38bdf8;
  --status-progress: #a855f7;
  --status-completed: #22c55e;
  --status-closed: #ef4444;
  --status-pending-text: var(--status-pending);
  --status-reviewing-text: var(--status-reviewing);
  --status-planned-text: var(--status-planned);
  --status-progress-text: #c084fc;
  --status-completed-text: #4ade80;
  --status-closed-text: #f87171;
  --shadow-sticker: 0 3px 8px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.45);
  --shadow-float: 0 14px 36px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.5);
  --shadow-card-float: 0 8px 20px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.35);
  --shadow-inset-sm: inset 0 1.7px 2.55px -0.85px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);
}

@media (prefers-color-scheme: light) {
  :root:not([data-theme='dark']) {
    color-scheme: light;
    --ground: #f3f1ef;
    --ground-sunken: #ebe7e4;
    --surface: #ffffff;
    --surface-2: #efeae6;
    --surface-3: #e4ddd8;
    --border: #e3ddd8;
    --border-strong: #cfc6bf;
    --dot-empty: #8c817a;
    --ink: #1e1a18;
    --ink-2: #3d3532;
    --muted: #655b55;
    --primary: #cc3a6d;
    --on-primary: #ffffff;
    --primary-text: #b02a5a;
    --primary-soft: #f9e3eb;
    --keyline: #ffffff;
    --focus: #1e1a18;
    --danger-text: #b42318;
    --note-bg: #fbf3df;
    --note-border: #c9a961;
    --note-ink: #7a5a12;
    --status-pending: #b27703;
    --status-reviewing: #e2520b;
    --status-planned: #0284c7;
    --status-progress: #9333ea;
    --status-completed: #15913f;
    --status-closed: #dc2626;
    --status-pending-text: #8f5606;
    --status-reviewing-text: #b23a0a;
    --status-planned-text: #0369a1;
    --status-progress-text: #7e22ce;
    --status-completed-text: #13733a;
    --status-closed-text: #b91c1c;
    --shadow-sticker: 0 2px 6px rgba(60, 30, 20, 0.28);
    --shadow-float: 0 14px 36px rgba(60, 40, 30, 0.18);
    --shadow-card-float: 0 8px 20px rgba(60, 40, 30, 0.12);
    --shadow-inset-sm: inset 0 1.7px 2.55px -0.85px rgba(60, 30, 20, 0.2);
  }
}

[data-theme='light'] {
  color-scheme: light;
  --ground: #f3f1ef;
  --ground-sunken: #ebe7e4;
  --surface: #ffffff;
  --surface-2: #efeae6;
  --surface-3: #e4ddd8;
  --border: #e3ddd8;
  --border-strong: #cfc6bf;
  --dot-empty: #8c817a;
  --ink: #1e1a18;
  --ink-2: #3d3532;
  --muted: #655b55;
  --primary: #cc3a6d;
  --on-primary: #ffffff;
  --primary-text: #b02a5a;
  --primary-soft: #f9e3eb;
  --keyline: #ffffff;
  --focus: #1e1a18;
  --danger-text: #b42318;
  --note-bg: #fbf3df;
  --note-border: #c9a961;
  --note-ink: #7a5a12;
  --status-pending: #b27703;
  --status-reviewing: #e2520b;
  --status-planned: #0284c7;
  --status-progress: #9333ea;
  --status-completed: #15913f;
  --status-closed: #dc2626;
  --status-pending-text: #8f5606;
  --status-reviewing-text: #b23a0a;
  --status-planned-text: #0369a1;
  --status-progress-text: #7e22ce;
  --status-completed-text: #13733a;
  --status-closed-text: #b91c1c;
  --shadow-sticker: 0 2px 6px rgba(60, 30, 20, 0.28);
  --shadow-float: 0 14px 36px rgba(60, 40, 30, 0.18);
  --shadow-card-float: 0 8px 20px rgba(60, 40, 30, 0.12);
  --shadow-inset-sm: inset 0 1.7px 2.55px -0.85px rgba(60, 30, 20, 0.2);
}

:root {
  --space-1: 4px;
  --space-2: 8px;
  --space-3: 12px;
  --space-4: 16px;
  --space-5: 20px;
  --space-6: 24px;
  --space-8: 32px;
  --space-10: 40px;
  --space-14: 56px;
  --radius-sm: 10px;
  --radius-md: 16px;
  --radius-lg: 18px;
  --radius-xl: 22px;
  --radius-pill: 999px;
  --radius-full: 50%;
  --tilt-sticker: -7deg;
  --tilt-mark: -4deg;
}

.display-xl {
  font-family: var(--font-display);
  font-size: 44px;
  line-height: 46px;
  font-weight: 800;
  letter-spacing: -0.03em;
}
.display-l {
  font-family: var(--font-display);
  font-size: 34px;
  line-height: 38px;
  font-weight: 800;
  letter-spacing: -0.025em;
}
.title {
  font-family: var(--font-display);
  font-size: 20px;
  line-height: 25px;
  font-weight: 700;
  letter-spacing: -0.01em;
}
.count {
  font-family: var(--font-display);
  font-size: 17px;
  line-height: 17px;
  font-weight: 800;
}
.body-lg {
  font-family: var(--font-sans);
  font-size: 17px;
  line-height: 26px;
  font-weight: 400;
}
.body {
  font-family: var(--font-sans);
  font-size: 15px;
  line-height: 23px;
  font-weight: 400;
}
.label {
  font-family: var(--font-sans);
  font-size: 14px;
  line-height: 20px;
  font-weight: 600;
}
.caption {
  font-family: var(--font-sans);
  font-size: 13px;
  line-height: 18px;
  font-weight: 500;
}
.micro {
  font-family: var(--font-sans);
  font-size: 11px;
  line-height: 14px;
  font-weight: 700;
}
```

`web/app/lort.css`:

```css
/* Lort components, ported from design/components/lort.css. Every value comes from tokens.css. */

/* ---------- Helpers ---------- */
.lt-sr {
  position: absolute;
  width: 1px;
  height: 1px;
  overflow: hidden;
  clip: rect(0 0 0 0);
  white-space: nowrap;
}
.lt-grow {
  flex: 1;
}

/* ---------- Shadows (utility classes, one per shadow token) ---------- */
.lt-shadow-sticker {
  box-shadow: var(--shadow-sticker);
}
.lt-shadow-float {
  box-shadow: var(--shadow-float);
}
.lt-shadow-card-float {
  box-shadow: var(--shadow-card-float);
}
.lt-shadow-inset-sm {
  box-shadow: var(--shadow-inset-sm);
}

/* ---------- Icon ---------- */
.lt-icon {
  flex: none;
  display: block;
}

/* ---------- StatusIcon ---------- */
.lt-status {
  display: inline-flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 7px;
  color: var(--ink);
  font-weight: 600;
}
.lt-status-icon {
  flex: none;
  display: block;
}
.lt-si-keyline {
  fill: var(--keyline);
}
.lt-si-ground {
  fill: var(--lt-si-ground, var(--surface));
}
.lt-si-tint {
  fill: currentColor;
  opacity: 0.3;
}
.lt-si-ring {
  fill: none;
  stroke: currentColor;
}
.lt-si-fill {
  fill: currentColor;
}
.lt-si-glyph {
  fill: none;
  stroke: var(--lt-si-ground, var(--surface));
  stroke-width: 1.8;
  stroke-linecap: round;
  stroke-linejoin: round;
}
.lt-status-text {
  font-weight: 600;
}

/* ---------- VoteSticker ---------- */
.lt-vote {
  all: unset;
  box-sizing: border-box;
  flex: none;
  width: 58px;
  height: 58px;
  border-radius: var(--radius-full);
  display: inline-flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: center;
  cursor: pointer;
  font-family: var(--font-display);
  font-weight: 800;
  font-size: 17px;
  line-height: 1;
  font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;
  color: var(--ink);
  border: 2px dashed var(--dot-empty);
  transition: border-color 0.15s ease-out;
}
.lt-vote:hover {
  border-color: var(--muted);
}
.lt-vote:focus-visible {
  outline: 3px solid var(--focus);
  outline-offset: 3px;
}
.lt-vote.is-voted {
  border: 3px solid var(--keyline);
  background: var(--primary);
  color: var(--on-primary);
  transform: rotate(var(--tilt-sticker));
  box-shadow: var(--shadow-sticker);
}
.lt-vote-sm {
  width: 40px;
  height: 40px;
  font-size: 14px;
}
.lt-vote.is-static {
  cursor: default;
}
.lt-vote.is-static:hover {
  border-color: var(--dot-empty);
}
.lt-vote.is-slap {
  animation: lt-slap 0.22s cubic-bezier(0.34, 1.56, 0.64, 1);
}
.lt-vote.is-peel {
  animation: lt-peel 0.16s ease-out;
}
@keyframes lt-slap {
  0% {
    transform: scale(1.15) rotate(0deg);
    box-shadow: 0 12px 20px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.25);
  }
  100% {
    transform: scale(1) rotate(var(--tilt-sticker));
    box-shadow: var(--shadow-sticker);
  }
}
@keyframes lt-peel {
  0% {
    transform: rotate(var(--tilt-sticker));
  }
  60% {
    transform: rotate(-14deg) scale(1.06);
  }
  100% {
    transform: none;
  }
}

/* ---------- Avatar ---------- */
.lt-avatar {
  flex: none;
  display: inline-grid;
  place-items: center;
  border-radius: var(--radius-full);
  overflow: hidden;
  color: #1a1716;
  font-weight: 800;
  line-height: 1;
  box-sizing: border-box;
}
.lt-avatar img {
  width: 100%;
  height: 100%;
  object-fit: cover;
  display: block;
}
.lt-avatar.has-keyline {
  border: 2px solid var(--keyline);
}
.lt-avatar.is-team {
  box-shadow:
    0 0 0 2px var(--ground),
    0 0 0 4px var(--primary);
}

/* ---------- Button ---------- */
.lt-btn {
  all: unset;
  box-sizing: border-box;
  display: inline-flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: center;
  gap: 8px;
  height: 42px;
  padding: 0 18px;
  border-radius: var(--radius-pill);
  cursor: pointer;
  white-space: nowrap;
  font-family: var(--font-sans);
  font-size: 14px;
  line-height: 20px;
  font-weight: 600;
  color: var(--ink);
  transition:
    background-color 0.15s ease-out,
    color 0.15s ease-out;
}
.lt-btn:focus-visible {
  outline: 3px solid var(--focus);
  outline-offset: 3px;
}
.lt-btn:disabled {
  opacity: 0.5;
  cursor: not-allowed;
}
.lt-btn-primary {
  background: var(--primary);
  color: var(--on-primary);
  font-weight: 700;
}
.lt-btn-secondary {
  background: var(--surface-3);
}
.lt-btn-ghost {
  color: var(--muted);
}
.lt-btn-ghost:hover:not(:disabled),
.lt-btn-neutral:hover:not(:disabled) {
  background: var(--surface-3);
  color: var(--ink);
}
.lt-btn-neutral {
  color: var(--ink);
}
.lt-btn-danger {
  color: var(--danger-text);
}
.lt-btn-danger:hover:not(:disabled) {
  background: var(--surface-3);
}
.lt-btn-sm {
  height: 32px;
  padding: 0 12px;
  font-size: 13px;
  gap: 6px;
}
.lt-btn-icon {
  width: 42px;
  padding: 0;
}
.lt-btn-sm.lt-btn-icon {
  width: 32px;
}
.lt-btn.is-recording {
  color: var(--primary-text);
  background: var(--primary-soft);
}

/* ---------- Chip ---------- */
.lt-chip {
  all: unset;
  box-sizing: border-box;
  display: inline-flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 7px;
  height: 34px;
  padding: 0 14px;
  border-radius: var(--radius-pill);
  background: var(--surface-2);
  color: var(--ink);
  cursor: pointer;
  font-family: var(--font-sans);
  font-size: 13px;
  line-height: 18px;
  font-weight: 600;
  --lt-si-ground: var(--surface-2);
}
span.lt-chip {
  cursor: default;
}
.lt-chip:focus-visible {
  outline: 3px solid var(--focus);
  outline-offset: 3px;
}
button.lt-chip:hover {
  background: var(--surface-3);
  --lt-si-ground: var(--surface-3);
}
.lt-chip-tag {
  color: var(--ink-2);
}
.lt-chip-square {
  width: 34px;
  padding: 0;
  justify-content: center;
}
.lt-switch {
  position: relative;
  width: 30px;
  height: 18px;
  border-radius: 9px;
  background: var(--dot-empty);
  transition: background-color 0.15s ease-out;
}
.lt-switch::after {
  content: '';
  position: absolute;
  left: 2px;
  top: 2px;
  width: 14px;
  height: 14px;
  border-radius: var(--radius-full);
  background: var(--keyline);
  transition: transform 0.15s ease-out;
}
.lt-switch.is-on {
  background: var(--primary);
}
.lt-switch.is-on::after {
  transform: translateX(12px);
  background: var(--on-primary);
}

/* ---------- SortTabs ---------- */
.lt-tabs {
  position: relative;
  display: inline-flex;
  padding: 4px;
  border-radius: var(--radius-pill);
  background: var(--ground);
  border: 1px solid var(--border);
  box-shadow: var(--shadow-inset-sm);
}
.lt-tabs-ind {
  position: absolute;
  top: 4px;
  bottom: 4px;
  left: 0;
  border-radius: var(--radius-pill);
  background: var(--ink);
  transition:
    transform 0.25s cubic-bezier(0.2, 0.8, 0.2, 1),
    width 0.25s cubic-bezier(0.2, 0.8, 0.2, 1);
}
.lt-tab {
  all: unset;
  position: relative;
  z-index: 1;
  display: inline-flex;
  align-items: center;
  height: 36px;
  padding: 0 18px;
  border-radius: var(--radius-pill);
  cursor: pointer;
  font-family: var(--font-sans);
  font-size: 14px;
  font-weight: 600;
  color: var(--muted);
  transition: color 0.2s ease-out;
}
.lt-tab[aria-selected='true'] {
  color: var(--ground);
}
.lt-tab:focus-visible {
  outline: 3px solid var(--focus);
  outline-offset: 3px;
}

/* ---------- FilterMenu ---------- */
.lt-filter {
  position: relative;
  display: inline-flex;
}
.lt-menu {
  position: absolute;
  left: 0;
  z-index: 30;
  min-width: 220px;
  padding: 6px;
  border-radius: var(--radius-md);
  background: var(--surface);
  border: 1px solid var(--border-strong);
  box-shadow: var(--shadow-float);
  --lt-si-ground: var(--surface);
}
.lt-menu-up {
  bottom: calc(100% + 10px);
}
.lt-menu-down {
  top: calc(100% + 10px);
}
.lt-menu-item {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 10px;
  height: 40px;
  padding: 0 10px;
  border-radius: 12px;
  font-size: 14px;
  font-weight: 500;
  color: var(--ink);
  cursor: pointer;
}
.lt-menu-item:hover {
  background: var(--surface-2);
  --lt-si-ground: var(--surface-2);
}
.lt-menu-item input {
  position: absolute;
  opacity: 0;
  pointer-events: none;
}
.lt-check {
  flex: none;
  width: 18px;
  height: 18px;
  border-radius: 6px;
  border: 2px solid var(--dot-empty);
  box-sizing: border-box;
  display: grid;
  place-items: center;
  color: transparent;
}
.lt-menu-item input:checked + .lt-check {
  background: var(--primary);
  border-color: var(--primary);
  color: var(--on-primary);
}
.lt-menu-item input:focus-visible + .lt-check {
  outline: 3px solid var(--focus);
  outline-offset: 2px;
}
.lt-menu-foot {
  border-top: 1px solid var(--border);
  margin-top: 6px;
  padding-top: 6px;
}
.lt-menu-clear {
  all: unset;
  box-sizing: border-box;
  width: 100%;
  height: 36px;
  padding: 0 10px;
  border-radius: 12px;
  font-size: 13px;
  font-weight: 600;
  color: var(--muted);
  cursor: pointer;
}
.lt-menu-clear:hover:not(:disabled) {
  background: var(--surface-2);
  color: var(--ink);
}
.lt-menu-clear:disabled {
  opacity: 0.5;
  cursor: not-allowed;
}
.lt-menu-clear:focus-visible {
  outline: 3px solid var(--focus);
  outline-offset: 2px;
}

/* ---------- FloatingBar & TriageBar ---------- */
.lt-bar {
  display: inline-flex;
  flex-wrap: wrap;
  align-items: center;
  gap: var(--space-2);
  padding: 8px;
  background: var(--surface-2);
  border: 1px solid var(--border-strong);
  border-radius: var(--radius-pill);
  box-shadow: var(--shadow-inset-sm), var(--shadow-float);
}
.lt-bar-compact .lt-btn-primary.lt-btn-icon {
  width: 48px;
  height: 48px;
}
.lt-triage {
  gap: 4px;
  padding: 6px;
}
.lt-triage .lt-btn-primary {
  margin-left: 4px;
}

/* ---------- RequestCard ---------- */
.lt-card {
  position: relative;
  display: flex;
  gap: 18px;
  align-items: flex-start;
  padding: var(--space-5) 22px;
  background: var(--surface);
  border: 1px solid var(--border);
  border-radius: var(--radius-lg);
  transition: background-color 0.15s ease-out;
  --lt-si-ground: var(--surface);
}
.lt-card:hover {
  background: var(--surface-2);
  --lt-si-ground: var(--surface-2);
}
.lt-card .lt-vote {
  position: relative;
  z-index: 1;
}
.lt-card-main {
  flex: 1;
  min-width: 0;
}
.lt-card-title {
  margin: 2px 0 0;
  font-family: var(--font-display);
  font-size: 20px;
  line-height: 25px;
  font-weight: 700;
  letter-spacing: -0.01em;
  text-wrap: balance;
}
.lt-card-link {
  color: var(--ink);
  text-decoration: none;
}
.lt-card-link::after {
  content: '';
  position: absolute;
  inset: 0;
  border-radius: inherit;
}
.lt-card-link:focus-visible {
  outline: none;
}
.lt-card:has(.lt-card-link:focus-visible) {
  outline: 3px solid var(--focus);
  outline-offset: 3px;
}
.lt-card-body {
  margin: 6px 0 0;
  font-size: 15px;
  line-height: 23px;
  color: var(--ink-2);
  display: -webkit-box;
  -webkit-line-clamp: 2;
  -webkit-box-orient: vertical;
  overflow: hidden;
}
.lt-meta {
  display: flex;
  flex-wrap: wrap;
  align-items: center;
  gap: var(--space-3) 14px;
  margin-top: var(--space-3);
  font-size: 13px;
  line-height: 18px;
  font-weight: 500;
  color: var(--muted);
}
.lt-meta-item {
  display: inline-flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 6px;
}
.lt-meta-pill {
  background: var(--surface-2);
  border-radius: var(--radius-pill);
  padding: 3px 10px;
}
.lt-card:hover .lt-meta-pill {
  background: var(--surface-3);
}

/* ---------- Composer ---------- */
.lt-composer {
  background: var(--surface);
  border: 1px solid var(--border);
  border-radius: var(--radius-xl);
  padding: 16px 16px 10px;
  transition:
    border-color 0.15s ease-out,
    background-color 0.15s ease-out;
}
.lt-composer:focus-within {
  border-color: var(--border-strong);
}
.lt-composer.is-internal {
  background: var(--note-bg);
  border: 2px dashed var(--note-border);
}
.lt-composer-field {
  all: unset;
  display: block;
  box-sizing: border-box;
  width: 100%;
  min-height: 52px;
  field-sizing: content;
  max-height: 240px;
  overflow: auto;
  font-family: var(--font-sans);
  font-size: 15px;
  line-height: 23px;
  color: var(--ink);
  white-space: pre-wrap;
}
.lt-composer-field::placeholder {
  color: var(--muted);
}
.lt-composer-error {
  margin: 6px 0 0;
  font-size: 13px;
  line-height: 18px;
  color: var(--danger-text);
}
.lt-composer-row {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 6px;
  margin-top: 6px;
}
.lt-composer-row .lt-btn-icon {
  width: 40px;
  height: 40px;
}
.lt-visibility {
  all: unset;
  box-sizing: border-box;
  display: inline-flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 6px;
  height: 32px;
  padding: 0 12px;
  border-radius: var(--radius-pill);
  background: transparent;
  color: var(--ink-2);
  font-size: 13px;
  font-weight: 600;
  cursor: pointer;
  transition:
    background-color 0.18s ease-out,
    color 0.18s ease-out;
}
.lt-visibility .lt-icon {
  transition: transform 0.22s cubic-bezier(0.34, 1.56, 0.64, 1);
}
.lt-visibility:hover {
  background: var(--surface-2);
  color: var(--ink);
}
.lt-visibility:focus-visible {
  outline: 3px solid var(--focus);
  outline-offset: 3px;
}
.lt-composer.is-internal .lt-visibility {
  color: var(--note-ink);
}
.lt-composer.is-internal .lt-visibility:hover {
  background: color-mix(in srgb, var(--note-border) 24%, transparent);
  color: var(--note-ink);
}
.lt-composer.is-internal .lt-visibility:hover .lt-icon {
  transform: rotate(-12deg) translateY(-1px);
}
.lt-thumbs {
  display: flex;
  gap: 8px;
  margin-bottom: 10px;
}
.lt-thumb {
  position: relative;
  width: 64px;
  height: 64px;
  border-radius: var(--radius-sm);
  overflow: hidden;
  background: var(--surface-2);
}
.lt-thumb img {
  width: 100%;
  height: 100%;
  object-fit: cover;
  display: block;
}
.lt-thumb-x {
  all: unset;
  position: absolute;
  top: 4px;
  right: 4px;
  width: 20px;
  height: 20px;
  border-radius: var(--radius-full);
  display: grid;
  place-items: center;
  background: var(--ink);
  color: var(--ground);
  cursor: pointer;
}
.lt-thumb-x:focus-visible {
  outline: 3px solid var(--focus);
  outline-offset: 2px;
}

/* ---------- InternalNote ---------- */
.lt-note {
  background: var(--note-bg);
  border: 2px dashed var(--note-border);
  border-radius: var(--radius-lg);
  padding: 14px 18px;
}
.lt-note-head {
  display: flex;
  flex-wrap: wrap;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 8px;
  font-size: 13px;
  line-height: 18px;
  color: var(--ink);
}
.lt-note-label {
  display: inline-flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 4px;
  color: var(--note-ink);
  font-weight: 600;
}
.lt-note-time {
  color: var(--muted);
}
.lt-note-body {
  margin-top: 6px;
  font-size: 15px;
  line-height: 23px;
  color: var(--ink-2);
}

/* ---------- Toast ---------- */
.lt-toast {
  position: relative;
  display: inline-flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 12px;
  min-height: 48px;
  padding: 8px 8px 8px 18px;
  overflow: hidden;
  border-radius: var(--radius-md);
  background: var(--surface-2);
  border: 1px solid var(--border-strong);
  box-shadow: var(--shadow-inset-sm), var(--shadow-float);
  font-size: 14px;
  line-height: 20px;
  font-weight: 500;
  color: var(--ink);
  box-sizing: border-box;
}
.lt-toast-action {
  all: unset;
  height: 32px;
  padding: 0 12px;
  border-radius: var(--radius-pill);
  font-weight: 700;
  color: var(--primary-text);
  cursor: pointer;
}
.lt-toast-action:hover {
  background: var(--surface-3);
}
.lt-toast-x {
  all: unset;
  width: 32px;
  height: 32px;
  border-radius: var(--radius-pill);
  display: grid;
  place-items: center;
  color: var(--muted);
  cursor: pointer;
}
.lt-toast-x:hover {
  background: var(--surface-3);
  color: var(--ink);
}
.lt-toast-action:focus-visible,
.lt-toast-x:focus-visible {
  outline: 3px solid var(--focus);
  outline-offset: 2px;
}
.lt-toast-timer {
  position: absolute;
  left: 0;
  bottom: 0;
  height: 3px;
  width: 100%;
  background: var(--primary);
  transform-origin: left;
  animation-name: lt-countdown;
  animation-timing-function: linear;
  animation-fill-mode: forwards;
}
@keyframes lt-countdown {
  from {
    transform: scaleX(1);
  }
  to {
    transform: scaleX(0);
  }
}

/* ---------- Reduced motion ---------- */
@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) {
  .lt-vote.is-slap,
  .lt-vote.is-peel {
    animation: none;
  }
  .lt-tabs-ind,
  .lt-tab,
  .lt-btn,
  .lt-switch,
  .lt-switch::after,
  .lt-card,
  .lt-composer,
  .lt-visibility,
  .lt-visibility .lt-icon {
    transition: none;
  }
  .lt-composer.is-internal .lt-visibility:hover .lt-icon {
    transform: none;
  }
}
```

`web/app/app.css`:

```css
/* App-level styles shared by the board and the dashboard, matched to design/canvas. */

/* ---------- Dialogs (shadcn Dialog and Sheet) ---------- */
[data-slot='dialog-overlay'],
[data-slot='sheet-overlay'] {
  background: rgba(12, 10, 9, 0.62);
  backdrop-filter: blur(6px);
  -webkit-backdrop-filter: blur(6px);
}
.lt-dialog {
  background: var(--surface);
  border: 1px solid var(--border-strong);
  border-radius: var(--radius-xl);
  box-shadow: var(--shadow-float);
  padding: 24px 26px 22px;
  color: var(--ink);
}

/* ---------- Form fields ---------- */
.lt-label {
  display: block;
  font-size: 13px;
  font-weight: 600;
  color: var(--ink-2);
  margin-bottom: 8px;
}
.lt-label-note {
  font-weight: 500;
  color: var(--muted);
}
.lt-field {
  all: unset;
  box-sizing: border-box;
  display: block;
  width: 100%;
  height: 46px;
  padding: 0 18px;
  border-radius: var(--radius-pill);
  background: var(--ground);
  border: 1px solid var(--border);
  font-family: var(--font-sans);
  font-size: 15px;
  color: var(--ink);
}
.lt-field::placeholder {
  color: var(--muted);
}
.lt-field:focus-visible {
  outline: 3px solid var(--focus);
  outline-offset: 3px;
}
.lt-field[aria-invalid='true'] {
  border-color: var(--danger-text);
}
.lt-field-title {
  height: auto;
  padding: 12px 16px;
  border-radius: 14px;
  font-family: var(--font-display);
  font-size: 18px;
  font-weight: 700;
}
.lt-hint {
  font-size: 12px;
  color: var(--muted);
  margin: 6px 0 0 18px;
}
.lt-error {
  font-size: 13px;
  color: var(--danger-text);
  margin: 6px 0 0 4px;
}
.lt-honeypot {
  position: absolute;
  left: -9999px;
  width: 1px;
  height: 1px;
  overflow: hidden;
}
.lt-link {
  color: var(--primary-text);
  font-weight: 600;
}
.lt-link:hover {
  text-decoration: underline;
  text-underline-offset: 3px;
}

/* ---------- Pills used as toggles (board picker, tags, label filters) ---------- */
.lt-segment {
  display: inline-flex;
  flex-wrap: wrap;
  padding: 4px;
  gap: 2px;
  border-radius: 999px;
  background: var(--ground);
  border: 1px solid var(--border);
  box-shadow: var(--shadow-inset-sm);
}
.lt-segment-item {
  all: unset;
  cursor: pointer;
  height: 34px;
  padding: 0 16px;
  border-radius: 999px;
  font-size: 14px;
  font-weight: 600;
  color: var(--muted);
  display: inline-flex;
  align-items: center;
}
.lt-segment-item[aria-checked='true'] {
  background: var(--ink);
  color: var(--ground);
}
.lt-segment-item:focus-visible {
  outline: 3px solid var(--focus);
  outline-offset: 2px;
}
.lt-toggle-tag {
  all: unset;
  box-sizing: border-box;
  display: inline-flex;
  align-items: center;
  height: 32px;
  padding: 0 13px;
  border-radius: 999px;
  background: var(--surface-2);
  font-size: 13px;
  font-weight: 600;
  color: var(--ink-2);
  cursor: pointer;
}
.lt-toggle-tag[aria-pressed='true'] {
  background: var(--primary-soft);
  color: var(--primary-text);
  box-shadow: inset 0 0 0 1.5px var(--primary-text);
}
.lt-toggle-tag:focus-visible {
  outline: 3px solid var(--focus);
  outline-offset: 3px;
}

/* ---------- Board top bar ---------- */
.lt-topbar {
  max-width: 1120px;
  margin: 0 auto;
  padding: 0 32px;
  min-height: 76px;
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 20px;
}
.lt-brand {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 10px;
  font-family: var(--font-display);
  font-weight: 800;
  font-size: 19px;
}
.lt-mark {
  width: 32px;
  height: 32px;
  flex: none;
  border-radius: var(--radius-sm);
  background: var(--primary);
  color: var(--on-primary);
  display: grid;
  place-items: center;
  font-size: 16px;
  transform: rotate(var(--tilt-mark));
  box-shadow: var(--shadow-sticker);
  overflow: hidden;
}
.lt-mark img {
  width: 100%;
  height: 100%;
  object-fit: cover;
}
.lt-nav {
  display: flex;
  gap: 4px;
  margin-left: 10px;
}
.lt-nav-pill {
  font-size: 15px;
  font-weight: 600;
  padding: 8px 14px;
  border-radius: var(--radius-pill);
  color: var(--muted);
}
.lt-nav-pill:hover {
  color: var(--ink);
}
.lt-nav-pill[aria-current='page'] {
  background: var(--surface-2);
  color: var(--ink);
}
.lt-search {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 8px;
  height: 40px;
  padding: 0 16px;
  width: 240px;
  box-sizing: border-box;
  border-radius: var(--radius-pill);
  background: var(--surface);
  border: 1px solid var(--border);
  color: var(--muted);
}
.lt-search:focus-within {
  outline: 3px solid var(--focus);
  outline-offset: 3px;
}
.lt-search input {
  all: unset;
  flex: 1;
  min-width: 0;
  font-size: 14px;
  color: var(--ink);
}
.lt-search input::placeholder {
  color: var(--muted);
}
.lt-avatar-button {
  all: unset;
  cursor: pointer;
  border-radius: 50%;
  display: inline-flex;
}
.lt-avatar-button:focus-visible {
  outline: 3px solid var(--focus);
  outline-offset: 3px;
}
@media (max-width: 640px) {
  .lt-topbar {
    padding: 12px 16px 6px;
    gap: 10px;
    flex-wrap: wrap;
    min-height: 0;
  }
  .lt-topbar .lt-nav {
    order: 3;
    width: 100%;
    margin-left: -4px;
  }
  .lt-topbar .lt-search {
    display: none;
  }
  .lt-topbar.is-searching .lt-search {
    display: flex;
    order: 4;
    width: 100%;
  }
}
@media (min-width: 641px) {
  .lt-search-toggle {
    display: none !important;
  }
}

/* ---------- Page bodies ---------- */
.lt-page {
  max-width: 860px;
  margin: 0 auto;
  padding: 36px 32px 160px;
  box-sizing: border-box;
}
.lt-page-narrow {
  max-width: 820px;
}
@media (max-width: 640px) {
  .lt-page {
    padding: 14px 16px 140px;
  }
}
.lt-footer {
  margin-top: 56px;
  display: flex;
  justify-content: center;
}
.lt-footer a {
  font-size: 13px;
  color: var(--muted);
}
.lt-footer b {
  color: var(--ink);
}
.lt-floating {
  position: fixed;
  z-index: 20;
  left: 0;
  right: 0;
  bottom: 28px;
  display: flex;
  justify-content: center;
  padding: 0 16px;
  pointer-events: none;
}
.lt-floating > * {
  pointer-events: auto;
}
@media (max-width: 640px) {
  .lt-floating {
    bottom: 20px;
  }
}

/* ---------- Skeletons: Raised Surface bones that pulse; still under reduced motion ---------- */
.sk {
  background: var(--surface-2);
  border-radius: 999px;
  animation: sk-pulse 1.6s ease-in-out infinite;
}
@keyframes sk-pulse {
  0%,
  100% {
    opacity: 1;
  }
  50% {
    opacity: 0.55;
  }
}
@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) {
  .sk {
    animation: none;
  }
}
.sk-card {
  display: flex;
  gap: 18px;
  align-items: flex-start;
  padding: 20px 22px;
  background: var(--surface);
  border: 1px solid var(--border);
  border-radius: var(--radius-lg);
}

/* ---------- Realtime: new requests arrive with a primary tint that fades ---------- */
.lt-card.is-new {
  animation: lt-new 4s ease-out;
}
@keyframes lt-new {
  from {
    background: var(--primary-soft);
  }
  to {
    background: var(--surface);
  }
}
@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) {
  .lt-card.is-new {
    animation: none;
  }
}

/* ---------- Request page ---------- */
.lt-back {
  display: inline-flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 6px;
  font-size: 14px;
  font-weight: 600;
  color: var(--muted);
  margin-bottom: 22px;
}
.lt-back:hover {
  color: var(--ink);
}
.lt-prose {
  white-space: pre-wrap;
  overflow-wrap: anywhere;
}
.lt-images {
  display: flex;
  gap: 10px;
  flex-wrap: wrap;
  margin-top: 16px;
}
.lt-images a {
  display: block;
  width: 120px;
  height: 90px;
  border-radius: var(--radius-sm);
  overflow: hidden;
  border: 1px solid var(--border);
}
.lt-images img {
  width: 100%;
  height: 100%;
  object-fit: cover;
}
.lt-team-badge {
  font-size: 11px;
  font-weight: 700;
  color: var(--on-primary);
  background: var(--primary);
  border-radius: var(--radius-pill);
  padding: 2px 8px;
}
.lt-reply {
  margin: 12px 0 0 34px;
  padding: 14px 16px;
  background: var(--surface);
  border-radius: var(--radius-md);
}
.lt-text-button {
  all: unset;
  cursor: pointer;
  font-size: 13px;
  font-weight: 600;
  color: var(--muted);
}
.lt-text-button:hover {
  color: var(--ink);
}
.lt-text-button:focus-visible {
  outline: 3px solid var(--focus);
  outline-offset: 2px;
  border-radius: 4px;
}
.lt-banner {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 10px;
  padding: 12px 16px;
  border-radius: var(--radius-md);
  background: var(--surface-2);
  font-size: 14px;
}

/* ---------- Empty states ---------- */
.lt-empty {
  text-align: center;
  padding: 56px 16px;
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 12px;
}
.lt-empty-dot {
  width: 58px;
  height: 58px;
  border-radius: 50%;
  border: 2px dashed var(--dot-empty);
}

/* The composer in the Suggest dialog sits a step above the dialog surface. */
.sg-compose .lt-composer:not(.is-internal) {
  background: var(--surface-2);
  border-color: var(--border-strong);
  --lt-si-ground: var(--surface-2);
}
.sg-compose .lt-composer:not(.is-internal) .lt-visibility:hover,
.sg-compose .lt-composer:not(.is-internal) .lt-btn-ghost:hover:not(:disabled) {
  background: var(--surface-3);
}

@media (max-width: 640px) {
  .display-xl {
    font-size: 32px;
    line-height: 34px;
  }
}

/* ---------- Dashboard shell (design/canvas/D-*.dc.html) ---------- */
.dash {
  min-height: 100vh;
  display: flex;
  background: var(--ground);
}
.dash-side {
  width: 244px;
  flex: none;
  padding: 16px 12px;
  box-sizing: border-box;
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  gap: 4px;
  background: var(--ground-sunken);
  border-right: 1px solid var(--surface-2);
  position: sticky;
  top: 0;
  height: 100vh;
}
.dash-side.is-rail {
  width: 76px;
  align-items: center;
}
.dash-brand {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 10px;
  padding: 4px 8px 18px;
}
.dash-brand-name {
  font-family: var(--font-display);
  font-weight: 800;
  font-size: 17px;
  flex: 1;
  min-width: 0;
  overflow: hidden;
  text-overflow: ellipsis;
  white-space: nowrap;
}
.side-item {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 10px;
  height: 40px;
  padding: 0 14px;
  border-radius: 999px;
  font-size: 15px;
  font-weight: 600;
  color: var(--muted);
}
.side-item:hover {
  color: var(--ink);
}
.side-item[aria-current='page'] {
  background: var(--surface-2);
  color: var(--ink);
}
.is-rail .side-item {
  width: 44px;
  height: 44px;
  padding: 0;
  justify-content: center;
}
.side-badge {
  margin-left: auto;
  font-size: 12px;
  font-weight: 700;
  background: var(--primary);
  color: var(--on-primary);
  border-radius: 999px;
  padding: 1px 8px;
}
.dash-main {
  flex: 1;
  min-width: 0;
  padding: 30px 40px 170px;
  box-sizing: border-box;
}
.dash-mobile-bar {
  display: none;
}
@media (max-width: 767px) {
  .dash {
    flex-direction: column;
  }
  .dash-side {
    display: none;
  }
  .dash-mobile-bar {
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    gap: 10px;
    padding: 12px 16px;
    border-bottom: 1px solid var(--surface-2);
    background: var(--ground-sunken);
  }
  .dash-main {
    padding: 20px 16px 170px;
  }
}
.dash-h1 {
  margin: 0;
  font-family: var(--font-display);
  font-weight: 800;
  font-size: 30px;
  letter-spacing: -0.025em;
}

/* ---------- Requests table ---------- */
.rq-table {
  background: var(--surface);
  border: 1px solid var(--border);
  border-radius: var(--radius-lg);
  overflow: hidden;
}
.rq-row {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: 28px minmax(0, 1fr) 64px 200px 64px 30px;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 14px;
  min-height: 56px;
  padding: 0 18px;
  border-top: 1px solid var(--border);
  --lt-si-ground: var(--surface);
  color: var(--ink);
}
a.rq-row:hover {
  background: var(--surface-2);
  --lt-si-ground: var(--surface-2);
}
.rq-row.is-new {
  background: var(--primary-soft);
  --lt-si-ground: var(--primary-soft);
}
.rq-head {
  min-height: 40px;
  border-top: none;
  font-size: 12px;
  font-weight: 700;
  color: var(--muted);
}
.rq-title {
  font-weight: 600;
  font-size: 15px;
  white-space: nowrap;
  overflow: hidden;
  text-overflow: ellipsis;
}
.rq-tag {
  font-size: 12px;
  font-weight: 600;
  color: var(--ink-2);
  background: var(--surface-2);
  border-radius: 999px;
  padding: 2px 9px;
  white-space: nowrap;
}
a.rq-row:hover .rq-tag {
  background: var(--surface-3);
}
.rq-num {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: flex-end;
  gap: 4px;
  font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;
  font-weight: 700;
  font-size: 14px;
}
.rq-badge {
  font-size: 11px;
  font-weight: 700;
  border-radius: 999px;
  padding: 1px 7px;
  margin-left: 8px;
  white-space: nowrap;
}
.rq-date {
  font-size: 13px;
  color: var(--muted);
  font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;
}
@media (max-width: 900px) {
  .rq-row {
    grid-template-columns: 24px minmax(0, 1fr) 52px;
  }
  .rq-row > :nth-child(n + 4) {
    display: none;
  }
}
.rq-search {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 8px;
  height: 42px;
  padding: 0 16px;
  width: 280px;
  max-width: 100%;
  box-sizing: border-box;
  border-radius: 999px;
  background: var(--surface);
  border: 1px solid var(--border);
  color: var(--muted);
}
.rq-search:focus-within {
  outline: 3px solid var(--focus);
  outline-offset: 3px;
}
.rq-search input {
  all: unset;
  flex: 1;
  min-width: 0;
  font-size: 14px;
  color: var(--ink);
}

/* ---------- Settings ---------- */
.toc a {
  display: block;
  padding: 6px 12px;
  border-radius: 999px;
  font-size: 14px;
  font-weight: 600;
  color: var(--muted);
}
.toc a:hover {
  color: var(--ink);
}
.toc .grp {
  font-size: 12px;
  font-weight: 700;
  color: var(--muted);
  padding: 14px 12px 4px;
}
.sec {
  background: var(--surface);
  border: 1px solid var(--border);
  border-radius: var(--radius-lg);
  padding: 22px 24px;
  scroll-margin-top: 24px;
}
.sec h2 {
  margin: 0;
  font-family: var(--font-display);
  font-size: 20px;
  line-height: 25px;
  font-weight: 700;
  letter-spacing: -0.01em;
}
.sec .lede {
  margin: 4px 0 18px;
  font-size: 14px;
  color: var(--muted);
  max-width: 62ch;
}
.set-row {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 16px;
  padding: 14px 0;
  border-top: 1px solid var(--border);
  flex-wrap: wrap;
}
.set-row:first-of-type {
  border-top: none;
}
.set-row-main {
  flex: 1;
  min-width: 200px;
}
.set-row-main b {
  display: block;
  font-size: 15px;
  font-weight: 600;
}
.set-row-main > span {
  display: block;
  font-size: 13px;
  color: var(--muted);
  margin-top: 2px;
}
.set-field {
  all: unset;
  box-sizing: border-box;
  height: 40px;
  padding: 0 14px;
  border-radius: 999px;
  background: var(--ground);
  border: 1px solid var(--border);
  font-size: 14px;
  color: var(--ink);
}
.set-field:focus-visible {
  outline: 3px solid var(--focus);
  outline-offset: 3px;
}
.sw {
  all: unset;
  cursor: pointer;
  display: inline-flex;
  border-radius: 999px;
}
.sw:focus-visible {
  outline: 3px solid var(--focus);
  outline-offset: 3px;
}
.sw:disabled {
  cursor: not-allowed;
  opacity: 0.5;
}
.swatch {
  all: unset;
  cursor: pointer;
  width: 30px;
  height: 30px;
  border-radius: 50%;
  box-sizing: border-box;
}
.swatch[aria-checked='true'] {
  box-shadow:
    0 0 0 3px var(--surface),
    0 0 0 5px var(--ink);
}
.swatch:focus-visible {
  outline: 3px solid var(--focus);
  outline-offset: 4px;
}
.grip {
  all: unset;
  color: var(--muted);
  cursor: grab;
  display: grid;
  place-items: center;
  border-radius: 6px;
  touch-action: none;
}
.grip:focus-visible {
  outline: 3px solid var(--focus);
  outline-offset: 2px;
}
.badge {
  font-size: 11px;
  font-weight: 700;
  border-radius: 999px;
  padding: 2px 8px;
  background: var(--surface-2);
  color: var(--ink-2);
}
.set-grid {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: 24px minmax(0, 1fr) 110px 150px 36px;
  gap: 12px;
  align-items: center;
}
@media (max-width: 767px) {
  .settings-layout {
    grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 1fr) !important;
  }
  .toc {
    display: none;
  }
  .set-grid {
    grid-template-columns: 24px minmax(0, 1fr) 56px 56px 36px;
  }
}

/* ---------- Menus (shadcn Popover / DropdownMenu content) ---------- */
.lt-pop {
  background: var(--surface);
  color: var(--ink);
  border: 1px solid var(--border-strong);
  border-radius: var(--radius-md);
  box-shadow: var(--shadow-float);
  padding: 6px;
}
.lt-pop-row {
  all: unset;
  box-sizing: border-box;
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 10px;
  width: 100%;
  min-height: 38px;
  padding: 0 10px;
  border-radius: 12px;
  font-size: 14px;
  font-weight: 600;
  color: var(--ink);
  cursor: pointer;
}
.lt-pop-row:hover,
.lt-pop-row[data-highlighted],
.lt-pop-row:focus-visible {
  background: var(--surface-2);
  outline: none;
}
.lt-pop-row[aria-checked='true'] {
  color: var(--primary-text);
}
.lt-pop-row.is-danger {
  color: var(--danger-text);
}

/* Floating bars centre on the dashboard's content area, not the whole window. */
.dash {
  --dash-side: 244px;
}
.dash:has(.dash-side.is-rail) {
  --dash-side: 76px;
}
.dash .lt-floating {
  left: var(--dash-side);
}
@media (max-width: 767px) {
  .dash {
    --dash-side: 0px;
  }
}
```

```css
@import 'tailwindcss';
@import 'tw-animate-css';
@import 'shadcn/tailwind.css';
@import './tokens.css';
@import './lort.css';
@import './app.css';

/* shadcn primitives draw with Lort's tokens. Lort owns --primary, --border and --muted
   (a text colour), so the shadcn palette is not defined here: its Tailwind colour names
   point straight at Lort tokens instead. */
@theme inline {
  --font-sans: var(--font-sans);
  --font-heading: var(--font-display);
  --font-display: var(--font-display);
  --color-background: var(--ground);
  --color-foreground: var(--ink);
  --color-card: var(--surface);
  --color-card-foreground: var(--ink);
  --color-popover: var(--surface);
  --color-popover-foreground: var(--ink);
  --color-primary: var(--primary);
  --color-primary-foreground: var(--on-primary);
  --color-secondary: var(--surface-3);
  --color-secondary-foreground: var(--ink);
  --color-muted: var(--surface-2);
  --color-muted-foreground: var(--muted);
  --color-accent: var(--surface-2);
  --color-accent-foreground: var(--ink);
  --color-destructive: var(--danger-text);
  --color-border: var(--border);
  --color-input: var(--border-strong);
  --color-ring: var(--focus);
  --color-ground: var(--ground);
  --color-ground-sunken: var(--ground-sunken);
  --color-surface: var(--surface);
  --color-surface-2: var(--surface-2);
  --color-surface-3: var(--surface-3);
  --color-ink: var(--ink);
  --color-ink-2: var(--ink-2);
  --color-primary-text: var(--primary-text);
  --color-danger-text: var(--danger-text);
  --radius-sm: 10px;
  --radius-md: 16px;
  --radius-lg: 18px;
  --radius-xl: 22px;
}

@layer base {
  * {
    @apply border-border outline-ring/50;
  }
  html {
    @apply font-sans;
    -webkit-font-smoothing: antialiased;
  }
  body {
    margin: 0;
    background: var(--ground);
    color: var(--ink);
  }
  a {
    color: inherit;
    text-decoration: none;
  }
  ::selection {
    background: var(--primary);
    color: var(--on-primary);
  }
  :focus-visible {
    outline: 3px solid var(--focus);
    outline-offset: 3px;
  }
}
```

### Task 3: Web libraries: colour, API types, sessions, data hooks, toasts

**Files:**
- Create: `web/lib/{types,color,errors,brand-defaults,brand,url-state,time,pb,session,toast,queries,team-queries,use-media}.ts(x)` and their tests
- Create: `web/app/providers.tsx`; replace `web/app/layout.tsx`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `@/components/lort` (`Toast`, `Status`), `@/components/ui/tooltip`, the server endpoints from 1A and Task 1.
- Produces (types): `Ref`, `Person`, `Item`, `Page`, `Activity`, `Comment`, `Detail` (`thread` holds the comments), `Change`, `Stats`, `Brand`, `Config` (Brand + `boards`, `tags`), `Member`, `UserSummary` in `@/lib/types`, mirroring the Go JSON exactly.
- Produces (functions): `contrast(a, b)`, `parseHex`, `toHex`, `deriveBrand(hex) → { dark, light }: BrandTokens{primary, onPrimary, primaryText, primarySoft}`, `brandCss(hex) → string`, `PRESETS`, `GROUNDS` (`@/lib/color`); `errorMessage(err) → string` (`@/lib/errors`); `DEFAULT_BRAND` (`@/lib/brand-defaults`); `parseBrand(header)`, `getBrand()` (server only, `@/lib/brand`); `withParams(search, patch)`, `listParam(params, key, fallback)`, `useUrlState() → { params, set(patch) }` (`@/lib/url-state`); `timeAgo(iso)`, `shortDate(iso)` (`@/lib/time`); `userPb`, `adminPb`, `fileUrl(collection, id, name, token?)`, `avatarUrl(person)` (`@/lib/pb`); `useUser()`, `useAdmin()`, `isRejected(err)`, `signIn`, `signUp(email, password, website)`, `signInAdmin`, `refreshProfile`, `signOut`, `signOutAdmin` (`@/lib/session`); `ToastProvider`, `useToast() → (t: {message, action?, countdown?}) => void` (`@/lib/toast`); `Scope = 'board' | 'team'`, `pbFor(scope)`, `keys`, `useConfig`, `useFeed(scope, filters, live)`, `useRequest(scope, id)`, `useSimilar(q)`, `patchRequest`, `useVote()`, `useLiveRequests(scope) → { live, fresh }` (`@/lib/queries`); `useAllBoards`, `useTags`, `useSettings`, `useStats`, `useTeam` (`@/lib/team-queries`); `useMedia(query)` (`@/lib/use-media`); `Providers` (`@/app/providers`).

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

The colour tests check every preset and some awkward colours (pure yellow, mid grey, black, white) against WCAG AA in both themes. The session test guards against a render loop: PocketBase's `LocalAuthStore` parses a fresh record object on every read. The vote test checks the optimistic update, the server's count winning, and the rollback when the vote fails.

`web/lib/color.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';

import { GROUNDS, PRESETS, brandCss, contrast, deriveBrand, parseHex } from './color';

describe('contrast', () => {
  it('matches WCAG reference values', () => {
    expect(contrast('#000000', '#ffffff')).toBeCloseTo(21, 5);
    expect(contrast('#cc3a6d', '#ffffff')).toBeCloseTo(4.78, 2);
  });
});

describe('deriveBrand', () => {
  const inputs = [...PRESETS.map((p) => p.hex), '#ffff00', '#777777', '#000000', '#ffffff', '#00ff88'];

  it.each(inputs)('keeps every pairing readable for %s', (hex) => {
    const brand = deriveBrand(hex);
    for (const mode of ['dark', 'light'] as const) {
      const t = brand[mode];
      expect(contrast(t.onPrimary, t.primary)).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(4.5);
      for (const bg of [...Object.values(GROUNDS[mode]), t.primarySoft]) {
        expect(contrast(t.primaryText, bg)).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(4.5);
      }
    }
  });

  it('keeps Lort pink as it is, with white text on it', () => {
    const { dark, light } = deriveBrand('#cc3a6d');
    expect(dark.primary).toBe('#cc3a6d');
    expect(light.primary).toBe('#cc3a6d');
    expect(dark.onPrimary).toBe('#ffffff');
    // Pink is too dark to read as text on charcoal, so the dark theme lightens it.
    expect(dark.primaryText).not.toBe('#cc3a6d');
  });

  it('puts dark text on light primaries instead of changing them', () => {
    expect(deriveBrand('#ffff00').dark).toMatchObject({ primary: '#ffff00', onPrimary: '#1e1a18' });
  });

  it('falls back to Lort pink for anything that is not #rrggbb', () => {
    expect(deriveBrand('red').dark.primary).toBe('#cc3a6d');
  });
});

describe('brandCss', () => {
  it('writes both themes', () => {
    const css = brandCss('#2563eb');
    expect(css).toContain('--primary:#2563eb');
    expect(css).toContain('prefers-color-scheme: light');
    expect(css).toContain('[data-theme="light"]');
  });
});

it('parses hex with or without #', () => {
  expect(parseHex('cc3a6d')).toEqual([204, 58, 109]);
  expect(parseHex('#zzz')).toBeNull();
});
```

`web/lib/errors.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { ClientResponseError } from 'pocketbase';

import { errorMessage } from './errors';

const fail = (status: number, response: object) => new ClientResponseError({ status, response });

describe('errorMessage', () => {
  it('uses the server message', () => {
    expect(errorMessage(fail(400, { message: 'Pick a board for your idea.' }))).toBe('Pick a board for your idea.');
  });
  it('explains rate limits and lost connections', () => {
    expect(errorMessage(fail(429, { message: 'Too Many Requests.' }))).toMatch(/a bit fast/);
    expect(errorMessage(fail(0, {}))).toMatch(/Can't reach/);
  });
  it('prefers a field error over a generic message', () => {
    const err = fail(400, {
      message: 'Failed to create record.',
      data: { title: { message: 'Must be at least 3 character(s).' } },
    });
    expect(errorMessage(err)).toBe('Must be at least 3 character(s).');
  });
  it('falls back to a friendly sentence', () => {
    expect(errorMessage(new Error('boom'))).toMatch(/still here/);
  });
});
```

`web/lib/brand.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';

import { DEFAULT_BRAND, parseBrand } from './brand';

const encode = (value: object) => Buffer.from(JSON.stringify(value)).toString('base64');

describe('parseBrand', () => {
  it('reads the header and keeps defaults for anything missing', () => {
    const brand = parseBrand(
      encode({ appName: 'Driftnote — notes', primaryColor: '#2563eb', features: { email: true } }),
    );
    expect(brand.appName).toBe('Driftnote — notes');
    expect(brand.primaryColor).toBe('#2563eb');
    expect(brand.features).toEqual({ email: true, voice: false, turnstile: false });
    expect(brand.roadmapPublic).toBe(true);
  });
  it('falls back to defaults without a header or with a bad one', () => {
    expect(parseBrand(null)).toEqual(DEFAULT_BRAND);
    expect(parseBrand('not base64 json')).toEqual(DEFAULT_BRAND);
  });
});
```

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';

import { listParam, withParams } from './url-state';

describe('withParams', () => {
  it('sets, joins lists and removes empties', () => {
    expect(withParams('sort=newest&q=dark', { q: null, status: ['pending', 'planned'] })).toBe(
      '?sort=newest&status=pending%2Cplanned',
    );
    expect(withParams('q=dark', { q: '' })).toBe('');
  });
});

describe('listParam', () => {
  it('splits a list and falls back only when the key is absent', () => {
    expect(listParam(new URLSearchParams('status=pending,planned'), 'status')).toEqual(['pending', 'planned']);
    expect(listParam(new URLSearchParams(''), 'status', ['pending'])).toEqual(['pending']);
    expect(listParam(new URLSearchParams('status='), 'status', ['pending'])).toEqual([]);
  });
});
```

`web/lib/session.test.tsx`:

```tsx
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { act, renderHook } from '@testing-library/react';

import { userPb } from './pb';
import { ClientResponseError } from 'pocketbase';

import { isRejected, useUser } from './session';

// A token whose payload expires far in the future, so the auth store counts it as valid.
const payload = btoa(JSON.stringify({ exp: 4102444800, id: 'u1', type: 'auth' }));
const token = `x.${payload}.y`;

describe('useUser', () => {
  it('follows sign-in, profile changes and sign-out without re-render loops', () => {
    const { result } = renderHook(() => useUser());
    expect(result.current).toBeNull();
    act(() => userPb.authStore.save(token, { id: 'u1', collectionId: 'c', collectionName: 'users', name: 'mara' }));
    expect(result.current?.name).toBe('mara');
    const first = result.current;
    act(() => userPb.authStore.save(token, { id: 'u1', collectionId: 'c', collectionName: 'users', name: 'Mara K.' }));
    expect(result.current?.name).toBe('Mara K.');
    expect(result.current).not.toBe(first);
    act(() => userPb.authStore.clear());
    expect(result.current).toBeNull();
  });
});

describe('isRejected', () => {
  it('signs out on a refused session, not on a request that never got through', () => {
    expect(isRejected(new ClientResponseError({ status: 401 }))).toBe(true);
    expect(isRejected(new ClientResponseError({ status: 403 }))).toBe(true);
    // Status 0 is an aborted request (navigating away) or no network.
    expect(isRejected(new ClientResponseError({ status: 0, isAbort: true }))).toBe(false);
    expect(isRejected(new ClientResponseError({ status: 500 }))).toBe(false);
    expect(isRejected(new Error('boom'))).toBe(false);
  });
});
```

`web/lib/toast.test.tsx`:

```tsx
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { act, render, screen } from '@testing-library/react';

import { ToastProvider, useToast, type ToastInput } from './toast';

function Trigger({ toast }: { toast: ToastInput }) {
  const show = useToast();
  return <button onClick={() => show(toast)}>go</button>;
}

describe('toasts', () => {
  it('runs the action once and closes', () => {
    const onAction = vi.fn();
    render(
      <ToastProvider>
        <Trigger toast={{ message: 'Marked as completed', action: { label: 'Undo', onAction }, countdown: 60 }} />
      </ToastProvider>,
    );
    act(() => screen.getByText('go').click());
    act(() => screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Undo' }).click());
    expect(onAction).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    expect(screen.queryByText('Marked as completed')).toBeNull();
  });

  it('goes away on its own', () => {
    vi.useFakeTimers();
    render(
      <ToastProvider>
        <Trigger toast={{ message: 'Saved' }} />
      </ToastProvider>,
    );
    act(() => screen.getByText('go').click());
    expect(screen.getByText('Saved')).toBeTruthy();
    act(() => vi.advanceTimersByTime(5000));
    expect(screen.queryByText('Saved')).toBeNull();
    vi.useRealTimers();
  });
});
```

`web/lib/queries.test.tsx`:

```tsx
import { afterEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { act, renderHook, waitFor } from '@testing-library/react';
import { QueryClient, QueryClientProvider, type InfiniteData } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import type { ReactNode } from 'react';

import { userPb } from './pb';
import { useVote } from './queries';
import type { Item, Page } from './types';

const feedKey = ['feed', 'board', {}];

function setup(item: Partial<Item>) {
  const qc = new QueryClient({ defaultOptions: { queries: { retry: false }, mutations: { retry: false } } });
  const page = { page: 1, perPage: 20, totalItems: 1, items: [{ id: 'r1', ...item } as Item] } as Page;
  qc.setQueryData<InfiniteData<Page>>(feedKey, { pages: [page], pageParams: [1] });
  const wrapper = ({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) => (
    <QueryClientProvider client={qc}>{children}</QueryClientProvider>
  );
  const { result } = renderHook(() => useVote(), { wrapper });
  const current = () => qc.getQueryData<InfiniteData<Page>>(feedKey)!.pages[0].items[0];
  return { result, current };
}

afterEach(() => vi.restoreAllMocks());

describe('useVote', () => {
  it('shows the vote at once, then keeps what the server says', async () => {
    let answer!: (v: unknown) => void;
    vi.spyOn(userPb, 'send').mockReturnValue(new Promise((r) => (answer = r)));
    const { result, current } = setup({ voted: false, votes: 4 });
    act(() => result.current.mutate('r1'));
    await waitFor(() => expect(current()).toMatchObject({ voted: true, votes: 5 }));
    // Someone else voted meanwhile: the server's count wins.
    await act(async () => answer({ voted: true, votes: 6 }));
    await waitFor(() => expect(current()).toMatchObject({ voted: true, votes: 6 }));
  });

  it('puts the count back when the vote fails', async () => {
    let fail!: (e: unknown) => void;
    vi.spyOn(userPb, 'send').mockReturnValue(new Promise((_, r) => (fail = r)));
    const { result, current } = setup({ voted: true, votes: 5 });
    act(() => result.current.mutate('r1'));
    await waitFor(() => expect(current()).toMatchObject({ voted: false, votes: 4 }));
    await act(async () => fail(new Error('offline')));
    await waitFor(() => expect(current()).toMatchObject({ voted: true, votes: 5 }));
  });
});
```

```ts
// Shapes of the Go server's /api/lort responses (server/internal/requests, team, api).
import type { Status } from '@/components/lort';

export type { Status };

export interface Ref {
  id: string;
  name: string;
}

export interface Person {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  avatar: string;
  team: boolean;
}

export interface Item {
  id: string;
  title: string;
  body: string;
  status: Status;
  board: Ref;
  author: Person;
  tags: Ref[];
  comments: number;
  votes: number;
  voted: boolean;
  visible: boolean;
  awaitingApproval: boolean;
  new: boolean;
  created: string;
}

export interface Page {
  items: Item[];
  page: number;
  perPage: number;
  totalItems: number;
}

export interface Activity {
  id: string;
  kind: 'created' | 'status_changed' | 'merged_in' | 'merged_from' | 'board_changed';
  actor: Person;
  from: string;
  to: string;
  created: string;
}

export interface Comment {
  id: string;
  body: string;
  images: string[];
  internal: boolean;
  author: Person;
  created: string;
  replies: Comment[];
}

export interface Detail extends Item {
  images: string[];
  mergedInto: Ref | null;
  activity: Activity[];
  /** Top-level comments with their replies. (Item.comments is the count.) */
  thread: Comment[];
}

export interface Change {
  from: Status;
  to: Status;
  roadmap: 'added' | 'removed' | '';
}

export interface Stats {
  total: number;
  waiting: number;
  new: number;
}

export interface Brand {
  appName: string;
  logo: string;
  primaryColor: string;
  showLandingCard: boolean;
  roadmapPublic: boolean;
  features: { email: boolean; voice: boolean; turnstile: boolean };
}

export interface Config extends Brand {
  boards: Ref[];
  tags: Ref[];
}

export interface Member {
  id: string;
  email: string;
  name: string;
  created: string;
}

export interface UserSummary {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  email: string;
  joined: string;
  requests: number;
  votes: number;
  verified: boolean;
  banned: boolean;
  team: boolean;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement colour, errors, branding, URL state and time**

`deriveBrand` keeps the owner's colour wherever it already reads well, puts dark text on light primaries, and only moves OKLCH lightness when it must. The brand header is base64 JSON so any app name (accents, dashes) survives.

`web/lib/color.ts`:

```ts
// Turns the owner's primary colour into the tokens that use it, keeping WCAG AA contrast in
// both themes: on-primary (text on a primary fill), primary-text (primary as text) and
// primary-soft (a quiet primary tint behind primary-text).

type RGB = [number, number, number];

/** The grounds primary-text and primary-soft sit on, per theme (DESIGN.md). */
export const GROUNDS = {
  dark: { ground: '#1a1716', surface: '#24201e', surface2: '#2c2725' },
  light: { ground: '#f3f1ef', surface: '#ffffff', surface2: '#efeae6' },
} as const;

const WHITE = '#ffffff';
const INK = '#1e1a18';
const AA = 4.5;

export function parseHex(hex: string): RGB | null {
  const m = /^#?([0-9a-f]{6})$/i.exec(hex.trim());
  if (!m) return null;
  const n = parseInt(m[1], 16);
  return [(n >> 16) & 255, (n >> 8) & 255, n & 255];
}

export function toHex([r, g, b]: RGB): string {
  return (
    '#' +
    [r, g, b]
      .map((v) =>
        Math.round(Math.min(255, Math.max(0, v)))
          .toString(16)
          .padStart(2, '0'),
      )
      .join('')
  );
}

const lin = (c: number) => {
  const s = c / 255;
  return s <= 0.04045 ? s / 12.92 : ((s + 0.055) / 1.055) ** 2.4;
};
const unlin = (l: number) => 255 * (l <= 0.0031308 ? 12.92 * l : 1.055 * l ** (1 / 2.4) - 0.055);

function luminance(hex: string): number {
  const [r, g, b] = parseHex(hex) ?? [0, 0, 0];
  return 0.2126 * lin(r) + 0.7152 * lin(g) + 0.0722 * lin(b);
}

/** WCAG contrast ratio between two hex colours. */
export function contrast(a: string, b: string): number {
  const [hi, lo] = [luminance(a), luminance(b)].sort((x, y) => y - x);
  return (hi + 0.05) / (lo + 0.05);
}

// OKLCH, so lightness can move without the hue drifting.
function toOklch(hex: string): [number, number, number] {
  const [r, g, b] = (parseHex(hex) ?? [0, 0, 0]).map(lin);
  const l = Math.cbrt(0.4122214708 * r + 0.5363325363 * g + 0.0514459929 * b);
  const m = Math.cbrt(0.2119034982 * r + 0.6806995451 * g + 0.1073969566 * b);
  const s = Math.cbrt(0.0883024619 * r + 0.2817188376 * g + 0.6299787005 * b);
  const L = 0.2104542553 * l + 0.793617785 * m - 0.0040720468 * s;
  const A = 1.9779984951 * l - 2.428592205 * m + 0.4505937099 * s;
  const B = 0.0259040371 * l + 0.7827717662 * m - 0.808675766 * s;
  return [L, Math.hypot(A, B), Math.atan2(B, A)];
}

function fromOklch([L, C, h]: [number, number, number]): string {
  const A = C * Math.cos(h);
  const B = C * Math.sin(h);
  const l = (L + 0.3963377774 * A + 0.2158037573 * B) ** 3;
  const m = (L - 0.1055613458 * A - 0.0638541728 * B) ** 3;
  const s = (L - 0.0894841775 * A - 1.291485548 * B) ** 3;
  return toHex([
    unlin(4.0767416621 * l - 3.3077115913 * m + 0.2309699292 * s),
    unlin(-1.2684380046 * l + 2.6097574011 * m - 0.3413193965 * s),
    unlin(-0.0041960863 * l - 0.7034186147 * m + 1.707614701 * s),
  ]);
}

/** Moves hex's OKLCH lightness in `direction` until ok() passes (or lightness runs out). */
function nudge(hex: string, direction: 1 | -1, ok: (c: string) => boolean): string {
  const [L, C, h] = toOklch(hex);
  for (let step = 0; step <= 100; step++) {
    const next = Math.min(1, Math.max(0, L + direction * step * 0.01));
    // Fade chroma near the ends so the colour stays inside sRGB.
    const candidate = fromOklch([next, C * Math.min(1, (1 - Math.abs(next - 0.5) * 2) * 2.5), h]);
    if (ok(candidate)) return candidate;
  }
  return direction === 1 ? WHITE : INK;
}

function mix(a: string, b: string, amount: number): string {
  const [ra, ga, ba] = parseHex(a) ?? [0, 0, 0];
  const [rb, gb, bb] = parseHex(b) ?? [0, 0, 0];
  return toHex([ra * amount + rb * (1 - amount), ga * amount + gb * (1 - amount), ba * amount + bb * (1 - amount)]);
}

export interface BrandTokens {
  primary: string;
  onPrimary: string;
  primaryText: string;
  primarySoft: string;
}

/** Derives the primary-based tokens for both themes from any #rrggbb colour. */
export function deriveBrand(input: string): { dark: BrandTokens; light: BrandTokens } {
  let primary = toHex(parseHex(input) ?? parseHex('#cc3a6d')!);
  let onPrimary = WHITE;
  if (contrast(primary, WHITE) < AA) {
    if (contrast(primary, INK) >= AA) onPrimary = INK;
    else primary = nudge(primary, -1, (c) => contrast(c, WHITE) >= AA);
  }
  const theme = (mode: 'dark' | 'light'): BrandTokens => {
    const g = GROUNDS[mode];
    const primarySoft = mode === 'dark' ? mix(primary, g.ground, 0.2) : mix(primary, WHITE, 0.13);
    const readable = (c: string) => [g.ground, g.surface, g.surface2, primarySoft].every((bg) => contrast(c, bg) >= AA);
    const primaryText = readable(primary) ? primary : nudge(primary, mode === 'dark' ? 1 : -1, readable);
    return { primary, onPrimary, primaryText, primarySoft };
  };
  return { dark: theme('dark'), light: theme('light') };
}

/** CSS that applies a brand to the page, in the same theme structure as tokens.css. */
export function brandCss(input: string): string {
  const { dark, light } = deriveBrand(input);
  const vars = (t: BrandTokens) =>
    `--primary:${t.primary};--on-primary:${t.onPrimary};--primary-text:${t.primaryText};--primary-soft:${t.primarySoft};`;
  return (
    `:root,[data-theme="dark"]{${vars(dark)}}` +
    `@media (prefers-color-scheme: light){:root:not([data-theme="dark"]){${vars(light)}}}` +
    `[data-theme="light"]{${vars(light)}}`
  );
}

/** The eight presets in Settings → Branding. */
export const PRESETS = [
  { name: 'Pink', hex: '#cc3a6d' },
  { name: 'Coral', hex: '#d4553a' },
  { name: 'Amber', hex: '#b7791f' },
  { name: 'Leaf', hex: '#2f855a' },
  { name: 'Teal', hex: '#0f8a8a' },
  { name: 'Ocean', hex: '#2563eb' },
  { name: 'Grape', hex: '#7c4dcc' },
  { name: 'Graphite', hex: '#4b5563' },
] as const;
```

`web/lib/errors.ts`:

```ts
import { ClientResponseError } from 'pocketbase';

const FALLBACK = 'Something went wrong. Your changes are still here, so try again.';

/** A friendly, specific message for any failed call. */
export function errorMessage(err: unknown): string {
  if (err instanceof ClientResponseError) {
    if (err.status === 0) return "Can't reach the board. Check your connection and try again.";
    if (err.status === 429) return "You're going a bit fast. Try again in a minute.";
    const data = err.response?.data as Record<string, { message?: string }> | undefined;
    const field = data && Object.values(data).find((f) => f?.message)?.message;
    const message = (err.response?.message as string | undefined) || field;
    if (message && !/^Failed to|^Something went wrong while processing/.test(message)) return message;
    if (field) return field;
    if (err.status === 404) return 'That page is gone.';
    if (err.status === 401 || err.status === 403) return 'Sign in again to do that.';
  }
  return FALLBACK;
}
```

```ts
export const DEFAULT_BRAND: Brand = {
  appName: 'My app',
  logo: '',
```
