`web/lib/brand.ts`:

```ts
import { headers } from 'next/headers';

import { DEFAULT_BRAND } from './brand-defaults';
import type { Brand } from './types';

export { DEFAULT_BRAND };

/** Parses the X-Lort-Config header the Go proxy adds to every page request. */
export function parseBrand(header: string | null): Brand {
  if (!header) return DEFAULT_BRAND;
  try {
    const raw = typeof atob === 'function' ? atob(header) : Buffer.from(header, 'base64').toString('utf8');
    const value = JSON.parse(new TextDecoder().decode(Uint8Array.from(raw, (c) => c.charCodeAt(0))));
    return { ...DEFAULT_BRAND, ...value, features: { ...DEFAULT_BRAND.features, ...value.features } };
  } catch {
    return DEFAULT_BRAND;
  }
}

/** The board's branding for this request (server components only). */
export async function getBrand(): Promise<Brand> {
  return parseBrand((await headers()).get('x-lort-config'));
}
```

`web/lib/url-state.ts`:

```ts
'use client';

import { usePathname, useRouter, useSearchParams } from 'next/navigation';
import { useCallback } from 'react';

export type Patch = Record<string, string | string[] | null | undefined>;

/** Applies a patch to a query string. Empty values and empty lists remove the key. */
export function withParams(search: string, patch: Patch): string {
  const params = new URLSearchParams(search);
  for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(patch)) {
    const text = Array.isArray(value) ? value.join(',') : value;
    if (text) params.set(key, text);
    else params.delete(key);
  }
  const out = params.toString();
  return out ? `?${out}` : '';
}

/** Reads a comma-separated list param; `fallback` when the key is absent. */
export function listParam(params: URLSearchParams, key: string, fallback: string[] = []): string[] {
  const raw = params.get(key);
  if (raw === null) return fallback;
  return raw.split(',').filter(Boolean);
}

/** Filters, sorts and search live in the URL so they survive reloads and can be shared. */
export function useUrlState() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const pathname = usePathname();
  const params = useSearchParams();
  const set = useCallback(
    (patch: Patch) => router.replace(pathname + withParams(params.toString(), patch), { scroll: false }),
    [router, pathname, params],
  );
  return { params, set };
}
```

`web/lib/time.ts`:

```ts
const UNITS: [Intl.RelativeTimeFormatUnit, number][] = [
  ['year', 365 * 24 * 3600],
  ['month', 30 * 24 * 3600],
  ['week', 7 * 24 * 3600],
  ['day', 24 * 3600],
  ['hour', 3600],
  ['minute', 60],
];

const rtf = new Intl.RelativeTimeFormat('en', { numeric: 'auto' });

/** "just now", "2 hours ago", "yesterday", "3 weeks ago". */
export function timeAgo(iso: string, now = new Date()): string {
  const seconds = (new Date(iso).getTime() - now.getTime()) / 1000;
  for (const [unit, size] of UNITS) {
    if (Math.abs(seconds) >= size) return rtf.format(Math.round(seconds / size), unit);
  }
  return 'just now';
}

/** "11 Oct" this year, "11 Oct 2025" otherwise. */
export function shortDate(iso: string, now = new Date()): string {
  const d = new Date(iso);
  const sameYear = d.getFullYear() === now.getFullYear();
  return d.toLocaleDateString('en-GB', { day: 'numeric', month: 'short', ...(sameYear ? {} : { year: 'numeric' }) });
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Implement the PocketBase clients, sessions and toasts**

Board users and admins have separate auth stores. Signing in to the dashboard also signs the admin in on the board as their linked profile, through `GET /api/lort/team/me`.

`web/lib/pb.ts`:

```ts
// The two PocketBase clients. Board users (and admins acting as their board profile) use
// userPb; the dashboard's superuser session lives in adminPb. Both talk to the same origin,
// where the Go server serves PocketBase and /api/lort.
import PocketBase, { LocalAuthStore } from 'pocketbase';

function client(storageKey: string) {
  const base = typeof window === 'undefined' ? 'http://localhost' : window.location.origin;
  const pb = new PocketBase(base, new LocalAuthStore(storageKey));
  // TanStack Query dedupes requests itself; PocketBase's auto-cancel would abort its refetches.
  pb.autoCancellation(false);
  return pb;
}

export const userPb = client('lort_user');
export const adminPb = client('lort_admin');

/** URL of a stored file. Protected files (hidden requests' images) need a file token. */
export function fileUrl(collection: string, recordId: string, name: string, token?: string) {
  const url = `/api/files/${collection}/${recordId}/${encodeURIComponent(name)}`;
  return token ? `${url}?token=${encodeURIComponent(token)}` : url;
}

export function avatarUrl(person: { id: string; avatar: string }) {
  return person.avatar ? fileUrl('users', person.id, person.avatar) : undefined;
}
```

`web/lib/session.ts`:

```ts
'use client';

import { useMemo, useSyncExternalStore } from 'react';
import type PocketBase from 'pocketbase';
import type { RecordModel } from 'pocketbase';

import { adminPb, userPb } from './pb';

function useRecord(pb: PocketBase): RecordModel | null {
  // LocalAuthStore parses its record from localStorage on every read, so the snapshot is a
  // string (stable between reads) and the record is rebuilt only when it changes.
  const snapshot = useSyncExternalStore(
    (onChange) => pb.authStore.onChange(onChange),
    () => (pb.authStore.isValid ? JSON.stringify(pb.authStore.record) : ''),
    () => '',
  );
  return useMemo(() => (snapshot ? (JSON.parse(snapshot) as RecordModel) : null), [snapshot]);
}

/** The signed-in board user (an admin's board profile counts), or null. */
export function useUser() {
  return useRecord(userPb);
}

/** The signed-in superuser, or null. */
export function useAdmin() {
  return useRecord(adminPb);
}

/** Signs a board user in. Throws a PocketBase error on bad credentials. */
export async function signIn(email: string, password: string) {
  await userPb.collection('users').authWithPassword(email, password);
}

/** Creates a board account and signs it in. `website` is the honeypot field: leave it empty. */
export async function signUp(email: string, password: string, website = '') {
  await userPb.collection('users').create({ email, password, passwordConfirm: password, website });
  await signIn(email, password);
}

/**
 * Signs an admin in to the dashboard, then signs them in on the board as their linked profile,
 * so their comments, votes and suggestions there carry their name and the Team badge.
 */
export async function signInAdmin(email: string, password: string) {
  await adminPb.collection('_superusers').authWithPassword(email, password);
  await refreshProfile();
}

/** True when the server turned the session down, as opposed to the request not getting through. */
export function isRejected(err: unknown): boolean {
  const status = (err as { status?: number } | null)?.status;
  return status === 401 || status === 403;
}

/** Re-fetches the admin's board-profile token (it expires sooner than a dashboard session). */
export async function refreshProfile() {
  const me = await adminPb.send<{ profile: RecordModel; token: string }>('/api/lort/team/me', {});
  userPb.authStore.save(me.token, me.profile);
}

export function signOut() {
  userPb.authStore.clear();
}

export function signOutAdmin() {
  adminPb.authStore.clear();
  userPb.authStore.clear();
}
```

`web/lib/toast.tsx`:

```tsx
'use client';

import { createContext, useCallback, useContext, useRef, useState, type ReactNode } from 'react';

import { Toast } from '@/components/lort';

export interface ToastInput {
  message: string;
  /** At most one action, e.g. Undo. */
  action?: { label: string; onAction: () => void };
  /** Seconds the action stays available; draws the countdown bar and sets how long the toast stays. */
  countdown?: number;
}

interface Shown extends ToastInput {
  id: number;
}

const ToastContext = createContext<(t: ToastInput) => void>(() => {});

/** Shows a toast: a short past-tense confirmation with at most one action. */
export function useToast() {
  return useContext(ToastContext);
}

/** Holds the toast stack above the floating bars, newest at the bottom, three at most. */
export function ToastProvider({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const [toasts, setToasts] = useState<Shown[]>([]);
  const next = useRef(1);

  const dismiss = useCallback((id: number) => setToasts((all) => all.filter((t) => t.id !== id)), []);

  const show = useCallback(
    (t: ToastInput) => {
      const id = next.current++;
      setToasts((all) => [...all, { ...t, id }].slice(-3));
      setTimeout(() => dismiss(id), (t.countdown ?? 5) * 1000);
    },
    [dismiss],
  );

  return (
    <ToastContext.Provider value={show}>
      {children}
      <div
        aria-live="polite"
        style={{
          position: 'fixed',
          left: 0,
          right: 0,
          bottom: 104,
          zIndex: 60,
          display: 'flex',
          flexDirection: 'column',
          alignItems: 'center',
          gap: 8,
          padding: '0 16px',
          pointerEvents: 'none',
        }}
      >
        {toasts.map((t) => (
          <div key={t.id} style={{ pointerEvents: 'auto' }}>
            <Toast
              actionLabel={t.action?.label}
              countdown={t.countdown}
              onAction={() => {
                t.action?.onAction();
                dismiss(t.id);
              }}
              onDismiss={() => dismiss(t.id)}
            >
              {t.message}
            </Toast>
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>
    </ToastContext.Provider>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Implement the data hooks**

Every list is a TanStack infinite query keyed by scope and filters. Realtime events refetch lists after 300 ms. If the realtime connection drops, `useFeed` polls every three minutes instead.

`web/lib/queries.ts`:

```ts
'use client';

import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import {
  useInfiniteQuery,
  useMutation,
  useQuery,
  useQueryClient,
  type InfiniteData,
  type QueryClient,
} from '@tanstack/react-query';
import type PocketBase from 'pocketbase';

import { adminPb, userPb } from './pb';
import type { Config, Detail, Item, Page } from './types';

/** Who is asking: the public board (as the signed-in user, if any) or the dashboard (superuser). */
export type Scope = 'board' | 'team';

export const pbFor = (scope: Scope): PocketBase => (scope === 'team' ? adminPb : userPb);

export interface FeedFilters {
  sort?: string;
  status?: string[];
  board?: string[];
  tag?: string[];
  visibility?: string;
  q?: string;
}

export const keys = {
  config: ['config'] as const,
  feed: (scope: Scope, f: FeedFilters) => ['feed', scope, f] as const,
  request: (scope: Scope, id: string) => ['request', scope, id] as const,
  similar: (q: string) => ['similar', q] as const,
  stats: ['stats'] as const,
};

export function useConfig() {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: keys.config,
    queryFn: () => userPb.send<Config>('/api/lort/config', {}),
    staleTime: 60_000,
  });
}

function feedQuery(f: FeedFilters, page: number) {
  return {
    page,
    perPage: 20,
    sort: f.sort ?? '',
    status: (f.status ?? []).join(','),
    board: (f.board ?? []).join(','),
    tag: (f.tag ?? []).join(','),
    visibility: f.visibility ?? '',
    q: f.q ?? '',
  };
}

/** One list of requests, loaded 20 at a time. `live` false switches on a 3-minute poll. */
export function useFeed(scope: Scope, filters: FeedFilters, live = true) {
  return useInfiniteQuery({
    queryKey: keys.feed(scope, filters),
    queryFn: ({ pageParam }) => pbFor(scope).send<Page>('/api/lort/requests', { query: feedQuery(filters, pageParam) }),
    initialPageParam: 1,
    getNextPageParam: (last) => (last.page * last.perPage < last.totalItems ? last.page + 1 : undefined),
    refetchInterval: live ? false : 180_000,
  });
}

export function useRequest(scope: Scope, id: string) {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: keys.request(scope, id),
    queryFn: () => pbFor(scope).send<Detail>(`/api/lort/requests/${id}`, {}),
  });
}

export function useSimilar(q: string) {
  const text = q.trim();
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: keys.similar(text),
    queryFn: () => userPb.send<{ items: Item[] }>('/api/lort/similar', { query: { q: text } }),
    enabled: text.length >= 3,
    staleTime: 30_000,
  });
}

/** Applies fn to every cached copy of request id: in each feed page and on its page. */
export function patchRequest(qc: QueryClient, id: string, fn: (it: Item) => Item) {
  qc.setQueriesData<InfiniteData<Page>>(
    { queryKey: ['feed'] },
    (data) =>
      data && {
        ...data,
        pages: data.pages.map((p) => ({ ...p, items: p.items.map((it) => (it.id === id ? fn(it) : it)) })),
      },
  );
  qc.setQueriesData<Detail>({ queryKey: ['request'] }, (d) => (d && d.id === id ? { ...d, ...fn(d) } : d));
}

/** Toggles the viewer's vote with an optimistic update that rolls back on failure. */
export function useVote() {
  const qc = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: (id: string) =>
      userPb.send<{ voted: boolean; votes: number }>(`/api/lort/requests/${id}/vote`, { method: 'POST' }),
    onMutate: async (id) => {
      await qc.cancelQueries({ queryKey: ['feed'] });
      await qc.cancelQueries({ queryKey: ['request'] });
      const snapshot = [...qc.getQueriesData({ queryKey: ['feed'] }), ...qc.getQueriesData({ queryKey: ['request'] })];
      patchRequest(qc, id, (it) => ({ ...it, voted: !it.voted, votes: it.votes + (it.voted ? -1 : 1) }));
      return { snapshot };
    },
    onError: (_err, _id, ctx) => ctx?.snapshot.forEach(([key, data]) => qc.setQueryData(key, data)),
    onSuccess: (res, id) => patchRequest(qc, id, (it) => ({ ...it, voted: res.voted, votes: res.votes })),
  });
}

/**
 * Keeps request lists fresh: subscribes to PocketBase realtime (which applies the same access
 * rules as the API) and refetches on any change. Returns whether the connection is up and the
 * ids created since the page opened, for the "new" tint.
 */
export function useLiveRequests(scope: Scope) {
  const qc = useQueryClient();
  const [live, setLive] = useState(true);
  const [fresh, setFresh] = useState<Set<string>>(new Set());
  const timer = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | undefined>(undefined);

  useEffect(() => {
    const pb = pbFor(scope);
    let cancelled = false;
    let unsubscribe: (() => Promise<void>) | undefined;
    const refetch = () => {
      clearTimeout(timer.current);
      timer.current = setTimeout(() => {
        qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['feed', scope] });
        qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['request', scope] });
        if (scope === 'team') qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: keys.stats });
      }, 300);
    };
    pb.realtime.onDisconnect = () => !cancelled && setLive(false);
    Promise.all([
      pb.collection('requests').subscribe('*', (e) => {
        if (e.action === 'create') setFresh((s) => new Set(s).add(e.record.id));
        refetch();
      }),
      pb.collection('comments').subscribe('*', refetch),
    ])
      .then((subs) => {
        if (cancelled) return subs.forEach((u) => u());
        unsubscribe = async () => void (await Promise.all(subs.map((u) => u())));
        setLive(true);
      })
      .catch(() => !cancelled && setLive(false));
    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
      clearTimeout(timer.current);
      void unsubscribe?.();
    };
  }, [scope, qc]);

  return { live, fresh };
}
```

`web/lib/team-queries.ts`:

```ts
'use client';

import { useQuery } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import type { RecordModel } from 'pocketbase';

import { adminPb } from './pb';
import { keys } from './queries';
import type { Member, Stats } from './types';

/** Every board, private ones included, in display order. */
export function useAllBoards() {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: ['boards'],
    queryFn: () => adminPb.collection('boards').getFullList({ sort: 'order,created' }),
  });
}

export function useTags() {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: ['tags'],
    queryFn: () => adminPb.collection('tags').getFullList({ sort: 'order,created' }),
  });
}

/** The single settings record. */
export function useSettings() {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: ['settings'],
    queryFn: () => adminPb.collection('settings').getFirstListItem<RecordModel>(''),
  });
}

export function useStats() {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: keys.stats,
    queryFn: () => adminPb.send<Stats>('/api/lort/stats', {}),
    refetchInterval: 60_000,
  });
}

export function useTeam() {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: ['team'],
    queryFn: () => adminPb.send<{ items: Member[]; you: string }>('/api/lort/team', {}),
  });
}
```

`web/lib/use-media.ts`:

```ts
'use client';

import { useSyncExternalStore } from 'react';

/** Whether a media query matches; false during the server render. */
export function useMedia(query: string) {
  return useSyncExternalStore(
    (onChange) => {
      const list = window.matchMedia(query);
      list.addEventListener('change', onChange);
      return () => list.removeEventListener('change', onChange);
    },
    () => window.matchMedia(query).matches,
    () => false,
  );
}
```

`web/app/providers.tsx`:

```tsx
'use client';

import { QueryClient, QueryClientProvider } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import type { ReactNode } from 'react';

import { TooltipProvider } from '@/components/ui/tooltip';
import { ToastProvider } from '@/lib/toast';

let browserClient: QueryClient | undefined;

function queryClient() {
  const make = () => new QueryClient({ defaultOptions: { queries: { staleTime: 15_000, retry: 1 } } });
  // A fresh client per server render; one shared client in the browser.
  if (typeof window === 'undefined') return make();
  browserClient ??= make();
  return browserClient;
}

export function Providers({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  return (
    <QueryClientProvider client={queryClient()}>
      <TooltipProvider delayDuration={300}>
        <ToastProvider>{children}</ToastProvider>
      </TooltipProvider>
    </QueryClientProvider>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import type { Metadata } from 'next';
import { Bricolage_Grotesque, Figtree } from 'next/font/google';

import './globals.css';
import { brandCss } from '@/lib/color';
import { getBrand } from '@/lib/brand';
import { Providers } from './providers';

const display = Bricolage_Grotesque({ subsets: ['latin'], weight: ['700', '800'], variable: '--font-display' });
const sans = Figtree({ subsets: ['latin'], weight: ['400', '500', '600', '700'], variable: '--font-sans' });

export async function generateMetadata(): Promise<Metadata> {
  const brand = await getBrand();
  return { title: { default: `${brand.appName} feedback`, template: `%s · ${brand.appName}` } };
}

export default async function RootLayout({ children }: LayoutProps<'/'>) {
  const brand = await getBrand();
  return (
    <html lang="en" className={`${display.variable} ${sans.variable}`}>
      <head>
        {/* The owner's colour, with readable text colours derived for both themes. */}
        <style dangerouslySetInnerHTML={{ __html: brandCss(brand.primaryColor) }} />
      </head>
      <body>
        <Providers>{children}</Providers>
      </body>
    </html>
  );
}
```

### Task 4: The board: accounts, feed, Suggest something, request pages

**Files:**
- Create: `web/playwright.config.ts`, `web/e2e/{start.sh,global-setup.ts,helpers.ts,accounts.spec.ts,board.spec.ts}`
- Create: `web/components/board/*`, `web/components/request/*`
- Create: `web/app/(board)/layout.tsx`, `web/app/(board)/feedback/page.tsx`, `web/app/(board)/feedback/[id]/page.tsx`, `web/app/(board)/reset-password/page.tsx`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: everything from Tasks 2–3; `POST /api/collections/users/records` (sign-up, `website` honeypot), `POST /api/collections/requests/records` and `/comments/records` (multipart, hooks from 1A), `/api/lort/{config,requests,requests/{id},requests/{id}/vote,similar}`.
- Produces: `BrandProvider`, `useBrand()`, `BrandMark` (`@/components/board/brand`); `AuthProvider`, `useAuth() → { requireUser(mode?) → Promise<boolean> }`; `ProfileDialog`; `TopBar`, `PoweredBy`; `toSummary(item)`; `useVoteAction() → (id) => Promise<void>`; `FeedSkeleton`, `RequestSkeleton`; `FilterSheet`; `SuggestDialog({ open, onOpenChange, boards?, hrefFor? })`; `Feed`, `OPEN`; `RequestView({ detail, scope, onVote?, chips?, renderAvatar?, onComment, onSignIn?, canUseInternal?, voiceEnabled?, placeholder })`, `CommentInput`; `postComment(pb, requestId, input)`; `RequestPage`; `ResetPassword`. Routes `/feedback`, `/feedback/[id]`, `/reset-password`.
- E2E harness: `pnpm e2e` builds `../server` and this app, starts `lort` on a throwaway data dir (admin `admin@example.com` / `correcthorse`), seeds Features (public) and Bugs (public, needs approval) with tags editor, ui, sync and app name Driftnote, and turns rate limits off (the suite signs up more people per minute than one IP is allowed). Every spec runs in a dark and a light project. Set `PW_CHROMIUM` to use an installed Chromium.

- [ ] **Step 1: Add the end-to-end harness**

Playwright starts the real stack: the Go server supervising this app's standalone build.

`web/playwright.config.ts`:

```ts
import { defineConfig, devices } from '@playwright/test';

// End-to-end tests run against the real stack: the Go server (built from ../server) serving
// PocketBase and /api/lort, supervising this app's standalone build, on a throwaway data dir.
export default defineConfig({
  testDir: './e2e',
  fullyParallel: false,
  workers: 1,
  retries: 0,
  timeout: 30_000,
  globalSetup: './e2e/global-setup.ts',
  use: {
    baseURL: 'http://localhost:4052',
    trace: 'retain-on-failure',
    // Set PW_CHROMIUM to use an installed Chromium instead of Playwright's download.
    launchOptions: process.env.PW_CHROMIUM ? { executablePath: process.env.PW_CHROMIUM } : {},
  },
  projects: [
    { name: 'dark', use: { ...devices['Desktop Chrome'], colorScheme: 'dark' } },
    { name: 'light', use: { ...devices['Desktop Chrome'], colorScheme: 'light' } },
  ],
  webServer: {
    command: 'sh e2e/start.sh',
    url: 'http://localhost:4052/api/lort/health',
    timeout: 240_000,
    reuseExistingServer: false,
    stdout: 'pipe',
  },
});
```

`web/e2e/start.sh`:

```sh
#!/bin/sh
# Builds the Go server and this app, then starts the server on a fresh data dir with a known admin.
set -e
cd "$(dirname "$0")/.."
WEB=$(pwd)
OUT=$(mktemp -d)
(cd ../server && CGO_ENABLED=0 go build -o "$OUT/lort" .)
pnpm build > "$OUT/next-build.log"
cp -r public .next/standalone/ 2>/dev/null || true
cp -r .next/static .next/standalone/.next/
export LORT_DATA="$OUT/pb_data"
export LORT_URL=http://localhost:4052
export LORT_ADMIN_EMAIL=admin@example.com
export LORT_ADMIN_PASSWORD=correcthorse
export LORT_WEB_CMD="node $WEB/.next/standalone/server.js"
exec "$OUT/lort"
```

`web/e2e/global-setup.ts`:

```ts
// Seeds the fresh stack once: two boards (Bugs needs approval), three tags, the app name.
// It also switches rate limits off: the suite signs up more people per minute than Lort allows one IP.
const BASE = 'http://localhost:4052';

async function call(path: string, token: string, method: string, body?: unknown) {
  const res = await fetch(BASE + path, {
    method,
    headers: { Authorization: token, 'content-type': 'application/json' },
    body: body ? JSON.stringify(body) : undefined,
  });
  if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`${method} ${path}: ${res.status} ${await res.text()}`);
  return res.json();
}

export default async function globalSetup() {
  const auth = await fetch(`${BASE}/api/collections/_superusers/auth-with-password`, {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' },
    body: JSON.stringify({ identity: 'admin@example.com', password: 'correcthorse' }),
  }).then((r) => r.json());
  const token = auth.token as string;
  await call('/api/settings', token, 'PATCH', { rateLimits: { enabled: false } });
  await call('/api/collections/boards/records', token, 'POST', { name: 'Features', isPublic: true, order: 0 });
  await call('/api/collections/boards/records', token, 'POST', {
    name: 'Bugs',
    isPublic: true,
    requireApproval: true,
    order: 1,
  });
  for (const [i, name] of ['editor', 'ui', 'sync'].entries()) {
    await call('/api/collections/tags/records', token, 'POST', { name, order: i });
  }
  const settings = await call('/api/collections/settings/records', token, 'GET');
  await call(`/api/collections/settings/records/${settings.items[0].id}`, token, 'PATCH', { appName: 'Driftnote' });
}
```

`web/e2e/helpers.ts`:

```ts
import type { Page } from '@playwright/test';

let n = 0;

/** A unique email per test run, so tests and projects never collide. */
export function email(name: string) {
  return `${name}-${Date.now()}-${n++}@example.com`;
}

/** Signs up through the board's dialog, which opens from whatever asked for an account. */
export async function signUpInDialog(page: Page, address: string) {
  await page.getByLabel('Email').fill(address);
  await page.getByLabel('Password').fill('correcthorse');
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Create account' }).click();
}

export async function signInAdmin(page: Page) {
  await page.goto('/dashboard');
  await page.waitForURL(/\/dashboard\/login/);
  await page.getByLabel('Email').fill('admin@example.com');
  await page.getByLabel('Password').fill('correcthorse');
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Sign in' }).click();
  await page.waitForURL(/\/dashboard\/requests/);
}
```

```ts
import { expect, test } from '@playwright/test';

import { email, signUpInDialog } from './helpers';

test('a visitor creates an account and signs back in', async ({ page }) => {
  const address = email('sam');
  await page.goto('/feedback');
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Sign in' }).click();
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Create an account' }).click();
  await signUpInDialog(page, address);
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Your profile' }).click();
  await page.getByLabel('Name').fill('Sam W.');
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Save' }).click();
  await expect(page.getByText('Profile saved')).toBeVisible();

  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Your profile' }).click();
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Sign out' }).click();
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Sign in' }).click();
  await page.getByLabel('Email').fill(address);
  await page.getByLabel('Password').fill('wrong password');
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Sign in', exact: true }).last().click();
  await expect(page.getByRole('alert')).toContainText('don’t match');
  await page.getByLabel('Password').fill('correcthorse');
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Sign in', exact: true }).last().click();
  await expect(page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Your profile' })).toBeVisible();
});

test('the honeypot stops form-filling bots', async ({ page }) => {
  await page.goto('/feedback');
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Sign in' }).click();
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Create an account' }).click();
  await page.locator('.lt-honeypot input').fill('http://spam.example', { force: true });
  await signUpInDialog(page, email('bot'));
  await expect(page.getByRole('alert')).toContainText('Something went wrong');
});
```

```ts
import { expect, test } from '@playwright/test';

import { email, signUpInDialog } from './helpers';

test('a visitor signs up, suggests an idea, comments and votes', async ({ page }) => {
  const title = `Dark mode ${test.info().project.name} ${Date.now()}`;
  await page.goto('/feedback');
  await expect(page.getByRole('heading', { name: 'Got an idea for Driftnote?' })).toBeVisible();

  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Suggest something' }).first().click();
  await signUpInDialog(page, email('mara'));
  await page.getByLabel('Title').fill(title);
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'editor' }).click();
  await page.getByPlaceholder(/What would it help/).fill('Writing at night is rough on the eyes.');
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Send' }).click();

  await page.waitForURL(/\/feedback\/\w+/);
  await expect(page.getByRole('heading', { name: title })).toBeVisible();
  await expect(page.getByText('Your dot')).toBeVisible();

  await page.getByPlaceholder(/Add a comment/).fill('Same on iOS!');
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Send' }).click();
  await expect(page.getByText('Same on iOS!')).toBeVisible();
  await expect(page.getByRole('heading', { name: '1 comment' })).toBeVisible();

  await page.getByRole('link', { name: 'All ideas' }).click();
  const sticker = page.getByRole('button', { name: new RegExp(`Remove your upvote from ${title}`) });
  await expect(sticker).toHaveAttribute('aria-pressed', 'true');
  await sticker.click();
  await expect(page.getByRole('button', { name: new RegExp(`Upvote ${title}, 0 votes`) })).toBeVisible();
});

test('ideas on an approval board wait for the team', async ({ page }) => {
  const title = `Crash on paste ${test.info().project.name} ${Date.now()}`;
  await page.goto('/feedback');
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Suggest something' }).first().click();
  await signUpInDialog(page, email('priya'));
  await page.getByRole('radio', { name: 'Bugs' }).click();
  await page.getByLabel('Title').fill(title);
  await page.getByPlaceholder(/What would it help/).fill('The app closes when I paste a long screenshot.');
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Send' }).click();
  await expect(page.getByText('The team will check it before it shows on the board.')).toBeVisible();
  await page.waitForURL(/\/feedback\/\w+/);
  await expect(page.getByText(/Waiting for approval/)).toBeVisible();

  // Someone else can't see it.
  const url = page.url();
  await page.context().clearCookies();
  await page.evaluate(() => localStorage.clear());
  await page.goto(url);
  await expect(page.getByText('This idea isn’t here')).toBeVisible();
});
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Build branding, accounts and the top bar**

`requireUser()` opens the sign-up dialog and resolves once someone is signed in, so a sticker or Suggest click picks up where it left off. Signing in refetches everything, because votes and visibility depend on the viewer.

`web/components/board/brand.tsx`:

```tsx
'use client';

import { createContext, useContext, type ReactNode } from 'react';

import { DEFAULT_BRAND } from '@/lib/brand-defaults';
import type { Brand } from '@/lib/types';

const BrandContext = createContext<Brand>(DEFAULT_BRAND);

/** Hands the server-read branding (X-Lort-Config) to client components. */
export function BrandProvider({ brand, children }: { brand: Brand; children: ReactNode }) {
  return <BrandContext.Provider value={brand}>{children}</BrandContext.Provider>;
}

export function useBrand() {
  return useContext(BrandContext);
}

/** The app's logo, or its first letter on the primary colour, tilted like a sticker. */
export function BrandMark({ size = 32 }: { size?: number }) {
  const brand = useBrand();
  return (
    <span className="lt-mark" style={{ width: size, height: size, fontSize: size / 2 }} aria-hidden="true">
      {brand.logo ? <img src={brand.logo} alt="" /> : (brand.appName.trim()[0] ?? 'L').toUpperCase()}
    </span>
  );
}
```

`web/components/board/auth.tsx`:

```tsx
'use client';

import { useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { createContext, useCallback, useContext, useRef, useState, type FormEvent, type ReactNode } from 'react';

import { Button } from '@/components/lort';
import { Dialog, DialogContent, DialogDescription, DialogTitle } from '@/components/ui/dialog';
import { errorMessage } from '@/lib/errors';
import { userPb } from '@/lib/pb';
import { signIn, signUp } from '@/lib/session';
import { useBrand } from './brand';

type Mode = 'signin' | 'signup';

interface AuthApi {
  /** Resolves true once someone is signed in, opening the sign-up step if needed. */
  requireUser: (mode?: Mode) => Promise<boolean>;
}

const AuthContext = createContext<AuthApi>({ requireUser: async () => false });

export function useAuth() {
  return useContext(AuthContext);
}

export function AuthProvider({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const [mode, setMode] = useState<Mode | null>(null);
  const resolver = useRef<((ok: boolean) => void) | null>(null);
  const qc = useQueryClient();

  const finish = useCallback(
    (ok: boolean) => {
      // Signing in changes what every list shows (votes, the viewer's own hidden requests).
      if (ok) void qc.invalidateQueries();
      resolver.current?.(ok);
      resolver.current = null;
      setMode(null);
    },
    [qc],
  );

  const requireUser = useCallback(async (start: Mode = 'signup') => {
    if (userPb.authStore.isValid) return true;
    resolver.current?.(false);
    setMode(start);
    return new Promise<boolean>((resolve) => (resolver.current = resolve));
  }, []);

  return (
    <AuthContext.Provider value={{ requireUser }}>
      {children}
      <Dialog open={mode !== null} onOpenChange={(open) => !open && finish(false)}>
        {mode && <AuthForm mode={mode} onMode={setMode} onDone={() => finish(true)} />}
      </Dialog>
    </AuthContext.Provider>
  );
}

function AuthForm({ mode, onMode, onDone }: { mode: Mode; onMode: (m: Mode) => void; onDone: () => void }) {
  const brand = useBrand();
  const [email, setEmail] = useState('');
  const [password, setPassword] = useState('');
  const [website, setWebsite] = useState('');
  const [error, setError] = useState('');
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const signup = mode === 'signup';

  const submit = async (e: FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setBusy(true);
    setError('');
    try {
      if (signup) await signUp(email.trim(), password, website);
      else await signIn(email.trim(), password);
      onDone();
    } catch (err) {
      setError(signup ? errorMessage(err) : 'That email and password don’t match an account here.');
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <DialogContent showCloseButton={false} className="lt-dialog max-w-[420px] gap-[18px] sm:max-w-[420px]">
      <form onSubmit={submit} style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 18 }}>
        <div>
          <DialogTitle className="title" style={{ fontSize: 26, lineHeight: '31px' }}>
            {signup ? `Join the ${brand.appName} board` : 'Welcome back'}
          </DialogTitle>
          <DialogDescription style={{ margin: '8px 0 0', fontSize: 15, lineHeight: '22px', color: 'var(--ink-2)' }}>
            {signup
              ? `Suggest ideas, stick dots on the ones you want and hear when ${brand.appName} adds them.`
              : 'Sign in to suggest, vote and comment.'}
          </DialogDescription>
        </div>
        <div>
          <label className="lt-label" htmlFor="auth-email">
            Email
          </label>
          <input
            id="auth-email"
            className="lt-field"
            type="email"
            required
            autoComplete="email"
            value={email}
            onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
          />
        </div>
        <div>
          <label className="lt-label" htmlFor="auth-password">
            Password
          </label>
          <input
            id="auth-password"
            className="lt-field"
            type="password"
            required
            minLength={8}
            autoComplete={signup ? 'new-password' : 'current-password'}
            value={password}
            onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)}
          />
          {signup && <p className="lt-hint">At least 8 characters.</p>}
        </div>
        <label className="lt-honeypot" aria-hidden="true">
          Website
          <input tabIndex={-1} autoComplete="off" value={website} onChange={(e) => setWebsite(e.target.value)} />
        </label>
        {error && (
          <p className="lt-error" role="alert">
            {error}
          </p>
        )}
        <Button variant="primary" type="submit" disabled={busy} style={{ width: '100%' }}>
          {signup ? 'Create account' : 'Sign in'}
        </Button>
        <p style={{ margin: 0, fontSize: 14, color: 'var(--muted)', textAlign: 'center' }}>
          {signup ? 'Already have one? ' : 'New here? '}
          <button
            type="button"
            className="lt-link"
            style={{ all: 'unset', cursor: 'pointer', color: 'var(--primary-text)', fontWeight: 600 }}
            onClick={() => onMode(signup ? 'signin' : 'signup')}
          >
            {signup ? 'Sign in' : 'Create an account'}
          </button>
        </p>
        {!signup && !brand.features.email && (
          <p style={{ margin: 0, fontSize: 13, color: 'var(--muted)', textAlign: 'center' }}>
            Forgot your password? Ask the {brand.appName} team for a reset link.
          </p>
        )}
      </form>
    </DialogContent>
  );
}
```

`web/components/board/profile-dialog.tsx`:

```tsx
'use client';

import { useState, type FormEvent } from 'react';

import { Avatar, Button } from '@/components/lort';
import { Dialog, DialogContent, DialogTitle } from '@/components/ui/dialog';
import { errorMessage } from '@/lib/errors';
import { avatarUrl, userPb } from '@/lib/pb';
import { signOut, useUser } from '@/lib/session';
import { useToast } from '@/lib/toast';

/** Name, picture and sign out. (Email notification switches arrive with email in phase 3.) */
export function ProfileDialog({ open, onOpenChange }: { open: boolean; onOpenChange: (open: boolean) => void }) {
  const user = useUser();
  const toast = useToast();
  const [name, setName] = useState('');
  const [file, setFile] = useState<File | null>(null);
  const [error, setError] = useState('');
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  if (!user) return null;

  const save = async (e: FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setBusy(true);
    setError('');
    try {
      const data = new FormData();
      data.set('name', name.trim() || user.name);
      if (file) data.set('avatar', file);
      const record = await userPb.collection('users').update(user.id, data);
      userPb.authStore.save(userPb.authStore.token, record);
      toast({ message: 'Profile saved' });
      onOpenChange(false);
    } catch (err) {
      setError(errorMessage(err));
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <Dialog
      open={open}
      onOpenChange={(next) => {
        if (next) {
          setName(user.name);
          setFile(null);
          setError('');
        }
        onOpenChange(next);
      }}
    >
      <DialogContent showCloseButton={false} className="lt-dialog max-w-[420px] sm:max-w-[420px]">
        <form onSubmit={save} style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 18 }}>
          <DialogTitle className="title">Your profile</DialogTitle>
          <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 14 }}>
            <Avatar
              name={user.name || user.email}
              src={file ? URL.createObjectURL(file) : avatarUrl({ id: user.id, avatar: user.avatar })}
              size={56}
              keyline
            />
            <label className="lt-text-button" style={{ color: 'var(--primary-text)' }}>
              Change picture
              <input
                type="file"
                accept="image/png,image/jpeg,image/webp"
                hidden
                onChange={(e) => setFile(e.target.files?.[0] ?? null)}
              />
            </label>
          </div>
          <div>
            <label className="lt-label" htmlFor="profile-name">
              Name
            </label>
            <input
              id="profile-name"
              className="lt-field"
              maxLength={60}
              value={name}
              onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)}
            />
            <p className="lt-hint">Shown next to your ideas and comments.</p>
          </div>
          {error && (
            <p className="lt-error" role="alert">
              {error}
            </p>
          )}
          <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 10, alignItems: 'center' }}>
            <Button
              variant="ghost"
              type="button"
              onClick={() => {
                signOut();
                onOpenChange(false);
              }}
            >
              Sign out
            </Button>
            <span style={{ flex: 1 }} />
            <Button variant="primary" type="submit" disabled={busy}>
              Save
            </Button>
          </div>
        </form>
      </DialogContent>
    </Dialog>
  );
}
```

```tsx
'use client';

import Link from 'next/link';
import { usePathname, useRouter, useSearchParams } from 'next/navigation';
import { useState, type FormEvent } from 'react';

import { Avatar, Button, cx, Icon } from '@/components/lort';
import { avatarUrl } from '@/lib/pb';
import { useUser } from '@/lib/session';
import { useAuth } from './auth';
import { BrandMark, useBrand } from './brand';
import { ProfileDialog } from './profile-dialog';

/** Board pages. Roadmap and Changelog join in phase 2. */
const NAV = [{ href: '/feedback', label: 'Feedback' }];

export function TopBar() {
  const brand = useBrand();
  const user = useUser();
  const { requireUser } = useAuth();
  const pathname = usePathname();
  const router = useRouter();
  const params = useSearchParams();
  const [q, setQ] = useState(params.get('q') ?? '');
  const [searching, setSearching] = useState(false);
  const [profile, setProfile] = useState(false);

  const search = (e: FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    const text = q.trim();
    router.push(text ? `/feedback?q=${encodeURIComponent(text)}` : '/feedback');
  };

  return (
    <header className={cx('lt-topbar', searching && 'is-searching')}>
      <Link href="/feedback" className="lt-brand">
        <BrandMark />
        {brand.appName}
      </Link>
      <nav aria-label="Board" className="lt-nav">
        {NAV.map((item) => (
          <Link
            key={item.href}
            href={item.href}
            className="lt-nav-pill"
            aria-current={pathname.startsWith(item.href) ? 'page' : undefined}
          >
            {item.label}
          </Link>
        ))}
      </nav>
      <div style={{ flex: 1 }} />
      <form role="search" onSubmit={search} className="lt-search">
        <Icon name="search" size={16} />
        <input
          type="search"
          placeholder="Search ideas"
          aria-label="Search ideas"
          value={q}
          onChange={(e) => setQ(e.target.value)}
        />
      </form>
      <Button
        variant="ghost"
        icon="search"
        iconOnly
        aria-label="Search"
        className="lt-search-toggle"
        aria-expanded={searching}
        onClick={() => setSearching(!searching)}
      />
      {user ? (
        <>
          <button className="lt-avatar-button" aria-label="Your profile" onClick={() => setProfile(true)}>
            <Avatar
              name={user.name || user.email}
              src={avatarUrl({ id: user.id, avatar: user.avatar })}
              size={38}
              keyline
              team={user.isAdmin}
            />
          </button>
          <ProfileDialog open={profile} onOpenChange={setProfile} />
        </>
      ) : (
        <Button variant="secondary" onClick={() => requireUser('signin')}>
          Sign in
        </Button>
      )}
    </header>
  );
}

export function PoweredBy() {
  return (
    <footer className="lt-footer">
      <a href="https://github.com/hacker4ofakind/lort" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer">
        Powered by <b>Lort</b>
      </a>
    </footer>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Build the feed, the Suggest dialog and voting**

Signed-out visitors who click a sticker sign up first, then the vote is added (never removed, in case they had voted before). Filters live in the URL. Unticking every status goes back to the open ones. Phones get the compact bar and a filter sheet. New requests arrive with a fading tint. Requests created here invalidate the feed directly, because the realtime refetch is debounced and can be cancelled by navigation.

`web/components/board/summary.ts`:

```ts
import type { RequestSummary } from '@/components/lort';
import { avatarUrl } from '@/lib/pb';
import type { Item } from '@/lib/types';

/** An API item as the RequestCard expects it. */
export function toSummary(it: Item): RequestSummary {
  return {
    title: it.title,
    body: it.body,
    status: it.status,
    board: it.board.name,
    author: { name: it.author.name || 'Deleted account', avatarUrl: avatarUrl(it.author) },
    comments: it.comments,
    votes: it.votes,
    voted: it.voted,
    awaitingApproval: it.awaitingApproval,
  };
}
```

`web/components/board/vote.ts`:

```ts
'use client';

import { useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';

import { errorMessage } from '@/lib/errors';
import { userPb } from '@/lib/pb';
import { useVote } from '@/lib/queries';
import { useToast } from '@/lib/toast';
import type { Detail } from '@/lib/types';
import { useAuth } from './auth';

/**
 * What a sticker click does. Signed-out visitors sign up first, then the vote is added
 * (never removed: they may have voted before on another device).
 */
export function useVoteAction() {
  const { requireUser } = useAuth();
  const vote = useVote();
  const toast = useToast();
  const qc = useQueryClient();

  return async (id: string) => {
    if (!userPb.authStore.isValid) {
      if (!(await requireUser())) return;
      const fresh = await userPb.send<Detail>(`/api/lort/requests/${id}`, {});
      await qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['feed'] });
      if (fresh.voted) return;
    }
    vote.mutate(id, { onError: (err) => toast({ message: errorMessage(err) }) });
  };
}
```

`web/components/board/filter-sheet.tsx`:

```tsx
'use client';

import type { ReactNode } from 'react';

import { Button, StatusIcon, STATUS, STATUS_ORDER, Icon } from '@/components/lort';
import { Sheet, SheetContent, SheetTitle } from '@/components/ui/sheet';
import type { Ref } from '@/lib/types';

interface Props {
  open: boolean;
  onOpenChange: (open: boolean) => void;
  boards: Ref[];
  board: string[];
  status: string[];
  onChange: (patch: { board?: string[]; status?: string[] }) => void;
}

function toggle(list: string[], value: string) {
  return list.includes(value) ? list.filter((v) => v !== value) : [...list, value];
}

/** Phones: both filters in one bottom sheet, opened from the compact floating bar. */
export function FilterSheet({ open, onOpenChange, boards, board, status, onChange }: Props) {
  const row = (checked: boolean, onToggle: () => void, label: ReactNode, key: string) => (
    <label key={key} className="lt-menu-item">
      <input type="checkbox" checked={checked} onChange={onToggle} />
      <span className="lt-check" aria-hidden="true">
        <Icon name="check" size={12} strokeWidth={3} />
      </span>
      {label}
    </label>
  );
  return (
    <Sheet open={open} onOpenChange={onOpenChange}>
      <SheetContent
        side="bottom"
        className="lt-dialog rounded-b-none"
        style={{ borderBottom: 0, padding: '20px 16px 28px' }}
      >
        <SheetTitle className="title">Filter ideas</SheetTitle>
        {boards.length > 1 && (
          <fieldset style={{ border: 0, padding: 0, margin: 0 }}>
            <legend className="lt-label">Boards</legend>
            {boards.map((b) =>
              row(
                board.length === 0 || board.includes(b.id),
                () => onChange({ board: toggle(board.length ? board : boards.map((x) => x.id), b.id) }),
                b.name,
                b.id,
              ),
            )}
          </fieldset>
        )}
        <fieldset style={{ border: 0, padding: 0, margin: 0 }}>
          <legend className="lt-label">Statuses</legend>
          {STATUS_ORDER.map((s) =>
            row(
              status.includes(s),
              () => onChange({ status: toggle(status, s) }),
              <>
                <StatusIcon status={s} />
                {STATUS[s].label}
              </>,
              s,
            ),
          )}
        </fieldset>
        <Button variant="primary" onClick={() => onOpenChange(false)} style={{ width: '100%' }}>
          Show ideas
        </Button>
      </SheetContent>
    </Sheet>
  );
}
```

`web/components/board/suggest-dialog.tsx`:

```tsx
'use client';

import { useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';

import { Button, Composer, StatusIcon, VoteSticker, type ComposerSubmit } from '@/components/lort';
import { Dialog, DialogContent, DialogTitle } from '@/components/ui/dialog';
import { errorMessage } from '@/lib/errors';
import { userPb } from '@/lib/pb';
import { useConfig, useSimilar } from '@/lib/queries';
import { useUser } from '@/lib/session';
import { useToast } from '@/lib/toast';
import { useBrand } from './brand';
import { useVoteAction } from './vote';

/** "Suggest something": board, title with look-alikes, tags, and the details composer that sends it all. */
interface SuggestProps {
  open: boolean;
  onOpenChange: (open: boolean) => void;
  /** Boards to offer; defaults to the public ones. The dashboard passes every board. */
  boards?: { id: string; name: string }[];
  /** Where to go once it's posted; defaults to its board page. */
  hrefFor?: (id: string) => string;
}

export function SuggestDialog({ open, onOpenChange, boards: only, hrefFor = (id) => `/feedback/${id}` }: SuggestProps) {
  const brand = useBrand();
  const user = useUser();
  const config = useConfig().data;
  const router = useRouter();
  const toast = useToast();
  const vote = useVoteAction();
  const qc = useQueryClient();
  const boards = only ?? config?.boards ?? [];
  const tags = config?.tags ?? [];
  const [pickedBoard, setBoard] = useState('');
  // The top board is the default until someone picks another.
  const board = boards.some((b) => b.id === pickedBoard) ? pickedBoard : (boards[0]?.id ?? '');
  const [title, setTitle] = useState('');
  const [picked, setPicked] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [titleError, setTitleError] = useState('');
  const [debounced, setDebounced] = useState('');
  const similar = useSimilar(debounced).data?.items ?? [];

  useEffect(() => {
    const t = setTimeout(() => setDebounced(title), 300);
    return () => clearTimeout(t);
  }, [title]);

  const submit = async ({ text, images, visibility }: ComposerSubmit) => {
    if (title.trim().length < 3) {
      setTitleError('Give your idea a short title.');
      throw new Error('Add a title first. Your details are still here.');
    }
    const data = new FormData();
    data.set('board', board);
    data.set('title', title.trim());
    data.set('body', text);
    picked.forEach((t) => data.append('tags', t));
    images.forEach((f) => data.append('images', f));
    data.set('internal', String(visibility === 'internal'));
    data.set('website', '');
    try {
      const created = await userPb.collection('requests').create(data);
      void qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['feed'] });
      onOpenChange(false);
      setTitle('');
      setPicked([]);
      toast({
        message: created.awaitingApproval
          ? 'Thanks! The team will check it before it shows on the board.'
          : 'Posted. Thanks for the idea!',
      });
      router.push(hrefFor(created.id));
    } catch (err) {
      throw new Error(errorMessage(err));
    }
  };

  return (
    <Dialog open={open} onOpenChange={onOpenChange}>
      <DialogContent
        showCloseButton={false}
        className="lt-dialog top-[72px] max-h-[calc(100vh-96px)] max-w-[640px] translate-y-0 gap-5 overflow-y-auto sm:max-w-[640px]"
      >
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 12 }}>
          <DialogTitle className="title" style={{ flex: 1 }}>
            Suggest something
          </DialogTitle>
          <Button variant="ghost" icon="x" iconOnly aria-label="Close" onClick={() => onOpenChange(false)} />
        </div>

        {boards.length > 1 && (
          <div>
            <span className="lt-label" id="sg-board">
              Board
            </span>
            <div role="radiogroup" aria-labelledby="sg-board" className="lt-segment">
              {boards.map((b) => (
                <button
                  key={b.id}
                  type="button"
                  role="radio"
                  aria-checked={board === b.id}
                  className="lt-segment-item"
                  onClick={() => setBoard(b.id)}
                >
                  {b.name}
                </button>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        <div>
          <label className="lt-label" htmlFor="sg-title">
            Title
          </label>
          <input
            id="sg-title"
            className="lt-field lt-field-title"
            maxLength={140}
            placeholder={`What should ${brand.appName} do?`}
            value={title}
            aria-invalid={!!titleError}
            aria-describedby={titleError ? 'sg-title-error' : undefined}
            onChange={(e) => {
              setTitle(e.target.value);
              setTitleError('');
            }}
          />
          {titleError && (
            <p className="lt-error" id="sg-title-error" role="alert">
              {titleError}
            </p>
          )}
          {similar.length > 0 && (
            <div
              style={{
                marginTop: 10,
                padding: '12px 14px',
                borderRadius: 'var(--radius-md)',
                background: 'var(--surface-2)',
                ['--lt-si-ground' as string]: 'var(--surface-2)',
              }}
            >
              <div style={{ fontSize: 13, fontWeight: 600, color: 'var(--ink-2)', marginBottom: 8 }}>
                Is it one of these? A dot counts the same as a new post.
              </div>
              {similar.map((s) => (
                <div key={s.id} style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 12, padding: '8px 0' }}>
                  <VoteSticker
                    count={s.votes}
                    voted={s.voted}
                    size="sm"
                    subject={s.title}
                    onVotedChange={() => vote(s.id)}
                  />
                  <div style={{ flex: 1, minWidth: 0 }}>
                    <div style={{ fontWeight: 600, fontSize: 15 }}>{s.title}</div>
                    <div
                      style={{
                        display: 'flex',
                        alignItems: 'center',
                        gap: 8,
                        marginTop: 2,
                        fontSize: 13,
                        color: 'var(--muted)',
                      }}
                    >
                      <StatusIcon status={s.status} showLabel size={14} />· {s.board.name}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                  <a href={`/feedback/${s.id}`} className="lt-link" style={{ fontSize: 13 }}>
                    Open
                  </a>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>

        {tags.length > 0 && (
          <div>
            <span className="lt-label">
              Tags <span className="lt-label-note">(optional)</span>
            </span>
            <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexWrap: 'wrap', gap: 8 }}>
              {tags.map((t) => (
                <button
                  key={t.id}
                  type="button"
                  className="lt-toggle-tag"
                  aria-pressed={picked.includes(t.id)}
                  onClick={() =>
                    setPicked(picked.includes(t.id) ? picked.filter((x) => x !== t.id) : [...picked, t.id])
                  }
                >
                  {t.name}
                </button>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        <div className="sg-compose">
          <span className="lt-label">Details</span>
          <Composer
            placeholder="What would it help you do? Screenshots welcome."
            allowImages
            voiceEnabled={brand.features.voice}
            canUseInternal={!!user?.isAdmin}
            onSubmit={submit}
          />
        </div>
      </DialogContent>
    </Dialog>
  );
}
```

`web/components/board/feed.tsx`:

```tsx
'use client';

import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';

import { Button, FloatingBar, RequestCard } from '@/components/lort';
import { listParam, useUrlState } from '@/lib/url-state';
import { useConfig, useFeed, useLiveRequests } from '@/lib/queries';
import { useMedia } from '@/lib/use-media';
import { useAuth } from './auth';
import { FilterSheet } from './filter-sheet';
import { FeedSkeleton } from './skeletons';
import { SuggestDialog } from './suggest-dialog';
import { toSummary } from './summary';
import { useVoteAction } from './vote';

export const OPEN = ['pending', 'reviewing', 'planned', 'progress'];

/** The board: request cards, the floating bar, and the Suggest dialog. */
export function Feed() {
  const { params, set } = useUrlState();
  const sort = params.get('sort') ?? 'trending';
  const status = listParam(params, 'status', OPEN);
  const board = listParam(params, 'board');
  const q = params.get('q') ?? '';
  const filtered = params.has('status') || board.length > 0 || q !== '';

  const boards = useConfig().data?.boards ?? [];
  const { live, fresh } = useLiveRequests('board');
  const feed = useFeed('board', { sort, status, board, q }, live);
  const vote = useVoteAction();
  const { requireUser } = useAuth();
  const compact = useMedia('(max-width: 640px)');
  const [suggesting, setSuggesting] = useState(false);
  const [filtering, setFiltering] = useState(false);

  const sentinel = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  useEffect(() => {
    const el = sentinel.current;
    if (!el) return;
    const io = new IntersectionObserver(([entry]) => {
      if (entry.isIntersecting && feed.hasNextPage && !feed.isFetchingNextPage) void feed.fetchNextPage();
    });
    io.observe(el);
    return () => io.disconnect();
  }, [feed]);

  const suggest = async () => {
    if (await requireUser()) setSuggesting(true);
  };

  const items = feed.data?.pages.flatMap((p) => p.items) ?? [];

  return (
    <>
      {feed.isPending ? (
        <FeedSkeleton />
      ) : feed.isError ? (
        <div className="lt-empty" role="alert">
          <p className="title">The ideas didn’t load</p>
          <p style={{ color: 'var(--ink-2)', margin: 0 }}>Check your connection, then try again.</p>
          <Button variant="secondary" onClick={() => feed.refetch()}>
            Try again
          </Button>
        </div>
      ) : items.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="lt-empty">
          <span className="lt-empty-dot" aria-hidden="true" />
          {filtered ? (
            <>
              <p className="title" style={{ margin: 0 }}>
                Nothing matches
              </p>
              <p style={{ color: 'var(--ink-2)', margin: 0 }}>Try another search, or clear the filters.</p>
              <Button variant="secondary" onClick={() => set({ status: null, board: null, q: null })}>
                Clear filters
              </Button>
            </>
          ) : (
            <>
              <p className="title" style={{ margin: 0 }}>
                No ideas yet
              </p>
              <p style={{ color: 'var(--ink-2)', margin: 0 }}>Be the first to stick one on the wall.</p>
              <Button variant="primary" icon="plus" onClick={suggest}>
                Suggest something
              </Button>
            </>
          )}
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 12 }}>
          {items.map((it) => (
            <RequestCard
              key={it.id}
              request={toSummary(it)}
              href={`/feedback/${it.id}`}
              className={fresh.has(it.id) ? 'is-new' : undefined}
              onVotedChange={() => vote(it.id)}
            />
          ))}
          <div ref={sentinel} aria-hidden="true" style={{ height: 1 }} />
          {feed.isFetchingNextPage && <FeedSkeleton />}
        </div>
      )}

      <div className="lt-floating">
        <FloatingBar
          compact={compact}
          boards={boards.map((b) => ({ value: b.id, label: b.name }))}
          sort={sort}
          onSortChange={(s) => set({ sort: s === 'trending' ? null : s })}
          selectedBoards={board.length ? board : boards.map((b) => b.id)}
          onSelectedBoardsChange={(v) => set({ board: v.length === boards.length ? null : v })}
          selectedStatuses={status}
          onSelectedStatusesChange={(v) => set({ status: v.length === 0 || sameSet(v, OPEN) ? null : v })}
          onSuggest={suggest}
          onOpenFilters={() => setFiltering(true)}
        />
      </div>
      <FilterSheet
        open={filtering}
        onOpenChange={setFiltering}
        boards={boards}
        board={board}
        status={status}
        onChange={(patch) =>
          set({
            ...(patch.board && { board: patch.board.length === boards.length ? null : patch.board }),
            ...(patch.status && {
              status: patch.status.length === 0 || sameSet(patch.status, OPEN) ? null : patch.status,
            }),
          })
        }
      />
      <SuggestDialog open={suggesting} onOpenChange={setSuggesting} />
    </>
  );
}

function sameSet(a: string[], b: string[]) {
  return a.length === b.length && a.every((x) => b.includes(x));
}
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Build the request page**

`RequestView` is shared with the dashboard (Task 5): timeline newest first, one level of replies, internal notes for the team, and file tokens for a hidden request's protected images.

`web/components/request/request-view.tsx`:

```tsx
'use client';

import { useQuery } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import Link from 'next/link';
import { useState, type ReactNode } from 'react';
import { IconArrowsExchange } from '@tabler/icons-react';

import {
  Avatar,
  Button,
  Chip,
  Composer,
  Icon,
  InternalNote,
  STATUS,
  StatusIcon,
  VoteSticker,
  type ComposerSubmit,
  type Status,
} from '@/components/lort';
import { avatarUrl, fileUrl } from '@/lib/pb';
import { pbFor, type Scope } from '@/lib/queries';
import { shortDate, timeAgo } from '@/lib/time';
import type { Activity, Comment, Detail, Person } from '@/lib/types';

export interface CommentInput extends ComposerSubmit {
  parent?: string;
}

interface Props {
  detail: Detail;
  scope: Scope;
  /** Board: a working sticker. Dashboard: the count, read-only. */
  onVote?: () => void;
  /** Replaces the read-only status/board/tag chips (the dashboard passes editable ones). */
  chips?: ReactNode;
  /** Wraps a person's avatar (the dashboard adds the moderation popover). */
  renderAvatar?: (person: Person, avatar: ReactNode) => ReactNode;
  /** Null when signed out: a sign-in prompt shows instead of the composer. */
  onComment: ((input: CommentInput) => Promise<void>) | null;
  onSignIn?: () => void;
  canUseInternal?: boolean;
  voiceEnabled?: boolean;
  placeholder: string;
}

type Entry = { at: string; activity?: Activity; comment?: Comment };

/** A request page: header, body, chips, composer and the timeline (newest first). */
export function RequestView(props: Props) {
  const { detail: d, scope, onVote, chips, onComment, onSignIn, canUseInternal, voiceEnabled, placeholder } = props;
  const avatar = props.renderAvatar ?? ((_p: Person, node: ReactNode) => node);
  const [replyTo, setReplyTo] = useState<string | null>(null);

  // Hidden requests' images are protected: they need a file token for the viewer.
  const token = useQuery({
    queryKey: ['file-token', scope],
    queryFn: () => pbFor(scope).files.getToken(),
    enabled: !d.visible,
    staleTime: 60_000,
  }).data;
  const image = (collection: string, id: string, name: string) =>
    fileUrl(collection, id, name, d.visible ? undefined : token);

  const entries: Entry[] = [
    ...d.activity.map((a) => ({ at: a.created, activity: a })),
    ...d.thread.map((c) => ({ at: c.created, comment: c })),
  ].sort((a, b) => b.at.localeCompare(a.at));

  const closed = d.status === 'closed' || !!d.mergedInto;

  return (
    <article>
      {d.mergedInto && (
        <p className="lt-banner" style={{ marginBottom: 20 }}>
          <Icon name="merge" size={18} />
          <span>
            Merged into{' '}
            <Link
              className="lt-link"
              href={`${scope === 'team' ? '/dashboard/requests' : '/feedback'}/${d.mergedInto.id}`}
            >
              {d.mergedInto.name}
            </Link>
            . Votes moved there too.
          </span>
        </p>
      )}
      <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 24, alignItems: 'flex-start' }}>
        <div style={{ flex: 1, minWidth: 0 }}>
          <h1 className="display-l" style={{ margin: 0, textWrap: 'balance', overflowWrap: 'anywhere' }}>
            {d.title}
          </h1>
          <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 10, marginTop: 14, fontSize: 14 }}>
            {avatar(
              d.author,
              <Avatar name={d.author.name || '?'} src={avatarUrl(d.author)} size={32} keyline team={d.author.team} />,
            )}
            <b>{d.author.name || 'Deleted account'}</b>
            <span style={{ color: 'var(--muted)' }}>· {timeAgo(d.created)}</span>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', alignItems: 'center', gap: 8, paddingTop: 4 }}>
          <VoteSticker
            count={d.votes}
            voted={d.voted}
            subject={d.title}
            readOnly={!onVote || closed}
            onVotedChange={onVote}
          />
          {onVote && !closed && (
            <span style={{ fontSize: 12, fontWeight: 600, color: 'var(--muted)' }}>
              {d.voted ? 'Your dot' : 'Stick a dot'}
            </span>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      {d.body && (
        <p className="body lt-prose" style={{ margin: '20px 0 0', color: 'var(--ink-2)', maxWidth: '68ch' }}>
          {d.body}
        </p>
      )}
      {d.images.length > 0 && (
        <div className="lt-images">
          {d.images.map((name) => (
            <a key={name} href={image('requests', d.id, name)} target="_blank" rel="noreferrer">
              <img src={image('requests', d.id, name)} alt="Attached screenshot" />
            </a>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}

      <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 8, marginTop: 20, flexWrap: 'wrap' }}>
        {chips ?? (
          <>
            <Chip kind="status" status={d.status} interactive={false} />
            <Chip kind="board" label={d.board.name} interactive={false} />
            {d.tags.map((t) => (
              <Chip key={t.id} kind="tag" label={t.name} interactive={false} />
            ))}
            {d.awaitingApproval && (
              <span style={{ fontSize: 13, color: 'var(--muted)' }}>
                Waiting for approval. Only you and the team can see it.
              </span>
            )}
          </>
        )}
      </div>

      <div style={{ height: 1, background: 'var(--border)', margin: '30px 0 24px' }} />

      <h2 className="title" style={{ margin: '0 0 14px' }}>
        {d.comments === 1 ? '1 comment' : `${d.comments} comments`}
      </h2>
      {onComment ? (
        <Composer
          placeholder={placeholder}
          allowImages
          voiceEnabled={voiceEnabled}
          canUseInternal={canUseInternal}
          onSubmit={(v) => onComment(v)}
        />
      ) : (
        <div className="lt-banner">
          <span style={{ flex: 1 }}>Sign in to join the conversation.</span>
          <Button variant="secondary" size="sm" onClick={onSignIn}>
            Sign in
          </Button>
        </div>
      )}

      <section aria-label="Activity" style={{ marginTop: 28, display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 20 }}>
        {entries.map((e) =>
          e.activity ? (
            <ActivityLine key={e.activity.id} a={e.activity} board={d.board.name} />
          ) : (
            <CommentBlock
              key={e.comment!.id}
              c={e.comment!}
              avatar={avatar}
              image={(name, id) => image('comments', id, name)}
              replying={replyTo === e.comment!.id}
              onReply={onComment ? () => setReplyTo(replyTo === e.comment!.id ? null : e.comment!.id) : undefined}
              reply={
                onComment && replyTo === e.comment!.id ? (
                  <Composer
                    placeholder={`Reply to ${e.comment!.author.name}…`}
                    allowImages
                    voiceEnabled={voiceEnabled}
                    onSubmit={async (v) => {
                      await onComment({ ...v, visibility: 'public', parent: e.comment!.id });
                      setReplyTo(null);
                    }}
                  />
                ) : null
              }
            />
          ),
        )}
      </section>
    </article>
  );
}

function ActivityLine({ a, board }: { a: Activity; board: string }) {
  const who = <b style={{ color: 'var(--ink)' }}>{a.actor.name || 'Someone'}</b>;
  const line = (icon: ReactNode, text: ReactNode) => (
    <div
      style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 12, fontSize: 14, color: 'var(--muted)', paddingLeft: 4 }}
    >
      {icon}
      <span>{text}</span>
    </div>
  );
  switch (a.kind) {
    case 'created':
      return line(
        <StatusIcon status="pending" keyline size={20} />,
        <>
          {who} posted this in {board} · {shortDate(a.created)}
        </>,
      );
    case 'status_changed':
      return line(
        <StatusIcon status={a.to as Status} keyline size={20} />,
        <>
          {who}
          {a.actor.team ? ' from the team' : ''} moved this to{' '}
          <b style={{ color: 'var(--ink)' }}>{STATUS[a.to as Status]?.label ?? a.to}</b> · {timeAgo(a.created)}
        </>,
      );
    case 'board_changed':
      return line(
        <IconArrowsExchange size={20} stroke={2} />,
        <>
          {who} moved this from {a.from || 'another board'} to {a.to} · {timeAgo(a.created)}
        </>,
      );
    case 'merged_from':
      return line(
        <Icon name="merge" size={20} />,
        <>
          {who} merged a duplicate into this · {timeAgo(a.created)}
        </>,
      );
    case 'merged_in':
      return line(
        <Icon name="merge" size={20} />,
        <>
          {who} merged this into another request · {timeAgo(a.created)}
        </>,
      );
  }
}

function CommentBlock({
  c,
  avatar,
  image,
  onReply,
  replying,
  reply,
}: {
  c: Comment;
  avatar: (p: Person, node: ReactNode) => ReactNode;
  image: (name: string, commentId: string) => string;
  onReply?: () => void;
  replying: boolean;
  reply: ReactNode;
}) {
  const head = (x: Comment) => (
    <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 8, fontSize: 14 }}>
      {avatar(
        x.author,
        <Avatar name={x.author.name || '?'} src={avatarUrl(x.author)} size={26} team={x.author.team} />,
      )}
      <b>{x.author.name || 'Deleted account'}</b>
      {x.author.team && <span className="lt-team-badge">Team</span>}
      <span style={{ color: 'var(--muted)' }}>· {timeAgo(x.created)}</span>
    </div>
  );
  const images = (x: Comment) =>
    x.images.length > 0 && (
      <div className="lt-images" style={{ marginLeft: 34 }}>
        {x.images.map((n) => (
          <a key={n} href={image(n, x.id)} target="_blank" rel="noreferrer">
            <img src={image(n, x.id)} alt="Attached screenshot" />
          </a>
        ))}
      </div>
    );

  if (c.internal) {
    return (
      <InternalNote author={c.author.name} time={timeAgo(c.created)}>
        <span className="lt-prose">{c.body}</span>
      </InternalNote>
    );
  }
  return (
    <div style={{ padding: '0 4px' }}>
      {head(c)}
      <p className="body lt-prose" style={{ margin: '6px 0 0 34px', color: 'var(--ink-2)' }}>
        {c.body}
      </p>
      {images(c)}
      {onReply && (
        <div style={{ margin: '4px 0 0 34px' }}>
          <button className="lt-text-button" aria-expanded={replying} onClick={onReply}>
            Reply
          </button>
        </div>
      )}
      {c.replies.map((r) => (
        <div key={r.id} className="lt-reply">
          {head(r)}
          <p className="body lt-prose" style={{ margin: '6px 0 0 34px', color: 'var(--ink-2)' }}>
            {r.body}
          </p>
          {images(r)}
        </div>
      ))}
      {reply && <div style={{ margin: '12px 0 0 34px' }}>{reply}</div>}
    </div>
  );
}
```

`web/components/request/post-comment.ts`:

```ts
import type PocketBase from 'pocketbase';

import { errorMessage } from '@/lib/errors';
import type { CommentInput } from './request-view';

/** Posts a comment or reply with its images. Throws a friendly Error for the composer to show. */
export async function postComment(pb: PocketBase, requestId: string, input: CommentInput) {
  const data = new FormData();
  data.set('request', requestId);
  data.set('body', input.text);
  data.set('internal', String(input.visibility === 'internal'));
  if (input.parent) data.set('parent', input.parent);
  input.images.forEach((f) => data.append('images', f));
  data.set('website', '');
  try {
    await pb.collection('comments').create(data);
  } catch (err) {
    throw new Error(errorMessage(err));
  }
}
```

`web/components/board/request-page.tsx`:

```tsx
'use client';

import Link from 'next/link';
import { useEffect } from 'react';
import { useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { ClientResponseError } from 'pocketbase';

import { Button, Icon } from '@/components/lort';
import { RequestView } from '@/components/request/request-view';
import { postComment } from '@/components/request/post-comment';
import { userPb } from '@/lib/pb';
import { keys, useRequest } from '@/lib/queries';
import { useUser } from '@/lib/session';
import { useAuth } from './auth';
import { useBrand } from './brand';
import { RequestSkeleton } from './skeletons';
import { PoweredBy } from './top-bar';
import { useVoteAction } from './vote';

export function RequestPage({ id }: { id: string }) {
  const brand = useBrand();
  const user = useUser();
  const { requireUser } = useAuth();
  const qc = useQueryClient();
  const vote = useVoteAction();
  const request = useRequest('board', id);

  // Signing in or out changes what this page may show (votes, own hidden requests, internal notes).
  useEffect(() => {
    void qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: keys.request('board', id) });
  }, [user?.id, id, qc]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (request.data) document.title = `${request.data.title} · ${brand.appName}`;
  }, [request.data, brand.appName]);

  return (
    <main className="lt-page lt-page-narrow">
      <Link href="/feedback" className="lt-back">
        <span style={{ display: 'inline-flex', transform: 'rotate(-90deg)' }}>
          <Icon name="arrowUp" size={16} />
        </span>
        All ideas
      </Link>
      {request.isPending ? (
        <RequestSkeleton />
      ) : request.isError ? (
        <div className="lt-empty" role="alert">
          <span className="lt-empty-dot" aria-hidden="true" />
          {request.error instanceof ClientResponseError && request.error.status === 404 ? (
            <>
              <p className="title" style={{ margin: 0 }}>
                This idea isn’t here
              </p>
              <p style={{ color: 'var(--ink-2)', margin: 0 }}>
                It may have been removed, or it’s waiting for the team to approve it.
              </p>
            </>
          ) : (
            <>
              <p className="title" style={{ margin: 0 }}>
                This idea didn’t load
              </p>
              <Button variant="secondary" onClick={() => request.refetch()}>
                Try again
              </Button>
            </>
          )}
        </div>
      ) : (
        <RequestView
          detail={request.data}
          scope="board"
          onVote={() => vote(id)}
          canUseInternal={!!user?.isAdmin}
          voiceEnabled={brand.features.voice}
          placeholder={`Add a comment for ${request.data.author.name || 'them'} and the ${brand.appName} team…`}
          onSignIn={() => void requireUser('signin')}
          onComment={
            user
              ? async (input) => {
                  await postComment(userPb, id, input);
                  await qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: keys.request('board', id) });
                  void qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['feed'] });
                }
              : null
          }
        />
      )}
      <PoweredBy />
    </main>
  );
}
```

`web/components/board/reset-password.tsx`:

```tsx
'use client';

import Link from 'next/link';
import { useSearchParams } from 'next/navigation';
import { useState, type FormEvent } from 'react';

import { Button } from '@/components/lort';
import { userPb } from '@/lib/pb';

/** Where a password reset link (from the team, or by email in phase 3) lands. */
export function ResetPassword() {
  const token = useSearchParams().get('token') ?? '';
  const [password, setPassword] = useState('');
  const [state, setState] = useState<'idle' | 'busy' | 'done' | 'failed'>('idle');

  const submit = async (e: FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setState('busy');
    try {
      await userPb.collection('users').confirmPasswordReset(token, password, password);
      setState('done');
    } catch {
      setState('failed');
    }
  };

  return (
    <section
      className="lt-dialog"
      style={{ width: 420, maxWidth: '100%', display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 18 }}
    >
      <h1 className="title" style={{ margin: 0, fontSize: 26, lineHeight: '31px' }}>
        {state === 'done' ? 'Password changed' : 'Choose a new password'}
      </h1>
      {state === 'done' ? (
        <>
          <p style={{ margin: 0, color: 'var(--ink-2)' }}>You can sign in with it now.</p>
          <Link href="/feedback" className="lt-link">
            Back to the board
          </Link>
        </>
      ) : (
        <form onSubmit={submit} style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 18 }}>
          <div>
            <label className="lt-label" htmlFor="reset-password">
              New password
            </label>
            <input
              id="reset-password"
              className="lt-field"
              type="password"
              required
              minLength={8}
              autoComplete="new-password"
              value={password}
              onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)}
            />
            <p className="lt-hint">At least 8 characters.</p>
          </div>
          {state === 'failed' && (
            <p className="lt-error" role="alert">
              This link has expired or was already used. Ask the team for a new one.
            </p>
          )}
          <Button variant="primary" type="submit" disabled={state === 'busy' || !token} style={{ width: '100%' }}>
            Save password
          </Button>
        </form>
      )}
    </section>
  );
}
```
