---

### Task 5: The dashboard: sign-in, shell, requests table, request page

**Files:**
- Create: `web/e2e/dashboard.spec.ts`
- Create: `web/components/dashboard/{shell,login,requests-table,chip-button,triage,person-popover,request-dialogs,request-detail}.ts(x)`
- Create: `web/app/dashboard/layout.tsx`, `web/app/dashboard/(app)/{layout,page}.tsx`, `web/app/dashboard/(app)/requests/page.tsx`, `web/app/dashboard/(app)/requests/[id]/page.tsx`, `web/app/dashboard/{login,invite}/page.tsx`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Tasks 2–4 (`RequestView`, `RequestSkeleton`, `SuggestDialog`, `postComment`, `BrandProvider`, `useBrand`, `BrandMark`), the team endpoints from 1A, `/api/lort/requests/{id}/{status,undo,merge,seen,board}`, `/api/lort/stats`, `/api/lort/users/{id}{,/ban,/reset-link}`, PocketBase record updates as a superuser (`visible`, `tags+`/`tags-`, title, body, delete).
- Produces: `DashboardShell` (redirects to `/dashboard/login?next=` without a superuser session; renews the board-profile token on each visit and signs out only when the server refuses the session (401/403), never on an aborted request or a network blip; sidebar collapses to a 76px rail, remembered per browser); `LoginForm`, `InviteForm`; `RequestsTable`; `ChipButton`; `useTriage(detail) → (action: TriageAction) => Promise<void>`; `PersonPopover({ person, children })`; `MergeDialog`, `EditDialog`, `DeleteDialog`; `RequestDetail({ id })`. Routes `/dashboard` (→ requests), `/dashboard/requests`, `/dashboard/requests/[id]`, `/dashboard/login`, `/dashboard/invite`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing dashboard spec**

A visitor posts. The admin then signs in, finds the request by search with its New marker, and walks it from Pending to Completed: roadmap toast, Undo back to In progress, then an internal note and the member popover.

`web/e2e/dashboard.spec.ts`:

```ts
import { expect, test } from '@playwright/test';

import { email, signInAdmin, signUpInDialog } from './helpers';

test('an admin triages a request through to completed, with undo', async ({ page, browser }) => {
  // A visitor posts something first.
  const visitor = await browser.newPage();
  const title = `Export as Markdown ${test.info().project.name} ${Date.now()}`;
  await visitor.goto('/feedback');
  await visitor.getByRole('button', { name: 'Suggest something' }).first().click();
  await signUpInDialog(visitor, email('devon'));
  await visitor.getByLabel('Title').fill(title);
  await visitor.getByPlaceholder(/What would it help/).fill('One click to download everything as .md files.');
  await visitor.getByRole('button', { name: 'Send' }).click();
  await visitor.waitForURL(/\/feedback\/\w+/);
  await visitor.close();

  await signInAdmin(page);
  await page.getByLabel('Search requests').fill(title);
  const row = page.getByRole('row', { name: new RegExp(title) });
  await expect(row).toContainText('New');
  await row.click();

  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Accept' }).click();
  await expect(page.getByText('Moved to Reviewing')).toBeVisible();
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Add to roadmap' }).click();
  await expect(page.getByText('Added to the roadmap')).toBeVisible();
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Start working' }).click();
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Complete' }).click();
  await expect(page.getByText('Marked as completed')).toBeVisible();
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Undo' }).click();
  await expect(page.getByRole('toolbar', { name: 'Triage' }).getByRole('button', { name: 'Complete' })).toBeVisible();

  await page.getByRole('button', { name: /Visibility: Public/ }).click();
  await page.getByPlaceholder(/only the team can see/).fill('Reproduced on a Pixel 8.');
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Send' }).click();
  await expect(page.getByText('Only the team sees this')).toBeVisible();

  await page
    .getByRole('button', { name: /open member menu/ })
    .first()
    .click();
  await expect(page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Copy password reset link' })).toBeVisible();
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Build the shell and sign-in**

The guard waits for hydration (`useSyncExternalStore`), because the session lives in `localStorage`. A failed profile renewal signs out only on `isRejected(err)`: navigating away mid-request aborts it, and that must not end the session. Roadmap, Changelog and Docs join the sidebar in later phases.

`web/components/dashboard/shell.tsx`:

```tsx
'use client';

import Link from 'next/link';
import { usePathname, useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { useEffect, useState, useSyncExternalStore, type ReactNode } from 'react';
import {
  IconAdjustments,
  IconExternalLink,
  IconInbox,
  IconLayoutSidebarLeftCollapse,
  IconLayoutSidebarLeftExpand,
  IconMenu2,
} from '@tabler/icons-react';

import { Avatar, cx } from '@/components/lort';
import { BrandMark, useBrand } from '@/components/board/brand';
import { Sheet, SheetContent, SheetTitle } from '@/components/ui/sheet';
import { avatarUrl } from '@/lib/pb';
import { isRejected, refreshProfile, signOutAdmin, useAdmin, useUser } from '@/lib/session';
import { useStats } from '@/lib/team-queries';

/** Dashboard pages. Roadmap and Changelog arrive in phase 2, Docs in phase 6. */
const NAV = [
  { href: '/dashboard/requests', label: 'Requests', icon: IconInbox, badge: true },
  { href: '/dashboard/settings', label: 'Settings', icon: IconAdjustments, bottom: true },
];

const RAIL_KEY = 'lort_sidebar_rail';

const noop = () => () => {};

/** A boolean kept in localStorage (per browser), shared by every component that reads it. */
function useLocalFlag(key: string): [boolean, (on: boolean) => void] {
  const value = useSyncExternalStore(
    (onChange) => {
      window.addEventListener('storage', onChange);
      return () => window.removeEventListener('storage', onChange);
    },
    () => {
      try {
        return localStorage.getItem(key) === '1';
      } catch {
        return false;
      }
    },
    () => false,
  );
  const set = (on: boolean) => {
    try {
      localStorage.setItem(key, on ? '1' : '0');
    } catch {}
    window.dispatchEvent(new StorageEvent('storage', { key }));
  };
  return [value, set];
}

/** Signed-in admins only; everyone else goes to the sign-in page. */
export function DashboardShell({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const admin = useAdmin();
  const router = useRouter();
  const pathname = usePathname();
  // False during the server render and hydration, true after: the session lives in localStorage.
  const ready = useSyncExternalStore(
    noop,
    () => true,
    () => false,
  );

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!ready) return;
    if (!admin) {
      router.replace(`/dashboard/login?next=${encodeURIComponent(pathname)}`);
      return;
    }
    // The board-profile token expires sooner than the dashboard session: renew it each visit.
    // Only a rejected session signs out: a request cut short by navigating away, or a network
    // blip, must not.
    refreshProfile().catch((err: unknown) => {
      if (isRejected(err)) signOutAdmin();
    });
  }, [ready, admin, router, pathname]);

  if (!ready || !admin) return null;
  return <Frame>{children}</Frame>;
}

function Frame({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const [rail, setRail] = useLocalFlag(RAIL_KEY);
  const [menu, setMenu] = useState(false);
  const brand = useBrand();
  const toggleRail = () => setRail(!rail);

  return (
    <div className="dash">
      <aside aria-label="Dashboard" className={cx('dash-side', rail && 'is-rail')}>
        <div className="dash-brand" style={rail ? { padding: '4px 0 14px', flexDirection: 'column' } : undefined}>
          <BrandMark size={30} />
          {!rail && <span className="dash-brand-name">{brand.appName}</span>}
          <button
            type="button"
            className="lt-btn lt-btn-ghost lt-btn-icon lt-btn-sm"
            aria-label={rail ? 'Expand sidebar' : 'Collapse sidebar'}
            onClick={toggleRail}
          >
            {rail ? <IconLayoutSidebarLeftExpand size={18} /> : <IconLayoutSidebarLeftCollapse size={18} />}
          </button>
        </div>
        <Nav rail={rail} />
      </aside>

      <div className="dash-mobile-bar">
        <button
          type="button"
          className="lt-btn lt-btn-ghost lt-btn-icon"
          aria-label="Menu"
          onClick={() => setMenu(true)}
        >
          <IconMenu2 size={20} />
        </button>
        <BrandMark size={28} />
        <span className="dash-brand-name">{brand.appName}</span>
      </div>
      <Sheet open={menu} onOpenChange={setMenu}>
        <SheetContent side="left" className="w-[260px] p-3" style={{ background: 'var(--ground-sunken)' }}>
          <SheetTitle className="sr-only">Dashboard menu</SheetTitle>
          <div
            style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 4, height: '100%', paddingTop: 40 }}
            onClick={() => setMenu(false)}
          >
            <Nav rail={false} />
          </div>
        </SheetContent>
      </Sheet>

      <main className="dash-main">{children}</main>
    </div>
  );
}

function Nav({ rail }: { rail: boolean }) {
  const pathname = usePathname();
  const stats = useStats().data;
  const user = useUser();
  const item = (n: (typeof NAV)[number]) => {
    const Icon = n.icon;
    return (
      <Link
        key={n.href}
        href={n.href}
        className="side-item"
        aria-current={pathname.startsWith(n.href) ? 'page' : undefined}
        title={rail ? n.label : undefined}
      >
        <Icon size={20} stroke={2} aria-hidden="true" />
        {!rail && n.label}
        {!rail && n.badge && stats && stats.new > 0 && <span className="side-badge">{stats.new} new</span>}
      </Link>
    );
  };
  return (
    <>
      {NAV.filter((n) => !n.bottom).map(item)}
      <div style={{ flex: 1 }} />
      <a
        className="side-item"
        href="/feedback"
        target="_blank"
        rel="noreferrer"
        title={rail ? 'View the board' : undefined}
      >
        <IconExternalLink size={20} stroke={2} aria-hidden="true" />
        {!rail && 'View the board'}
      </a>
      {NAV.filter((n) => n.bottom).map(item)}
      {user && (
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 10, padding: rail ? '12px 0 2px' : '12px 8px 2px' }}>
          <Avatar name={user.name || user.email} src={avatarUrl({ id: user.id, avatar: user.avatar })} size={28} team />
          {!rail && (
            <>
              <span
                style={{
                  fontSize: 13,
                  fontWeight: 600,
                  flex: 1,
                  minWidth: 0,
                  overflow: 'hidden',
                  textOverflow: 'ellipsis',
                }}
              >
                {user.name}
              </span>
              <button
                className="lt-text-button"
                style={{ fontSize: 12 }}
                onClick={() => {
                  signOutAdmin();
                }}
              >
                Sign out
              </button>
            </>
          )}
        </div>
      )}
    </>
  );
}
```

`web/components/dashboard/login.tsx`:

```tsx
'use client';

import Link from 'next/link';
import { useRouter, useSearchParams } from 'next/navigation';
import { useState, type FormEvent } from 'react';

import { Button } from '@/components/lort';
import { BrandMark, useBrand } from '@/components/board/brand';
import { adminPb } from '@/lib/pb';
import { signInAdmin } from '@/lib/session';

/** Admin sign-in (design/canvas/D-Login.dc.html). */
export function LoginForm() {
  const brand = useBrand();
  const router = useRouter();
  const next = useSearchParams().get('next') || '/dashboard/requests';
  const [email, setEmail] = useState('');
  const [password, setPassword] = useState('');
  const [error, setError] = useState('');
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);

  const submit = async (e: FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setBusy(true);
    setError('');
    try {
      await signInAdmin(email.trim(), password);
      router.replace(next.startsWith('/dashboard') ? next : '/dashboard/requests');
    } catch {
      adminPb.authStore.clear();
      setError('That email and password don’t match an admin here.');
      setBusy(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <form
      onSubmit={submit}
      className="lt-dialog"
      style={{ width: 420, maxWidth: '100%', display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 18 }}
    >
      <BrandMark size={44} />
      <div>
        <h1 className="title" style={{ margin: 0, fontSize: 26, lineHeight: '31px' }}>
          {brand.appName} dashboard
        </h1>
        <p style={{ margin: '8px 0 0', fontSize: 15, color: 'var(--ink-2)' }}>
          For the {brand.appName} team. Looking for the board?{' '}
          <Link href="/feedback" className="lt-link">
            Go to feedback
          </Link>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div>
        <label className="lt-label" htmlFor="login-email">
          Email
        </label>
        <input
          id="login-email"
          className="lt-field"
          type="email"
          required
          autoComplete="email"
          value={email}
          onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
        />
      </div>
      <div>
        <label className="lt-label" htmlFor="login-password">
          Password
        </label>
        <input
          id="login-password"
          className="lt-field"
          type="password"
          required
          autoComplete="current-password"
          value={password}
          onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)}
        />
      </div>
      {error && (
        <p className="lt-error" role="alert">
          {error}
        </p>
      )}
      <Button variant="primary" type="submit" disabled={busy} style={{ width: '100%' }}>
        Sign in
      </Button>
      <p style={{ margin: 0, fontSize: 13, color: 'var(--muted)', textAlign: 'center' }}>
        Forgot it? On the server, run{' '}
        <code style={{ fontSize: 12, color: 'var(--ink-2)' }}>./setup.sh --reset-admin</code>
      </p>
    </form>
  );
}

/** Where an admin invite link lands: set a password, then sign in. */
export function InviteForm() {
  const token = useSearchParams().get('token') ?? '';
  const router = useRouter();
  const [password, setPassword] = useState('');
  const [error, setError] = useState('');
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);

  const submit = async (e: FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setBusy(true);
    setError('');
    try {
      await adminPb.collection('_superusers').confirmPasswordReset(token, password, password);
      router.replace('/dashboard/login');
    } catch {
      setError('This invite has expired or was already used. Ask for a new one.');
      setBusy(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <form
      onSubmit={submit}
      className="lt-dialog"
      style={{ width: 420, maxWidth: '100%', display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 18 }}
    >
      <h1 className="title" style={{ margin: 0, fontSize: 26, lineHeight: '31px' }}>
        Welcome to the team
      </h1>
      <p style={{ margin: 0, color: 'var(--ink-2)' }}>
        Choose a password for the dashboard. You’ll sign in with it next.
      </p>
      <div>
        <label className="lt-label" htmlFor="invite-password">
          Password
        </label>
        <input
          id="invite-password"
          className="lt-field"
          type="password"
          required
          minLength={8}
          autoComplete="new-password"
          value={password}
          onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)}
        />
        <p className="lt-hint">At least 8 characters.</p>
      </div>
      {error && (
        <p className="lt-error" role="alert">
          {error}
        </p>
      )}
      <Button variant="primary" type="submit" disabled={busy || !token} style={{ width: '100%' }}>
        Set password
      </Button>
    </form>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Build the requests table**

The filters, sort and search live in the URL. Rows link to the request with that query string attached, so the request page knows its queue. Refresh is limited to once every 10 seconds, and the Live label changes when realtime falls back to polling. Build class names with `cx()`, never template strings: the Prettier Tailwind plugin trims the space in `` `rq-row${x ? ' is-new' : ''}` ``.

`web/components/dashboard/requests-table.tsx`:

```tsx
'use client';

import Link from 'next/link';
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';

import { Avatar, Button, cx, FilterMenu, Icon, StatusIcon, STATUS, STATUS_ORDER } from '@/components/lort';
import { SuggestDialog } from '@/components/board/suggest-dialog';
import {
  DropdownMenu,
  DropdownMenuContent,
  DropdownMenuItem,
  DropdownMenuTrigger,
} from '@/components/ui/dropdown-menu';
import { Tooltip, TooltipContent, TooltipTrigger } from '@/components/ui/tooltip';
import { avatarUrl } from '@/lib/pb';
import { useFeed, useLiveRequests } from '@/lib/queries';
import { useAllBoards, useStats, useTags } from '@/lib/team-queries';
import { shortDate } from '@/lib/time';
import { listParam, useUrlState } from '@/lib/url-state';

const OPEN = ['pending', 'reviewing', 'planned', 'progress'];
const SORTS = [
  { value: 'newest', label: 'Newest first' },
  { value: 'oldest', label: 'Oldest first' },
  { value: 'top', label: 'Most upvoted' },
  { value: 'updated', label: 'Recently updated' },
];

/** /dashboard/requests (design/canvas/D-Requests.dc.html). Filters live in the URL. */
export function RequestsTable() {
  const { params, set } = useUrlState();
  const sort = params.get('sort') ?? 'newest';
  const status = listParam(params, 'status', OPEN);
  const board = listParam(params, 'board');
  const tag = listParam(params, 'tag');
  const visibility = params.get('visibility') ?? '';
  const q = params.get('q') ?? '';
  const [search, setSearch] = useState(q);
  const [suggesting, setSuggesting] = useState(false);
  const [cooling, setCooling] = useState(false);
  const qc = useQueryClient();

  const boards = useAllBoards().data ?? [];
  const tags = useTags().data ?? [];
  const stats = useStats().data;
  const { live, fresh } = useLiveRequests('team');
  const feed = useFeed('team', { sort, status, board, tag, visibility, q }, live);
  const items = feed.data?.pages.flatMap((p) => p.items) ?? [];
  const total = feed.data?.pages[0]?.totalItems ?? 0;

  useEffect(() => {
    const t = setTimeout(() => {
      if (search !== q) set({ q: search.trim() || null });
    }, 300);
    return () => clearTimeout(t);
  }, [search, q, set]);

  const sentinel = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  useEffect(() => {
    const el = sentinel.current;
    if (!el) return;
    const io = new IntersectionObserver(([e]) => {
      if (e.isIntersecting && feed.hasNextPage && !feed.isFetchingNextPage) void feed.fetchNextPage();
    });
    io.observe(el);
    return () => io.disconnect();
  }, [feed]);

  // Refreshing is limited to once every 10 seconds.
  const refresh = () => {
    setCooling(true);
    setTimeout(() => setCooling(false), 10_000);
    void qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['feed', 'team'] });
    void qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['stats'] });
  };

  const queue = params.toString();

  return (
    <div style={{ maxWidth: 1060, margin: '0 auto' }}>
      <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 12, flexWrap: 'wrap' }}>
        <h1 className="dash-h1">Requests</h1>
        {stats && (
          <span style={{ fontSize: 14, color: 'var(--muted)', marginTop: 6 }}>
            {stats.total} total · {stats.waiting} waiting for you
          </span>
        )}
        <span style={{ flex: 1 }} />
        <span style={{ fontSize: 13, color: 'var(--muted)' }}>{live ? 'Live' : 'Updates every 3 minutes'}</span>
        <Button variant="ghost" icon="rotate" iconOnly aria-label="Refresh" disabled={cooling} onClick={refresh} />
        <Button variant="primary" icon="plus" onClick={() => setSuggesting(true)}>
          New request
        </Button>
      </div>

      <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 8, margin: '22px 0 16px', flexWrap: 'wrap' }}>
        <label className="rq-search">
          <Icon name="search" size={16} />
          <input
            type="search"
            placeholder="Search titles and details"
            aria-label="Search requests"
            value={search}
            onChange={(e) => setSearch(e.target.value)}
          />
        </label>
        <FilterMenu
          allLabel="Every status"
          noun="statuses"
          placement="down"
          options={STATUS_ORDER.map((s) => ({ value: s, label: STATUS[s].label, icon: <StatusIcon status={s} /> }))}
          selected={status}
          onSelectedChange={(v) => set({ status: v.length === 0 || same(v, OPEN) ? null : v })}
          summarize={(v) => (same(v, OPEN) ? 'Open' : undefined)}
        />
        <FilterMenu
          allLabel="All boards"
          noun="boards"
          placement="down"
          options={boards.map((b) => ({ value: b.id, label: b.isPublic ? b.name : `${b.name} (private)` }))}
          selected={board}
          onSelectedChange={(v) => set({ board: v.length === boards.length ? null : v })}
        />
        {tags.length > 0 && (
          <FilterMenu
            allLabel="Any tag"
            noun="tags"
            placement="down"
            options={tags.map((t) => ({ value: t.id, label: t.name }))}
            selected={tag}
            onSelectedChange={(v) => set({ tag: v.length === tags.length ? null : v })}
          />
        )}
        <FilterMenu
          allLabel="Public and hidden"
          noun="kinds"
          placement="down"
          options={[
            { value: 'public', label: 'On the board' },
            { value: 'hidden', label: 'Hidden' },
          ]}
          selected={visibility ? [visibility] : []}
          onSelectedChange={(v) => set({ visibility: v.length === 1 ? v[0] : null })}
        />
        <span style={{ flex: 1 }} />
        <DropdownMenu>
          <DropdownMenuTrigger asChild>
            <Button variant="secondary" trailingIcon="chevronDown">
              {SORTS.find((s) => s.value === sort)?.label ?? 'Newest first'}
            </Button>
          </DropdownMenuTrigger>
          <DropdownMenuContent align="end" className="lt-pop">
            {SORTS.map((s) => (
              <DropdownMenuItem
                key={s.value}
                className="lt-pop-row"
                aria-checked={s.value === sort}
                onSelect={() => set({ sort: s.value === 'newest' ? null : s.value })}
              >
                {s.label}
              </DropdownMenuItem>
            ))}
          </DropdownMenuContent>
        </DropdownMenu>
      </div>

      <div role="table" aria-label="Requests" className="rq-table" aria-busy={feed.isPending}>
        <div role="row" className="rq-row rq-head">
          <span role="columnheader">
            <span className="lt-sr">Status</span>
          </span>
          <span role="columnheader">Request</span>
          <span role="columnheader" style={{ textAlign: 'right' }}>
            Votes
          </span>
          <span role="columnheader">Tags</span>
          <span role="columnheader">Date</span>
          <span role="columnheader">
            <span className="lt-sr">Requested by</span>
          </span>
        </div>
        {feed.isPending ? (
          <TableSkeleton />
        ) : items.length === 0 ? (
          <div className="lt-empty">
            <span className="lt-empty-dot" aria-hidden="true" />
            <p className="title" style={{ margin: 0 }}>
              {params.size ? 'Nothing matches' : 'No requests yet'}
            </p>
            <p style={{ color: 'var(--ink-2)', margin: 0 }}>
              {params.size
                ? 'Try another search, or clear the filters.'
                : 'Share your board link and the ideas will start coming in.'}
            </p>
          </div>
        ) : (
          items.map((r) => (
            <Link
              role="row"
              key={r.id}
              href={`/dashboard/requests/${r.id}${queue ? `?${queue}` : ''}`}
              className={cx('rq-row', (r.new || fresh.has(r.id)) && 'is-new')}
            >
              <span role="cell">
                <Tooltip>
                  <TooltipTrigger asChild>
                    <span style={{ display: 'inline-flex' }}>
                      <StatusIcon status={r.status} size={18} />
                    </span>
                  </TooltipTrigger>
                  <TooltipContent>{STATUS[r.status].label}</TooltipContent>
                </Tooltip>
              </span>
              <span role="cell" style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', minWidth: 0 }}>
                <span className="rq-title">{r.title}</span>
                {r.new && (
                  <span className="rq-badge" style={{ background: 'var(--primary)', color: 'var(--on-primary)' }}>
                    New
                  </span>
                )}
                {!r.visible && (
                  <span className="rq-badge" style={{ background: 'var(--surface-3)', color: 'var(--ink-2)' }}>
                    Hidden
                  </span>
                )}
              </span>
              <span role="cell" className="rq-num">
                <Icon name="arrowUp" size={14} strokeWidth={2.6} />
                {r.votes}
              </span>
              <span role="cell" style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 6, overflow: 'hidden' }}>
                {r.tags.map((t) => (
                  <span key={t.id} className="rq-tag">
                    {t.name}
                  </span>
                ))}
              </span>
              <span role="cell" className="rq-date">
                {shortDate(r.created)}
              </span>
              <span role="cell" title={r.author.name}>
                <Avatar name={r.author.name || '?'} src={avatarUrl(r.author)} size={26} team={r.author.team} />
              </span>
            </Link>
          ))
        )}
        <div ref={sentinel} aria-hidden="true" style={{ height: 1 }} />
      </div>
      {items.length > 0 && (
        <p style={{ margin: '14px 4px 0', fontSize: 13, color: 'var(--muted)' }}>
          Showing {items.length} of {total} requests{feed.hasNextPage ? '. Scroll for more.' : '.'}
        </p>
      )}
      <SuggestDialog
        open={suggesting}
        onOpenChange={setSuggesting}
        boards={boards.map((b) => ({ id: b.id, name: b.name }))}
        hrefFor={(id) => `/dashboard/requests/${id}`}
      />
    </div>
  );
}

function same(a: string[], b: string[]) {
  return a.length === b.length && a.every((x) => b.includes(x));
}

const ROWS = ['52%', '68%', '41%', '59%', '74%', '47%', '63%', '55%', '38%', '70%'];

/** Loading rows (design/canvas/S-Requests.dc.html). */
function TableSkeleton() {
  return (
    <>
      {ROWS.map((w, i) => (
        <div key={i} className="rq-row">
          <div className="sk" style={{ width: 18, height: 18 }} />
          <div className="sk" style={{ width: w, height: 14 }} />
          <div className="sk" style={{ width: 34, height: 14, justifySelf: 'end' }} />
          <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 6 }}>
            <div className="sk" style={{ width: i % 3 === 0 ? 64 : 48, height: 20 }} />
          </div>
          <div className="sk" style={{ width: 44, height: 12 }} />
          <div className="sk" style={{ width: 26, height: 26 }} />
        </div>
      ))}
    </>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Build the request page**

Status and board chips open menus (Radix triggers need `ChipButton`, which forwards props). Complete shows a 60-second Undo toast. It says people will be notified only once email is on (phase 3). Skip moves through the table's queue. Merge searches every request. The avatar popover copies a reset link or bans someone, and is never offered for team members.

```tsx
'use client';

import type { ComponentProps } from 'react';

import { cx } from '@/components/lort';

/** A Lort chip that can be a Radix menu trigger (forwards ref and every button prop). */
export function ChipButton({
  kind,
  className,
  ...rest
}: ComponentProps<'button'> & { kind: 'status' | 'board' | 'tag' | 'add' }) {
  return (
    <button
      type="button"
      className={cx('lt-chip', `lt-chip-${kind}`, kind === 'add' && 'lt-chip-square', className)}
      {...rest}
    />
  );
}
```

`web/components/dashboard/triage.ts`:

```ts
'use client';

import { useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';

import { STATUS, type TriageAction } from '@/components/lort';
import { useBrand } from '@/components/board/brand';
import { errorMessage } from '@/lib/errors';
import { adminPb } from '@/lib/pb';
import { keys } from '@/lib/queries';
import { useToast } from '@/lib/toast';
import type { Change, Detail } from '@/lib/types';

/** Runs a status action from the triage bar or the status chip, with its toast. */
export function useTriage(detail: Detail | undefined) {
  const qc = useQueryClient();
  const toast = useToast();
  const brand = useBrand();

  const refresh = () => {
    void qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['request', 'team'] });
    void qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['feed', 'team'] });
    void qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: keys.stats });
  };

  return async (action: TriageAction) => {
    if (!detail || !action.next) return;
    try {
      const ch = await adminPb.send<Change>(`/api/lort/requests/${detail.id}/status`, {
        method: 'POST',
        body: { action: action.id },
      });
      refresh();
      if (ch.to === 'completed') {
        const people = detail.votes;
        toast({
          // Emails go out after the undo window once email is set up (phase 3).
          message: brand.features.email
            ? `Marked as completed · ${people} ${people === 1 ? 'person' : 'people'} will be notified`
            : 'Marked as completed',
          countdown: 60,
          action: {
            label: 'Undo',
            onAction: async () => {
              try {
                await adminPb.send(`/api/lort/requests/${detail.id}/undo`, { method: 'POST' });
                refresh();
              } catch (err) {
                toast({ message: errorMessage(err) });
              }
            },
          },
        });
      } else if (ch.roadmap === 'added') toast({ message: 'Added to the roadmap' });
      else if (ch.roadmap === 'removed') toast({ message: 'Taken off the roadmap' });
      else toast({ message: `Moved to ${STATUS[ch.to].label}` });
    } catch (err) {
      toast({ message: errorMessage(err) });
    }
  };
}
```

`web/components/dashboard/person-popover.tsx`:

```tsx
'use client';

import { useState, type ReactNode } from 'react';
import { useQuery, useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';

import { Avatar } from '@/components/lort';
import { Popover, PopoverContent, PopoverTrigger } from '@/components/ui/popover';
import { errorMessage } from '@/lib/errors';
import { adminPb, avatarUrl } from '@/lib/pb';
import { useToast } from '@/lib/toast';
import { shortDate } from '@/lib/time';
import type { Person, UserSummary } from '@/lib/types';

/** Wraps a person's avatar with the moderation popover: who they are, reset link, ban. */
export function PersonPopover({ person, children }: { person: Person; children: ReactNode }) {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const toast = useToast();
  const qc = useQueryClient();
  const summary = useQuery({
    queryKey: ['user', person.id],
    queryFn: () => adminPb.send<UserSummary>(`/api/lort/users/${person.id}`, {}),
    enabled: open && !!person.id,
  }).data;

  if (!person.id) return <>{children}</>;

  const copyReset = async () => {
    try {
      const { link } = await adminPb.send<{ link: string }>(`/api/lort/users/${person.id}/reset-link`, {
        method: 'POST',
      });
      await navigator.clipboard.writeText(link);
      toast({ message: `Reset link copied. Send it to ${person.name}.` });
    } catch (err) {
      toast({ message: errorMessage(err) });
    }
  };

  const setBanned = async (banned: boolean) => {
    try {
      await adminPb.send(`/api/lort/users/${person.id}/ban`, { method: 'POST', body: { banned } });
      await qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['user', person.id] });
      toast({ message: banned ? `${person.name} can’t post or vote any more` : `${person.name} can post again` });
      setOpen(false);
    } catch (err) {
      toast({ message: errorMessage(err) });
    }
  };

  return (
    <Popover open={open} onOpenChange={setOpen}>
      <PopoverTrigger asChild>
        <button className="lt-avatar-button" aria-label={`${person.name || 'This person'}, open member menu`}>
          {children}
        </button>
      </PopoverTrigger>
      <PopoverContent align="start" className="lt-pop w-[270px] p-3">
        <div
          style={{
            display: 'flex',
            alignItems: 'center',
            gap: 10,
            padding: '2px 4px 10px',
            borderBottom: '1px solid var(--border)',
            marginBottom: 6,
          }}
        >
          <Avatar name={person.name || '?'} src={avatarUrl(person)} size={36} team={person.team} />
          <div style={{ minWidth: 0 }}>
            <div style={{ fontWeight: 700, fontSize: 14 }}>{person.name}</div>
            {summary ? (
              <>
                <div style={{ fontSize: 12, color: 'var(--muted)' }}>
                  {summary.email} · joined {shortDate(summary.joined)}
                </div>
                <div style={{ fontSize: 12, color: 'var(--muted)' }}>
                  {summary.requests} requests · {summary.votes} votes ·{' '}
                  {summary.verified ? 'verified email' : 'email not verified'}
                </div>
              </>
            ) : (
              <div className="sk" style={{ width: 160, height: 12, marginTop: 4 }} />
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
        {summary?.team ? (
          <p style={{ margin: '6px 10px', fontSize: 13, color: 'var(--muted)' }}>
            On the team. Manage admins in Settings → Team.
          </p>
        ) : (
          <>
            <button className="lt-pop-row" onClick={copyReset}>
              Copy password reset link
            </button>
            {summary?.banned ? (
              <button className="lt-pop-row" onClick={() => setBanned(false)}>
                Unban {person.name}
              </button>
            ) : (
              <button className="lt-pop-row is-danger" onClick={() => setBanned(true)}>
                Ban {person.name}
              </button>
            )}
          </>
        )}
      </PopoverContent>
    </Popover>
  );
}
```

`web/components/dashboard/request-dialogs.tsx`:

```tsx
'use client';

import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { useEffect, useState, type FormEvent } from 'react';
import { useQuery, useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';

import { Button, StatusIcon } from '@/components/lort';
import { Command, CommandEmpty, CommandInput, CommandItem, CommandList } from '@/components/ui/command';
import { Dialog, DialogContent, DialogDescription, DialogTitle } from '@/components/ui/dialog';
import { errorMessage } from '@/lib/errors';
import { adminPb } from '@/lib/pb';
import { useToast } from '@/lib/toast';
import type { Detail, Page } from '@/lib/types';

/** Merge: pick the request this one duplicates. Its voters move there. */
export function MergeDialog({
  detail,
  open,
  onOpenChange,
}: {
  detail: Detail;
  open: boolean;
  onOpenChange: (o: boolean) => void;
}) {
  const [q, setQ] = useState('');
  const [debounced, setDebounced] = useState('');
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const router = useRouter();
  const toast = useToast();
  const qc = useQueryClient();

  useEffect(() => {
    const t = setTimeout(() => setDebounced(q.trim()), 250);
    return () => clearTimeout(t);
  }, [q]);

  const results =
    useQuery({
      queryKey: ['merge-search', debounced],
      queryFn: () =>
        adminPb.send<Page>('/api/lort/requests', {
          query: { q: debounced, perPage: 8, sort: debounced ? 'top' : 'newest' },
        }),
      enabled: open,
    }).data?.items.filter((it) => it.id !== detail.id) ?? [];

  const merge = async (targetId: string, title: string) => {
    setBusy(true);
    try {
      await adminPb.send(`/api/lort/requests/${detail.id}/merge`, { method: 'POST', body: { target: targetId } });
      void qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['feed', 'team'] });
      void qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['request', 'team'] });
      toast({ message: `Merged into “${title}”` });
      onOpenChange(false);
      router.push(`/dashboard/requests/${targetId}`);
    } catch (err) {
      toast({ message: errorMessage(err) });
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <Dialog open={open} onOpenChange={onOpenChange}>
      <DialogContent showCloseButton={false} className="lt-dialog max-w-[560px] gap-4 sm:max-w-[560px]">
        <DialogTitle className="title">Merge into another request</DialogTitle>
        <DialogDescription style={{ margin: 0, color: 'var(--ink-2)', fontSize: 14 }}>
          “{detail.title}” closes and points there. Its {detail.votes}{' '}
          {detail.votes === 1 ? 'vote moves' : 'votes move'} over.
        </DialogDescription>
        <Command shouldFilter={false} className="lt-pop" style={{ boxShadow: 'none' }}>
          <CommandInput placeholder="Search requests" value={q} onValueChange={setQ} />
          <CommandList>
            <CommandEmpty>No other requests match.</CommandEmpty>
            {results.map((it) => (
              <CommandItem
                key={it.id}
                value={it.id}
                disabled={busy}
                onSelect={() => merge(it.id, it.title)}
                className="lt-pop-row"
              >
                <StatusIcon status={it.status} size={16} />
                <span
                  style={{ flex: 1, minWidth: 0, overflow: 'hidden', textOverflow: 'ellipsis', whiteSpace: 'nowrap' }}
                >
                  {it.title}
                </span>
                <span style={{ fontSize: 12, color: 'var(--muted)' }}>{it.votes} votes</span>
              </CommandItem>
            ))}
          </CommandList>
        </Command>
      </DialogContent>
    </Dialog>
  );
}

/** Edit a request's title and details. */
export function EditDialog({
  detail,
  open,
  onOpenChange,
}: {
  detail: Detail;
  open: boolean;
  onOpenChange: (o: boolean) => void;
}) {
  const [title, setTitle] = useState(detail.title);
  const [body, setBody] = useState(detail.body);
  const [error, setError] = useState('');
  const qc = useQueryClient();
  const toast = useToast();

  const save = async (e: FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setError('');
    try {
      await adminPb.collection('requests').update(detail.id, { title: title.trim(), body });
      void qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['request', 'team'] });
      void qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['feed', 'team'] });
      toast({ message: 'Saved' });
      onOpenChange(false);
    } catch (err) {
      setError(errorMessage(err));
    }
  };

  return (
    <Dialog
      open={open}
      onOpenChange={(o) => {
        if (o) {
          setTitle(detail.title);
          setBody(detail.body);
        }
        onOpenChange(o);
      }}
    >
      <DialogContent showCloseButton={false} className="lt-dialog max-w-[640px] sm:max-w-[640px]">
        <form onSubmit={save} style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 18 }}>
          <DialogTitle className="title">Edit request</DialogTitle>
          <div>
            <label className="lt-label" htmlFor="edit-title">
              Title
            </label>
            <input
              id="edit-title"
              className="lt-field lt-field-title"
              required
              minLength={3}
              maxLength={140}
              value={title}
              onChange={(e) => setTitle(e.target.value)}
            />
          </div>
          <div>
            <label className="lt-label" htmlFor="edit-body">
              Details
            </label>
            <textarea
              id="edit-body"
              className="lt-field"
              style={{ height: 160, padding: '12px 16px', borderRadius: 14, lineHeight: '23px' }}
              maxLength={10000}
              value={body}
              onChange={(e) => setBody(e.target.value)}
            />
          </div>
          {error && (
            <p className="lt-error" role="alert">
              {error}
            </p>
          )}
          <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'flex-end', gap: 10 }}>
            <Button variant="ghost" type="button" onClick={() => onOpenChange(false)}>
              Cancel
            </Button>
            <Button variant="primary" type="submit">
              Save
            </Button>
          </div>
        </form>
      </DialogContent>
    </Dialog>
  );
}

/** Delete, after a confirmation that says what goes with it. */
export function DeleteDialog({
  detail,
  open,
  onOpenChange,
  back,
}: {
  detail: Detail;
  open: boolean;
  onOpenChange: (o: boolean) => void;
  back: string;
}) {
  const router = useRouter();
  const qc = useQueryClient();
  const toast = useToast();
  const remove = async () => {
    try {
      await adminPb.collection('requests').delete(detail.id);
      void qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['feed', 'team'] });
      void qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['stats'] });
      toast({ message: 'Request deleted' });
      router.push(back);
    } catch (err) {
      toast({ message: errorMessage(err) });
    }
  };
  return (
    <Dialog open={open} onOpenChange={onOpenChange}>
      <DialogContent showCloseButton={false} className="lt-dialog max-w-[460px] sm:max-w-[460px]">
        <DialogTitle className="title">Delete this request?</DialogTitle>
        <DialogDescription style={{ margin: 0, color: 'var(--ink-2)' }}>
          “{detail.title}”, its {detail.votes} votes and {detail.comments} comments go for good. Closing it keeps them
          instead.
        </DialogDescription>
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'flex-end', gap: 10 }}>
          <Button variant="ghost" onClick={() => onOpenChange(false)}>
            Keep it
          </Button>
          <Button variant="danger" onClick={remove}>
            Delete
          </Button>
        </div>
      </DialogContent>
    </Dialog>
  );
}
```

`web/components/dashboard/request-detail.tsx`:

```tsx
'use client';

import Link from 'next/link';
import { useRouter, useSearchParams } from 'next/navigation';
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { IconDots } from '@tabler/icons-react';

import { Chip, Icon, StatusIcon, TriageBar, triageActions, type TriageAction } from '@/components/lort';
import { useBrand } from '@/components/board/brand';
import { RequestSkeleton } from '@/components/board/skeletons';
import { RequestView } from '@/components/request/request-view';
import { postComment } from '@/components/request/post-comment';
import {
  DropdownMenu,
  DropdownMenuContent,
  DropdownMenuItem,
  DropdownMenuTrigger,
} from '@/components/ui/dropdown-menu';
import { errorMessage } from '@/lib/errors';
import { adminPb, userPb } from '@/lib/pb';
import { keys, useFeed, useRequest } from '@/lib/queries';
import { useAllBoards, useTags } from '@/lib/team-queries';
import { useToast } from '@/lib/toast';
import { listParam } from '@/lib/url-state';
import { ChipButton } from './chip-button';
import { PersonPopover } from './person-popover';
import { DeleteDialog, EditDialog, MergeDialog } from './request-dialogs';
import { useTriage } from './triage';

const OPEN = ['pending', 'reviewing', 'planned', 'progress'];

/** /dashboard/requests/<id> (design/canvas/D-Request.dc.html). The URL keeps the table's filters, which make the queue. */
export function RequestDetail({ id }: { id: string }) {
  const brand = useBrand();
  const router = useRouter();
  const params = useSearchParams();
  const qc = useQueryClient();
  const toast = useToast();
  const request = useRequest('team', id);
  const d = request.data;
  const triage = useTriage(d);
  const boards = useAllBoards().data ?? [];
  const tags = useTags().data ?? [];
  const [merging, setMerging] = useState(false);
  const [editing, setEditing] = useState(false);
  const [deleting, setDeleting] = useState(false);

  // The queue is the table this page was opened from, with the same filters and sort.
  const queueFilters = {
    sort: params.get('sort') ?? 'newest',
    status: listParam(params, 'status', OPEN),
    board: listParam(params, 'board'),
    tag: listParam(params, 'tag'),
    visibility: params.get('visibility') ?? '',
    q: params.get('q') ?? '',
  };
  const queue = useFeed('team', queueFilters);
  const ids = queue.data?.pages.flatMap((p) => p.items.map((it) => it.id)) ?? [];
  const position = ids.indexOf(id);
  const total = queue.data?.pages[0]?.totalItems ?? 0;
  const search = params.toString() ? `?${params.toString()}` : '';
  const back = `/dashboard/requests${search}`;

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!d?.new) return;
    adminPb
      .send(`/api/lort/requests/${id}/seen`, { method: 'POST' })
      .then(() => {
        void qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: keys.stats });
        void qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['feed', 'team'] });
      })
      .catch(() => {});
  }, [d?.new, id, qc]);

  const refresh = () => {
    void qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: keys.request('team', id) });
    void qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['feed', 'team'] });
  };

  const skip = async () => {
    let next: string | undefined = ids[position + 1];
    if (!next && queue.hasNextPage) {
      const res = await queue.fetchNextPage();
      next = res.data?.pages.flatMap((p) => p.items.map((it) => it.id))[position + 1];
    }
    if (next) router.push(`/dashboard/requests/${next}${search}`);
    else toast({ message: 'That was the last one in this list' });
  };

  const onAction = (a: TriageAction) => {
    if (a.id === 'merge') setMerging(true);
    else if (a.id === 'skip') void skip();
    else void triage(a);
  };

  const update = async (data: Record<string, unknown>, done: string) => {
    try {
      await adminPb.collection('requests').update(id, data);
      refresh();
      toast({ message: done });
    } catch (err) {
      toast({ message: errorMessage(err) });
    }
  };

  const moveBoard = async (boardId: string, name: string) => {
    try {
      await adminPb.send(`/api/lort/requests/${id}/board`, { method: 'POST', body: { board: boardId } });
      refresh();
      toast({ message: `Moved to ${name}` });
    } catch (err) {
      toast({ message: errorMessage(err) });
    }
  };

  const statusLabel = queueFilters.status.length === 1 ? queueFilters.status[0] : '';

  return (
    <div style={{ maxWidth: 820, margin: '0 auto' }}>
      <nav
        aria-label="Breadcrumb"
        style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 8, fontSize: 14, color: 'var(--muted)', marginBottom: 22 }}
      >
        <Link href={back} className="lt-text-button" style={{ fontSize: 14 }}>
          Requests
        </Link>
        <span>/</span>
        {d ? <span>{d.board.name}</span> : <span className="sk" style={{ width: 48, height: 12 }} />}
        <span style={{ flex: 1 }} />
        {position >= 0 && (
          <span style={{ fontSize: 13, display: 'inline-flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 6 }}>
            {position + 1} of {total}
            {statusLabel && (
              <>
                {' '}
                in <StatusIcon status={statusLabel as never} showLabel size={14} />
              </>
            )}
          </span>
        )}
        {d && (
          <DropdownMenu>
            <DropdownMenuTrigger asChild>
              <button className="lt-btn lt-btn-ghost lt-btn-icon lt-btn-sm" aria-label="More actions">
                <IconDots size={18} />
              </button>
            </DropdownMenuTrigger>
            <DropdownMenuContent align="end" className="lt-pop">
              <DropdownMenuItem className="lt-pop-row" onSelect={() => setEditing(true)}>
                Edit title and details
              </DropdownMenuItem>
              <DropdownMenuItem className="lt-pop-row" asChild>
                <a href={`/feedback/${id}`} target="_blank" rel="noreferrer">
                  View on the board
                </a>
              </DropdownMenuItem>
              <DropdownMenuItem className="lt-pop-row is-danger" onSelect={() => setDeleting(true)}>
                Delete
              </DropdownMenuItem>
            </DropdownMenuContent>
          </DropdownMenu>
        )}
      </nav>

      {request.isPending ? (
        <RequestSkeleton />
      ) : request.isError || !d ? (
        <div className="lt-empty" role="alert">
          <p className="title" style={{ margin: 0 }}>
            This request is gone
          </p>
          <Link href={back} className="lt-link">
            Back to requests
          </Link>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <>
          <RequestView
            detail={d}
            scope="team"
            canUseInternal
            voiceEnabled={brand.features.voice}
            placeholder={`Reply to ${d.author.name || 'them'}, or leave a note for the team…`}
            renderAvatar={(person, avatar) => <PersonPopover person={person}>{avatar}</PersonPopover>}
            onComment={async (input) => {
              await postComment(userPb, id, input);
              refresh();
            }}
            chips={
              <>
                <DropdownMenu>
                  <DropdownMenuTrigger asChild>
                    <ChipButton kind="status" aria-label={`Status: ${d.status}. Change it`}>
                      <StatusIcon status={d.status} keyline showLabel size={18} />
                    </ChipButton>
                  </DropdownMenuTrigger>
                  <DropdownMenuContent align="start" className="lt-pop">
                    {triageActions(d.status)
                      .filter((a) => a.next)
                      .map((a) => (
                        <DropdownMenuItem key={a.id} className="lt-pop-row" onSelect={() => void triage(a)}>
                          <StatusIcon status={a.next!} size={16} />
                          {a.label}
                        </DropdownMenuItem>
                      ))}
                  </DropdownMenuContent>
                </DropdownMenu>

                <DropdownMenu>
                  <DropdownMenuTrigger asChild>
                    <ChipButton kind="board" aria-label={`Board: ${d.board.name}. Move it`}>
                      {d.board.name}
                    </ChipButton>
                  </DropdownMenuTrigger>
                  <DropdownMenuContent align="start" className="lt-pop">
                    {boards.map((b) => (
                      <DropdownMenuItem
                        key={b.id}
                        className="lt-pop-row"
                        aria-checked={b.id === d.board.id}
                        onSelect={() => b.id !== d.board.id && void moveBoard(b.id, b.name)}
                      >
                        {b.name}
                        {!b.isPublic && <span style={{ color: 'var(--muted)', fontWeight: 500 }}> (private)</span>}
                      </DropdownMenuItem>
                    ))}
                  </DropdownMenuContent>
                </DropdownMenu>

                {d.tags.map((t) => (
                  <DropdownMenu key={t.id}>
                    <DropdownMenuTrigger asChild>
                      <ChipButton kind="tag" aria-label={`Tag ${t.name}`}>
                        {t.name}
                      </ChipButton>
                    </DropdownMenuTrigger>
                    <DropdownMenuContent align="start" className="lt-pop">
                      <DropdownMenuItem
                        className="lt-pop-row"
                        onSelect={() => void update({ 'tags-': [t.id] }, `Removed ${t.name}`)}
                      >
                        Remove {t.name}
                      </DropdownMenuItem>
                    </DropdownMenuContent>
                  </DropdownMenu>
                ))}
                {tags.some((t) => !d.tags.find((x) => x.id === t.id)) && (
                  <DropdownMenu>
                    <DropdownMenuTrigger asChild>
                      <ChipButton kind="add" aria-label="Add a tag">
                        <Icon name="plus" size={16} />
                      </ChipButton>
                    </DropdownMenuTrigger>
                    <DropdownMenuContent align="start" className="lt-pop">
                      {tags
                        .filter((t) => !d.tags.find((x) => x.id === t.id))
                        .map((t) => (
                          <DropdownMenuItem
                            key={t.id}
                            className="lt-pop-row"
                            onSelect={() => void update({ 'tags+': [t.id] }, `Tagged ${t.name}`)}
                          >
                            {t.name}
                          </DropdownMenuItem>
                        ))}
                    </DropdownMenuContent>
                  </DropdownMenu>
                )}

                <Chip
                  kind="switch"
                  label="On the board"
                  checked={d.visible}
                  onCheckedChange={(on) =>
                    void update(
                      on ? { visible: true, awaitingApproval: false } : { visible: false },
                      on ? 'Now on the board' : 'Hidden from the board',
                    )
                  }
                />
              </>
            }
          />
          <div className="lt-floating">
            <TriageBar status={d.status} onAction={onAction} />
          </div>
          <MergeDialog detail={d} open={merging} onOpenChange={setMerging} />
          <EditDialog detail={d} open={editing} onOpenChange={setEditing} />
          <DeleteDialog detail={d} open={deleting} onOpenChange={setDeleting} back={back} />
        </>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
```

```tsx
describe('Toggle', () => {
```

```ts
import { expect, test } from '@playwright/test';

import { signInAdmin } from './helpers';

test('changing the primary colour rebrands the board', async ({ page }) => {

    .poll(() => page.evaluate(() => getComputedStyle(document.documentElement).getPropertyValue('--primary').trim()))

});

test('boards and tags can be added, renamed and removed', async ({ page }) => {

  await page.goto('/dashboard/settings');

  await page.getByRole('button', { name: `Delete ${board}` }).click();
  await expect(page.getByText(`Deleted ${board}`)).toBeVisible();

  const tag = `tag-${Date.now()}`;
  await page.getByLabel('New tag').fill(tag);
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Add tag' }).click();
  await expect(page.getByText(`Added ${tag}`)).toBeVisible();

});

test('an admin invites a teammate who sets a password and signs in', async ({ page, browser }) => {
  const invitee = `sofia-${Date.now()}@driftnote.app`;
  await signInAdmin(page);
  await page.goto('/dashboard/settings');
  await page.getByLabel('Email to invite').fill(invitee);
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Invite an admin' }).click();
  const link = await page.locator('#team code').textContent();
  expect(link).toContain('/dashboard/invite?token=');

  const other = await browser.newPage();
  await other.goto(link!);
  await other.getByLabel('Password').fill('anothergoodpassword');
  await other.getByRole('button', { name: 'Set password' }).click();
  await other.waitForURL(/\/dashboard\/login/);
  await other.getByLabel('Email').fill(invitee);

  await other.waitForURL(/\/dashboard\/requests/);
  await other.close();

  await page.reload();
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Remove' }).last().click();
  await expect(page.getByText(/is no longer an admin/)).toBeVisible();
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Build the settings sections**

Each change saves on its own with a toast. Branding re-renders through `router.refresh()`, because pages get the brand from the server render. Reordering works by dragging the grip, or with Space and the arrow keys on it, and saves only the rows that moved. Deleting a board that still has requests shows the server's message.

`web/components/dashboard/settings/toggle.tsx`:

```tsx
'use client';

import { cx } from '@/components/lort';

/** The Lort switch (DESIGN.md): a pill track with a knob, primary when on. */
export function Toggle({
  checked,
  onChange,
  label,
  disabled,
}: {
  checked: boolean;
  onChange: (on: boolean) => void;
  label: string;
  disabled?: boolean;
}) {
  return (
    <button
      type="button"
      className="sw"
      role="switch"
      aria-checked={checked}
      aria-label={label}
      disabled={disabled}
      onClick={() => onChange(!checked)}
    >
      <span className={cx('lt-switch', checked && 'is-on')} aria-hidden="true" />
    </button>
  );
}
```

`web/components/dashboard/settings/sortable-rows.tsx`:

```tsx
'use client';

import type { ReactNode } from 'react';
import {
  closestCenter,
  DndContext,
  KeyboardSensor,
  PointerSensor,
  useSensor,
  useSensors,
  type DragEndEvent,
} from '@dnd-kit/core';
import {
  arrayMove,
  SortableContext,
  sortableKeyboardCoordinates,
  useSortable,
  verticalListSortingStrategy,
} from '@dnd-kit/sortable';
import { CSS } from '@dnd-kit/utilities';

/** Rows reordered by dragging the grip, or with Space and the arrow keys on it. */
export function SortableRows<T extends { id: string }>({
  items,
  label,
  onReorder,
  children,
}: {
  items: T[];
  label: (item: T) => string;
  onReorder: (items: T[]) => void;
  children: (item: T, grip: ReactNode, index: number) => ReactNode;
}) {
  const sensors = useSensors(
    useSensor(PointerSensor),
    useSensor(KeyboardSensor, { coordinateGetter: sortableKeyboardCoordinates }),
  );
  const end = ({ active, over }: DragEndEvent) => {
    if (!over || active.id === over.id) return;
    const from = items.findIndex((i) => i.id === active.id);
    const to = items.findIndex((i) => i.id === over.id);
    onReorder(arrayMove(items, from, to));
  };
  return (
    <DndContext sensors={sensors} collisionDetection={closestCenter} onDragEnd={end}>
      <SortableContext items={items.map((i) => i.id)} strategy={verticalListSortingStrategy}>
        {items.map((item, i) => (
          <Row key={item.id} id={item.id} label={label(item)}>
            {(grip) => children(item, grip, i)}
          </Row>
        ))}
      </SortableContext>
    </DndContext>
  );
}

function Row({ id, label, children }: { id: string; label: string; children: (grip: ReactNode) => ReactNode }) {
  const { attributes, listeners, setNodeRef, setActivatorNodeRef, transform, transition, isDragging } = useSortable({
    id,
  });
  const grip = (
    <button
      ref={setActivatorNodeRef}
      type="button"
      className="grip"
      aria-label={`Move ${label}`}
      {...attributes}
      {...listeners}
    >
      <svg width="16" height="16" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="currentColor" aria-hidden="true">
        {[6, 12, 18].flatMap((y) => [9, 15].map((x) => <circle key={`${x}${y}`} cx={x} cy={y} r="1.6" />))}
      </svg>
    </button>
  );
  return (
    <div
      ref={setNodeRef}
      style={{
        transform: CSS.Transform.toString(transform),
        transition,
        position: 'relative',
        zIndex: isDragging ? 2 : undefined,
        background: isDragging ? 'var(--surface)' : undefined,
        boxShadow: isDragging ? 'var(--shadow-card-float)' : undefined,
        borderRadius: isDragging ? 12 : undefined,
      }}
    >
      {children(grip)}
    </div>
  );
}

/** The order values to save after a reorder: only rows whose position changed. */
export function orderChanges<T extends { id: string; order?: number }>(items: T[]): { id: string; order: number }[] {
  return items.flatMap((item, i) => (item.order === i ? [] : [{ id: item.id, order: i }]));
}
```

`web/components/dashboard/settings/branding.tsx`:

```tsx
'use client';

import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { useState } from 'react';
import type { RecordModel } from 'pocketbase';
import { useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';

import { Button } from '@/components/lort';
import { BrandMark } from '@/components/board/brand';
import { PRESETS, parseHex } from '@/lib/color';
import { errorMessage } from '@/lib/errors';
import { adminPb } from '@/lib/pb';
import { useSettings } from '@/lib/team-queries';
import { useToast } from '@/lib/toast';

export function BrandingSection() {
  const settings = useSettings().data;
  if (!settings)
    return (
      <section className="sec" id="branding" aria-busy="true">
        <h2>Branding</h2>
        <div className="sk" style={{ height: 160, borderRadius: 12, marginTop: 16 }} />
      </section>
    );
  // Re-keyed on every save, so the fields start again from what was saved.
  return <BrandingForm key={settings.updated} settings={settings} />;
}

function BrandingForm({ settings }: { settings: RecordModel }) {
  const qc = useQueryClient();
  const router = useRouter();
  const toast = useToast();
  const [name, setName] = useState<string>(settings.appName);
  const [hex, setHex] = useState<string>(settings.primaryColor);

  const save = async (data: Record<string, unknown> | FormData, message = 'Saved') => {
    try {
      await adminPb.collection('settings').update(settings.id, data);
      await qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['settings'] });
      // Branding reaches pages through the server render (X-Lort-Config), so render again.
      router.refresh();
      toast({ message });
    } catch (err) {
      toast({ message: errorMessage(err) });
    }
  };

  const saveColour = (value: string) => {
    const v = value.trim().toLowerCase();
    if (!parseHex(v) || !v.startsWith('#')) {
      toast({ message: 'Use a hex colour like #cc3a6d.' });
      setHex(settings.primaryColor);
      return;
    }
    if (v !== settings.primaryColor) void save({ primaryColor: v }, 'Colour updated');
  };

  return (
    <section className="sec" id="branding">
      <h2>Branding</h2>
      <p className="lede">How your board looks to your users. Lort stays in the background.</p>
      <div className="set-row">
        <BrandMark size={56} />
        <div className="set-row-main">
          <b>App logo</b>
          <span>Square PNG, SVG or WebP, at least 128 px.</span>
        </div>
        <label className="lt-btn lt-btn-secondary lt-btn-sm" style={{ cursor: 'pointer' }}>
          {settings.logo ? 'Replace' : 'Upload'}
          <input
            type="file"
            accept="image/png,image/svg+xml,image/webp"
            hidden
            onChange={(e) => {
              const f = e.target.files?.[0];
              if (!f) return;
              const data = new FormData();
              data.set('logo', f);
              void save(data, 'Logo updated');
            }}
          />
        </label>
        {settings.logo && (
          <Button variant="ghost" size="sm" onClick={() => void save({ logo: null }, 'Logo removed')}>
            Remove
          </Button>
        )}
      </div>
      <div className="set-row">
        <div className="set-row-main">
          <b>App name</b>
          <span>Shown in the top bar, emails and the browser tab.</span>
        </div>
        <input
          className="set-field"
          aria-label="App name"
          maxLength={60}
          style={{ width: 260 }}
          value={name}
          onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)}
          onBlur={() => name.trim() && name.trim() !== settings.appName && void save({ appName: name.trim() })}
          onKeyDown={(e) => e.key === 'Enter' && (e.target as HTMLInputElement).blur()}
        />
      </div>
      <div className="set-row" style={{ alignItems: 'flex-start' }}>
        <div className="set-row-main">
          <b>Primary colour</b>
          <span>
            Buttons, voted stickers and everything that’s on. Lort adjusts custom colours so text on them stays readable
            in light and dark.
          </span>
        </div>
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', alignItems: 'flex-end', gap: 12 }}>
          <div
            role="radiogroup"
            aria-label="Primary colour presets"
            style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 10, flexWrap: 'wrap' }}
          >
            {PRESETS.map((p) => (
              <button
                key={p.hex}
                type="button"
                className="swatch"
                role="radio"
                aria-label={p.name}
                aria-checked={settings.primaryColor === p.hex}
                style={{ background: p.hex }}
                onClick={() => {
                  setHex(p.hex);
                  saveColour(p.hex);
                }}
              />
            ))}
          </div>
          <label style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 8, fontSize: 13, color: 'var(--muted)' }}>
            Custom
            <input
              className="set-field"
              aria-label="Custom hex colour"
              value={hex}
              maxLength={7}
              style={{ width: 120, fontFamily: 'ui-monospace, monospace', fontSize: 13 }}
              onChange={(e) => setHex(e.target.value)}
              onBlur={() => saveColour(hex)}
              onKeyDown={(e) => e.key === 'Enter' && (e.target as HTMLInputElement).blur()}
            />
          </label>
        </div>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}
```

`web/components/dashboard/settings/boards.tsx`:

```tsx
'use client';

import { useState, type FormEvent } from 'react';
import { useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import type { RecordModel } from 'pocketbase';

import { Button } from '@/components/lort';
import { errorMessage } from '@/lib/errors';
import { adminPb } from '@/lib/pb';
import { useAllBoards } from '@/lib/team-queries';
import { useToast } from '@/lib/toast';
import { orderChanges, SortableRows } from './sortable-rows';
import { Toggle } from './toggle';

export function BoardsSection() {
  const boards = useAllBoards().data;
  const qc = useQueryClient();
  const toast = useToast();
  const [adding, setAdding] = useState('');

  const run = async (fn: () => Promise<unknown>, message?: string) => {
    try {
      await fn();
      if (message) toast({ message });
    } catch (err) {
      toast({ message: errorMessage(err) });
    } finally {
      void qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['boards'] });
      void qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['config'] });
    }
  };

  const reorder = (items: RecordModel[]) => {
    qc.setQueryData(['boards'], items);
    void run(() =>
      Promise.all(orderChanges(items).map((c) => adminPb.collection('boards').update(c.id, { order: c.order }))),
    );
  };

  const add = (e: FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    const name = adding.trim();
    if (!name) return;
    setAdding('');
    void run(
      () => adminPb.collection('boards').create({ name, isPublic: true, order: boards?.length ?? 0 }),
      `Added ${name}`,
    );
  };

  return (
    <section className="sec" id="boards">
      <h2>Boards</h2>
      <p className="lede">
        Drag to reorder. The top board is the default for new suggestions. Private boards are only visible to your team.
      </p>
      <div className="set-grid" style={{ fontSize: 12, fontWeight: 700, color: 'var(--muted)', paddingBottom: 6 }}>
        <span />
        <span>Board</span>
        <span>Public</span>
        <span>Needs approval</span>
        <span />
      </div>
      {!boards ? (
        <div className="sk" style={{ height: 96, borderRadius: 12 }} />
      ) : (
        <SortableRows items={boards} label={(b) => b.name} onReorder={reorder}>
          {(b, grip, i) => (
            <div className="set-grid" style={{ padding: '10px 0', borderTop: '1px solid var(--border)' }}>
              {grip}
              <span style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 8, minWidth: 0 }}>
                <input
                  className="set-field"
                  aria-label="Board name"
                  defaultValue={b.name}
                  maxLength={60}
                  style={{ width: '100%', maxWidth: 240, fontWeight: 600 }}
                  onBlur={(e) => {
                    const name = e.target.value.trim();
                    if (name && name !== b.name)
                      void run(() => adminPb.collection('boards').update(b.id, { name }), 'Renamed');
                  }}
                  onKeyDown={(e) => e.key === 'Enter' && (e.target as HTMLInputElement).blur()}
                />
                {i === 0 && <span className="badge">Default</span>}
              </span>
              <Toggle
                label={`${b.name} is public`}
                checked={b.isPublic}
                onChange={(on) => void run(() => adminPb.collection('boards').update(b.id, { isPublic: on }))}
              />
              <Toggle
                label={`${b.name} needs approval`}
                checked={b.requireApproval}
                onChange={(on) => void run(() => adminPb.collection('boards').update(b.id, { requireApproval: on }))}
              />
              <Button
                variant="ghost"
                size="sm"
                icon="x"
                iconOnly
                aria-label={`Delete ${b.name}`}
                onClick={() => void run(() => adminPb.collection('boards').delete(b.id), `Deleted ${b.name}`)}
              />
            </div>
          )}
        </SortableRows>
      )}
      <form onSubmit={add} style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 8, paddingTop: 12, borderTop: '1px solid var(--border)' }}>
        <input
          className="set-field"
          aria-label="New board name"
          placeholder="New board name"
          value={adding}
          maxLength={60}
          onChange={(e) => setAdding(e.target.value)}
        />
        <Button variant="secondary" size="sm" icon="plus" type="submit">
          Add board
        </Button>
      </form>
    </section>
  );
}
```

`web/components/dashboard/settings/tags.tsx`:

```tsx
'use client';

import { useState } from 'react';
import { useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import type { RecordModel } from 'pocketbase';

import { Button } from '@/components/lort';
import { errorMessage } from '@/lib/errors';
import { adminPb } from '@/lib/pb';
import { useTags } from '@/lib/team-queries';
import { useToast } from '@/lib/toast';
import { orderChanges, SortableRows } from './sortable-rows';

export function TagsSection() {
  const tags = useTags().data;
  const qc = useQueryClient();
  const toast = useToast();
  const [adding, setAdding] = useState('');

  const run = async (fn: () => Promise<unknown>, message?: string) => {
    try {
      await fn();
      if (message) toast({ message });
    } catch (err) {
      toast({ message: errorMessage(err) });
    } finally {
      void qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['tags'] });
      void qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['config'] });
    }
  };

  const reorder = (items: RecordModel[]) => {
    qc.setQueryData(['tags'], items);
    void run(() =>
      Promise.all(orderChanges(items).map((c) => adminPb.collection('tags').update(c.id, { order: c.order }))),
    );
  };

  return (
    <section className="sec" id="tags">
      <h2>Tags</h2>
      <p className="lede">Labels you can put on requests. People can pick from these when they suggest something.</p>
      {!tags ? (
        <div className="sk" style={{ height: 80, borderRadius: 12 }} />
      ) : (
        <SortableRows items={tags} label={(t) => t.name} onReorder={reorder}>
          {(t, grip) => (
            <div
              style={{
                display: 'flex',
                alignItems: 'center',
                gap: 12,
                padding: '8px 0',
                borderTop: '1px solid var(--border)',
              }}
            >
              {grip}
              <input
                className="set-field"
                aria-label="Tag name"
                defaultValue={t.name}
                maxLength={40}
                style={{ flex: 1, maxWidth: 260 }}
                onBlur={(e) => {
                  const name = e.target.value.trim();
                  if (name && name !== t.name)
                    void run(() => adminPb.collection('tags').update(t.id, { name }), 'Renamed');
                }}
                onKeyDown={(e) => e.key === 'Enter' && (e.target as HTMLInputElement).blur()}
              />
              <span style={{ flex: 1 }} />
              <Button
                variant="ghost"
                size="sm"
                icon="x"
                iconOnly
                aria-label={`Delete ${t.name}`}
                onClick={() => void run(() => adminPb.collection('tags').delete(t.id), `Deleted ${t.name}`)}
              />
            </div>
          )}
        </SortableRows>
      )}
      <form
        onSubmit={(e) => {
          e.preventDefault();
          const name = adding.trim();
          if (!name) return;
          setAdding('');
          void run(() => adminPb.collection('tags').create({ name, order: tags?.length ?? 0 }), `Added ${name}`);
        }}
        style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 8, paddingTop: 12, borderTop: '1px solid var(--border)' }}
      >
        <input
          className="set-field"
          aria-label="New tag"
          placeholder="New tag"
          value={adding}
          maxLength={40}
          onChange={(e) => setAdding(e.target.value)}
        />
        <Button variant="secondary" size="sm" icon="plus" type="submit">
          Add tag
        </Button>
      </form>
    </section>
  );
}
```

`web/components/dashboard/settings/team.tsx`:

```tsx
'use client';

import { useState, type FormEvent } from 'react';
import { useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';

import { Avatar, Button } from '@/components/lort';
import { errorMessage } from '@/lib/errors';
import { adminPb } from '@/lib/pb';
import { useTeam } from '@/lib/team-queries';
import { useToast } from '@/lib/toast';
import { shortDate } from '@/lib/time';

export function TeamSection() {
  const team = useTeam().data;
  const qc = useQueryClient();
  const toast = useToast();
  const [email, setEmail] = useState('');
  const [link, setLink] = useState('');

  const invite = async (e: FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    try {
      const res = await adminPb.send<{ link: string }>('/api/lort/team/invite', {
        method: 'POST',
        body: { email: email.trim() },
      });
      setLink(res.link);
      setEmail('');
      void qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['team'] });
    } catch (err) {
      toast({ message: errorMessage(err) });
    }
  };

  const remove = async (id: string, who: string) => {
    try {
      await adminPb.send(`/api/lort/team/${id}`, { method: 'DELETE' });
      void qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['team'] });
      toast({ message: `${who} is no longer an admin` });
    } catch (err) {
      toast({ message: errorMessage(err) });
    }
  };

  return (
    <section className="sec" id="team">
      <h2>Team</h2>
      <p className="lede">Admins can sign in to this dashboard. Their comments show a Team badge.</p>
      {!team ? (
        <div className="sk" style={{ height: 64, borderRadius: 12 }} />
      ) : (
        team.items.map((m, i) => (
          <div key={m.id} className="set-row">
            <Avatar name={m.name || m.email} size={32} team />
            <div className="set-row-main">
              <b>
                {m.name || m.email}
                {m.id === team.you && (
                  <span className="badge" style={{ marginLeft: 8 }}>
                    You
                  </span>
                )}
              </b>
              <span>
                {m.email} · {i === 0 ? 'set up Lort' : `invited ${shortDate(m.created)}`}
              </span>
            </div>
            {m.id !== team.you && (
              <Button variant="ghost" size="sm" onClick={() => void remove(m.id, m.name || m.email)}>
                Remove
              </Button>
            )}
          </div>
        ))
      )}
      <form
        onSubmit={invite}
        style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 8, paddingTop: 12, borderTop: '1px solid var(--border)', flexWrap: 'wrap' }}
      >
        <input
          className="set-field"
          type="email"
          required
          aria-label="Email to invite"
          placeholder="name@yourapp.com"
          value={email}
          onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
          style={{ width: 260 }}
        />
        <Button variant="secondary" size="sm" icon="plus" type="submit">
          Invite an admin
        </Button>
      </form>
      {link && (
        <div className="lt-banner" style={{ marginTop: 12, flexWrap: 'wrap' }} role="status">
          <span style={{ flex: 1, minWidth: 200 }}>Send this link to them. It works for 7 days.</span>
          <code style={{ fontSize: 12, wordBreak: 'break-all', color: 'var(--ink-2)' }}>{link}</code>
          <Button
            variant="secondary"
            size="sm"
            onClick={() =>
              void navigator.clipboard.writeText(link).then(() => toast({ message: 'Invite link copied' }))
            }
          >
            Copy
          </Button>
        </div>
      )}
    </section>
  );
}
```

```tsx
'use client';

import { BoardsSection } from './boards';
import { BrandingSection } from './branding';
import { TagsSection } from './tags';
import { TeamSection } from './team';

/** One page split into sections (design/canvas/D-Settings.dc.html). Later phases add Services, Connect and Server. */
const TOC = [
  {
    group: 'Your board',
    items: [
      ['branding', 'Branding'],
      ['boards', 'Boards'],
      ['tags', 'Tags'],
      ['team', 'Team'],
    ],
  },
];

export function SettingsPage() {
  return (
    <div
      className="settings-layout"
      style={{
        maxWidth: 1040,
        margin: '0 auto',
        display: 'grid',
        gridTemplateColumns: '190px minmax(0, 1fr)',
        gap: 32,
        alignItems: 'start',
      }}
    >
      <nav className="toc" aria-label="Settings sections" style={{ position: 'sticky', top: 24 }}>
        <h1 className="dash-h1" style={{ margin: '0 0 14px 12px' }}>
          Settings
        </h1>
        {TOC.map((g) => (
          <div key={g.group}>
            <div className="grp">{g.group}</div>
            {g.items.map(([id, label]) => (
              <a key={id} href={`#${id}`}>
                {label}
              </a>
            ))}
          </div>
        ))}
      </nav>
      <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 20 }}>
        <BrandingSection />
        <BoardsSection />
        <TagsSection />
        <TeamSection />
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```
