### Task 7: Accessibility, full verification, commit

**Files:**
- Create: `web/e2e/a11y.spec.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: every page from Tasks 4–6.
- Produces: an axe-core check (WCAG 2.0/2.1/2.2 A and AA) of the board, a request page, dashboard sign-in, the requests table, a dashboard request page and Settings, in both themes.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the accessibility spec**

`web/e2e/a11y.spec.ts`:

```ts
import AxeBuilder from '@axe-core/playwright';
import { expect, test, type Page } from '@playwright/test';

import { email, signInAdmin, signUpInDialog } from './helpers';

async function expectNoViolations(page: Page) {
  const { violations } = await new AxeBuilder({ page })
    .withTags(['wcag2a', 'wcag2aa', 'wcag21aa', 'wcag22aa'])
    .analyze();
  expect(violations.map((v) => `${v.id}: ${v.nodes.map((n) => n.target.join(' ')).join(', ')}`)).toEqual([]);
}

test('board pages meet WCAG 2.2 AA', async ({ page }) => {
  await page.goto('/feedback');
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Suggest something' }).first().click();
  await signUpInDialog(page, email('ada'));
  await page.getByLabel('Title').fill(`Accessible idea ${Date.now()}`);
  await page.getByPlaceholder(/What would it help/).fill('Checking contrast and names.');
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Send' }).click();
  await page.waitForURL(/\/feedback\/\w+/);
  await expect(page.getByText('Your dot')).toBeVisible();
  await expectNoViolations(page);
  await page.goto('/feedback');
  await expect(page.locator('article.lt-card').first()).toBeVisible();
  await expectNoViolations(page);
});

test('dashboard pages meet WCAG 2.2 AA', async ({ page }) => {
  await page.goto('/dashboard/login');
  await expectNoViolations(page);
  await signInAdmin(page);
  await expect(page.locator('a.rq-row').first()).toBeVisible();
  await expectNoViolations(page);
  await page.locator('a.rq-row').first().click();
  await expect(page.getByRole('toolbar', { name: 'Triage' })).toBeVisible();
  await expectNoViolations(page);
  await page.goto('/dashboard/settings');
  await expect(page.getByText('App logo')).toBeVisible();
  await expect(page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Move Features' })).toBeVisible();
  await expectNoViolations(page);
});
```

## Spec coverage

| Spec | Where |
|---|---|
| 2.2 Routing: everything else goes to Next.js, with the brand | Task 1 (`X-Lort-Config`, proxy) |
| 2.3 Frontend rules: shadcn CLI, Tabler, design system in `web/components/lort` | Task 2 |
| 4.1–4.3 Statuses, triage bar, merge | Task 5 (`useTriage`, `TriageBar`, `MergeDialog`) |
| 5.1 `/feedback`: headline, cards, search, floating bar, URL filters, pages of 20, awaiting approval | Task 4 (`Feed`, `FilterSheet`, `TopBar`) |
| 5.1 `/feedback/<id>`: sticker, body, images, timeline, one-level replies, Team badge, Merged into | Task 1 (`Get`), Task 4 (`RequestView`) |
| 5.1 Sign up, sign in, reset; profile dialog | Task 4 (`AuthProvider`, `ProfileDialog`, `ResetPassword`) |
| 5.2 Suggest something: boards, similar requests, tags, Composer, approval boards, author's vote | Task 4 (`SuggestDialog`) |
| 5.3 Votes: one per person, sign in first, optimistic with rollback, static when closed | Task 3 (`useVote` and its test), Task 4 (`useVoteAction`) |
| 5.4 Comments: signed in, one level, internal notes | Task 4 (`postComment`, `RequestView`) |
| 5.6 Freshness: realtime, fading tint, polling fallback, rate-limited refresh | Task 3 (`useLiveRequests`, `useFeed`), Task 5 (table refresh) |
| 5.7 Anti-spam: friendly 429 toasts, honeypot | Task 3 (`errorMessage`, `signUp`), Task 4 |
| 6 Sidebar, 76px rail, phone menu sheet | Task 5 (`DashboardShell`) |
| 6.1 Requests table: header counts, Live, refresh, New request, filters, sort, columns, New tint, infinite scroll | Task 1 (`Stats`), Task 5 (`RequestsTable`) |
| 6.2 Request: breadcrumb and queue, editable chips, Composer with Internal, popovers with reset link and ban, Edit and Delete, TriageBar | Task 1 (`MoveBoard`), Task 5 (`RequestDetail`) |
| 6.6 Settings: Branding, Boards, Tags, Team | Task 6 |
| 10 Branding rules: owner's brand leads, "Powered by Lort" footer | Tasks 1, 3, 4 |
| 11 Errors: 429s, inline upload errors, rollback with a toast, inline form errors | Tasks 3–6 |
| 12 Testing: Vitest for URL filters, optimistic vote, colour contrast, Composer; Playwright in both themes with axe | Tasks 2–7 |
| 14–16 Visual system, skeletons, page layouts | Tasks 2, 4–6 (against `design/canvas`) |

Left for later plans, as section 13 phases them:

- **1C (next):** the Dockerfile (Go build, pnpm build of `web/`, slim runtime with node and tini), `setup.sh` and `setup.ps1` including `--reset-admin`, and CI: Go tests, web unit tests, and this Playwright suite against the built container, `shellcheck` and `PSScriptAnalyzer`.
- **Phase 2:** the public and dashboard roadmaps (with their sidebar and top-bar links), the changelog, and Settings → Roadmap.
- **Phase 3:** email: "Check your inbox", reset by email, notification switches in the profile dialog, "will be notified" on Complete (already wired to `features.email`), and "New request" on behalf of an email.
- **Phases 4–6:** the rest of Settings, the voice mic (the Composer already takes `voiceEnabled`), the widget, the sidebar version line, the "Get your own board" card, `/lort-landing` and Docs.

## Self-review

- **Placeholders:** none. Every code step holds the complete file, copied from a build that passed `go test ./...`, `pnpm lint`, `pnpm typecheck`, 45 unit tests and 40 end-to-end runs (the suite twice, in both themes).
- **Type consistency:** the TypeScript types in `web/lib/types.ts` mirror the Go JSON from 1A and Task 1 field for field (`thread`, not `comments`, for the comment list; `comments` is the count). Every name in a task's **Interfaces** is defined in that task or an earlier one.
- **Known traps, written into the steps:** `cx()` instead of template-string class names; `ChipButton` for Radix triggers; `useMemo` over a JSON snapshot in `useRecord` to avoid a render loop; explicit query invalidation after creating a request; `waitForURL` before reading the URL in tests; signing out only on `isRejected`.
